But the others said, “Oh, do let’s try, now we *are* here,” and they went on into the dusky twilight of the cave.

It was a very pretty cave, not chalk, like the cliffs, but roofed and walled with gray flints such as the houses and churches are built of that you see on the downs near Brighton and Eastbourne.

“This isn’t an accidental cave, you know,” said Bernard importantly; “it’s built by the hand of man in distant ages, like Stonehenge and the Cheesewring and Kit’s Coty House.”

The cave was lighted from the entrance where the sunshine crept faintly through the brambles. Their eyes soon grew used to the gloom and they could see that the floor of the cave was of dry white sand, and that along one end was a narrow dark pool of water. Ferns fringed its edge and drooped their fronds to its smooth surface﻿—a surface which caught a gleam of light, and shone whitely; but the pool was very still, and they felt somehow, without knowing why, very deep.

===Chinese===
可它偏偏不是。他们爬上峭壁小路，鲁本指给他们该从哪儿拐下小路，又拨开把洞口遮得严严实实的荆棘和荆豆，弗朗西斯一眼便看出，这儿的水不可能是海水。它高出浪头能打到的线，实在太多了，哪怕天气再狂暴也是一样。

“所以这儿没用。”他解释道。

可其余的人都说：“哦，都*已经*到这儿了，就试试嘛。”于是他们继续走进山洞昏暗的暮色里去。

那是一个很好看的山洞。它不像峭壁那样是白垩的，顶和壁都是灰色燧石砌成——就是你在布莱顿（Brighton）和伊斯特本（Eastbourne）附近丘陵上见到的那些房子和教堂所用的那种石头。

“你们知道吗，这可不是个碰巧生出来的山洞，”伯纳德煞有介事地说，“它是远古时代人手建造的，就像巨石阵（Stonehenge）、切斯灵石（the Cheesewring）和基茨科蒂石屋（Kit's Coty House）一样。”

山洞里唯一的光亮来自洞口，阳光从荆棘间淡淡地渗进来。他们的眼睛很快习惯了幽暗，看得出洞底铺着干燥的白沙，洞的一头有一泓狭长幽暗的水潭。蕨草镶在潭边，叶穗垂向平滑的水面——那水面接住一缕微光，泛出白色；潭水却纹丝不动，而且不知怎么，他们没来由地觉得，它深极了。

===Original===
“It’s no good, no earthly,” said Francis.

“But it’s an awfully pretty cave,” said Mavis consolingly. “Thank you for showing it to us, Reuben. And it’s jolly cool. Do let’s rest a minute or two. I’m simply boiling, climbing that cliff path. We’ll go down to the sea in a minute. Reuben could wait here if he felt safer.”

“All right, squattez-vous,” said Bernard, and the children sat down at the water’s edge, Reuben still very awkward in his girl’s clothes.

It was very, very quiet. Only now and then one fat drop of water would fall from the cave’s roof into that quiet pool and just move its surface in a spreading circle.

“It’s a ripping place for a hidey-hole,” said Bernard, “better than that old bush of yours, anyhow. I don’t believe anybody knows of the way in.”

===Chinese===
“不行，根本不行，半点指望也没有。”弗朗西斯说。

“可这山洞实在好看呀，”梅维斯安慰他说，“谢谢你带我们来看，鲁本。而且这儿凉快极了。就让我们歇上一两分钟吧。爬那段峭壁小路，我简直都要煮沸了。一会儿我们就下海边去。鲁本要是觉得这儿更稳妥，尽可以在这儿等着。”

“好啦，都‘请坐’（squattez-vous）。”伯纳德说。孩子们在水潭边坐了下来，鲁本穿着女孩衣裳，还是别扭得很。

洞里非常非常安静。只有偶尔一滴饱满的水珠从洞顶落进那幽静的水潭，在水面荡开一圈涟漪。

“这地方做藏身洞再好不过，”伯纳德说，“反正比你那老灌木丛强。我看，进洞的这条路没人知道。”

===Original===
“*I* don’t think anyone does, either,” said Reuben, “because there wasn’t any way in till it fell in two days ago, when I was trying to dig up a furze root.”

“I should hide here if you want to hide,” said Bernard.

“I mean to,” said Reuben.

“Well, if you’re rested, let’s get on,” Francis said; but Kathleen urged:

“Do let’s say ‘Sabrina fair,’ first﻿—just to try!” So they said it﻿—all but the Spangled Child who did not know it﻿—

===Chinese===
“*我*看也没人知道，”鲁本说，“因为两天前洞口塌开之前，压根儿就没有路能进来——那会儿我正想挖一棵荆豆根。”

“你要是想藏身，我就藏在这儿。”伯纳德说。

“我正是这么打算的。”鲁本说。

“那么，歇够了就动身吧。”弗朗西斯说。可凯思琳一个劲儿央求：

“先念一遍‘美丽的萨布丽娜’吧——就试试嘛！”于是他们念了——只有亮片孩子没念，因为他不会——

===Original===
> “ ‘Sabrina fair
 

 Listen where thou art sitting
 

 Under the glassie, cool.﻿ ﻿…’ ”

===Chinese===
> "'美丽的萨布丽娜，

 请听，就在你端坐之处，

 在玻璃般清凉……'"

===Original===
There was a splash and a swirl in the pool, and there was the Mermaid herself, sure enough. Their eyes had grown used to the dusk and they could see her quite plainly, could see too that she was holding out her arms to them and smiling so sweetly that it almost took their breath away.

“My cherished preservers,” she cried, “my dear, darling, kind, brave, noble, unselfish dears!”

“You’re talking to Reuben, in the plural, by mistake, I suppose,” said Francis, a little bitterly.

“To him, too, of course. But you two most of all,” she said, swishing her tail around and leaning her hands on the edge of the pool. “I *am* so sorry I was so ungrateful the other night. I’ll tell you how it was. It’s in your air. You see, coming out of the water we’re very susceptible to aerial influences﻿—and that sort of ungratefulness and, what’s the word﻿—?”

===Chinese===
水潭里哗啦一声响，打了个旋儿，美人鱼果然就在那儿。他们的眼睛早已习惯了昏暗，把她看得分明，还看得出她正朝他们伸出双臂，笑得那么甜，甜得他们简直喘不过气来。

“我珍爱的救命恩人们，”她喊道，“我亲爱的、可爱的、好心的、勇敢的、高贵的、无私的宝贝们！”

“我猜你是失了口，用‘你们’把鲁本也一并包括进去了。”弗朗西斯说，口气有点辛酸。

“他当然也算。不过最要紧的还是你们两位。”她说着，把尾巴甩了个圈，双手撑在水潭边上。“那天晚上我那么忘恩负义，我*实在*太对不住你们了。我告诉你们那是怎么回事吧。是你们的空气闹的。你们要知道，我们一出水面，就特别容易受空气里的东西影响——于是就有了那种忘恩负义，还有，那词儿怎么说来着——？”

===Original===
“Snobbishness,” said Francis firmly.

“Is that what you call it?﻿—is most frightfully infectious, and your air’s absolutely crammed with the germs of it. That’s why I was so horrid. You do forgive me, don’t you, dears? And I was so selfish, too﻿—oh, horrid. But it’s all washed off now, in the nice clean sea, and I’m as sorry as if it had been my fault, which it really and truly wasn’t.”

The children said all right, and she wasn’t to mind, and it didn’t matter, and all the things you say when people say they are sorry, and you cannot kiss them and say, “Right oh,” which is the natural answer to such confessions.

===Chinese===
“势利眼。”弗朗西斯斩钉截铁地说。

“你们管那个叫这个？——它传染起来厉害得吓人，你们这空气里简直塞满了那种病菌。所以我才那么可恶。你们真原谅我了，对不对，亲爱的们？再说，我那时候还那么自私——哦，坏透了。不过现在全都洗干净了，在干干净净的大海里洗掉了；我心里难受得很，就仿佛那真是我的错似的——虽说天地良心，它实在不是。”

孩子们说，好啦，别放在心上，没关系啦，还有人家说对不起时你该说的那一套话——凡是没法亲亲人家、说一声“好嘞”的时候能说的话——而“好嘞”原是对这类表白最自然的回答。

===Original===
“It was very curious,” she said thoughtfully, “a most odd experience, that little boy﻿ ﻿… his having been born of people who had always been rich, really seemed to me to be important. I assure you it did. Funny, wasn’t it? And now I want you all to come home with me, and see where I live.”

She smiled radiantly at them, and they all said, “Thank you,” and looked at each other rather blankly.

“All our people will be unspeakably pleased to see you. We Mer-people are not really ungrateful. You mustn’t think that,” she said pleadingly.

She looked very kind, very friendly. But Francis thought of the Lorelei. Just so kind and friendly must the Lady of the Rhine have looked to the “sailor in a little skiff” whom he had disentangled from Heine’s poem, last term, with the aid of the German dicker. By a curious coincidence and the same hard means, Mavis had, only last term, read of Undine, and she tried not to think that there was any lack of soul in the Mermaid’s kind eyes. Kathleen who, by another coincidence, had fed her fancy in English literature on the “Forsaken Merman” was more at ease.

===Chinese===
“那真是非常奇怪，”她若有所思地说，“一次顶顶古怪的经历，就是那个小男孩……‘他生在祖祖辈辈有钱的人家’，这一层在我眼里竟好像当真要紧。我向你担保，真是这样。可笑不可笑？好了，现在我要你们全都跟我回家去，看看我住的地方。”

她朝他们发出光彩地微笑，他们都说：“谢谢。”随即面面相觑，一脸茫然。

“我们全族人见到你们，都会高兴得没法形容。我们人鱼族其实并不是忘恩负义的。你们千万别那么想。”她恳求似的说。

她看上去非常和善，非常友好。可是弗朗西斯想起了罗蕾莱（Lorelei）。上学期，他靠着一本德语词典的苦工，从海涅（Heine）的诗里把那个“驾一叶小舟的水手”拆解了出来；那位莱茵河的仙娘当年看在这个水手眼里，想必也正是这般和善、这般友好。说来也巧，梅维斯就在上学期，也是靠着同样艰苦的办法，读到了《温蒂妮》（Undine）的故事；她竭力不去想美人鱼那双和善的眼睛里缺少灵魂这回事。凯思琳呢，又凑巧在英国文学里拿《被弃的人鱼》（The Forsaken Merman）喂养过自己的想象，反倒自在些。

===Original===
“Do you mean down with you under the sea?” she asked﻿—

> “ ‘Where the sea snakes coil and twine,
 

 Dry their mail and bask in the brine,
 

 Where great whales go sailing by,
 

 Sail and sail with unshut eye
 

 Round the world for ever and aye?’ ”

===Chinese===
“你是说，跟你一块儿下到海底去？”她问——

> "'在海底，海蛇盘绕蜿蜒，

 晾干鳞甲，泡在咸浪里晒太阳，

 大鲸鱼一头头驶过，

 睁着不阖的眼睛，

 航呀，航呀，永永远远绕遍世界？'"

===Original===
“Well, it’s not exactly like that, really,” said the Mermaid; “but you’ll see soon enough.”

This had, in Bernard’s ears, a sinister ring.

“Why,” he asked suddenly, “did you say you wanted to see us at dead of night?”

“It’s the usual time, isn’t it?” she asked, looking at him with innocent surprise. “It is in all the stories. You know we have air stories just as you have fairy stories and water stories﻿—and the rescuer almost always comes to the castle gate at dead of night, on a coal-black steed or a dapple-gray, you know, or a red-roan steed of might; but as there were four of you, besides me and my tail, I thought it more considerate to suggest a chariot. Now, we really ought to be going.”

===Chinese===
“哦，实际上不完全是那个样子，”美人鱼说，“不过你们很快就会看到了。”

这话在伯纳德耳朵里，响出了一点不祥的味道。

“我问你，”他突然发问，“你干吗说要在半夜三更见我们？”

“寻常不都是那个时辰吗？”她问，带着无辜的惊讶望着他。“所有的故事里都是这样。你们知道，我们有讲空气的故事，就像你们有仙女的故事和水的故事——救星差不多总是半夜三更来到城堡门口，骑一匹乌黑的骏马，要不就是一匹灰花马，要么就是一匹枣红色的神力骏马；不过呢，你们有四个，再加上我和我的尾巴，我想还是提议御辇更体贴些。好了，咱们实在该动身了。”

===Original===
“Which way?” asked Bernard, and everyone held their breath to hear the answer.

“The way I came, of course,” she answered, “down here,” and she pointed to the water that rippled around her.

“Thank you so very, *very* much,” said Mavis, in a voice which trembled a little; “but I don’t know whether you’ve heard that people who go down into the water like that﻿—people like us﻿—without tails, you know﻿—they get drowned.”

“Not if they’re personally conducted,” said the Mermaid. “Of course we can’t be responsible for trespassers, though even with them I don’t think anything very dreadful has ever happened. Someone once told me a story about Water Babies. Did you ever hear of that?”

===Chinese===
“走哪条路？”伯纳德问。人人都屏住呼吸，等着听她的回答。

“当然是我来的那条路，”她答道，“就从这儿下去。”她指了指身边泛着涟漪的水。

“多谢，*多*谢您的好意，”梅维斯说，声音微微发颤，“不过我不知道您听说过没有，人要像那样下到水里去——像我们这样的人，您知道——没有尾巴的，您知道——是会淹死的。”

“有专人全程领着就不会。”美人鱼说。“当然，擅闯的人我们负不了责任；不过即便是他们，我看也从没出过什么太可怕的事。从前有人给我讲过一个《水孩子》（The Water Babies）的故事。你们听说过吗？”

===Original===
“Yes, but that was a made-up story,” said Bernard stolidly.

“Yes, of course,” she agreed, “but a great deal of it’s quite true, all the same. But you won’t grow fins and gills or anything like that. You needn’t be afraid.”

The children looked at each other, and then all looked at Francis. He spoke.

“Thank you,” he said. “Thank you very much, but we would rather not﻿—much rather.”

“Oh, nonsense,” said the lady kindly. “Look here, it’s as easy as easy. I give you each a lock of my hair,” she cut off the locks with her shell knife as she spoke, long locks they were and soft. “Look here, tie these round your necks﻿—if I’d had a lock of human hair round my neck I should never have suffered from the dryness as I did. And then just jump in. Keep your eyes shut. It’s rather confusing if you don’t; but there’s no danger.”

===Chinese===
“听说过，可那是个编出来的故事。”伯纳德倔头倔脑地说。

“是啊，当然是编的，”她附和道，“不过话说回来，那里头好些东西倒千真万确。你们不会长出鳍和鳃，也不会长出任何类似的东西。用不着害怕。”

孩子们互相看了看，然后全都望着弗朗西斯。他开口了。

“谢谢您，”他说，“多谢您了，可我们宁可不去——非常宁可不去。”

“哦，胡说，”这位夫人好脾气地说，“瞧着吧，这容易得不能再容易了。我给你们每人一绺我的头发。”她一面说，一面用贝壳刀把头发割下来——那几绺头发又长又软。“来，把这些系在你们脖子上——当年我要是脖子上系着一绺人类的头发，就绝不会吃那么多干燥的苦头了。然后只管跳进来。眼睛闭紧。不闭的话，会有些晕头转向；可是一点危险也没有。”

===Original===
The children took the locks of hair, but no one regarded them with any confidence at all as lifesaving apparatus. They still hung back.

“You really are silly,” said the sea lady indulgently. “Why did you meddle with magic at all if you weren’t prepared to go through with it? Why, this is one of the simplest forms of magic, and the safest. Whatever would you have done if you had happened to call up a fire spirit and had had to go down Vesuvius with a Salamander round your little necks?”

She laughed merrily at the thought. But her laugh sounded a little angry too.

“Come, don’t be foolish,” she said. “You’ll never have such a chance again. And I feel that this air is full of your horrid human microbes﻿—distrust, suspicion, fear, anger, resentment﻿—horrid little germs. I don’t want to risk catching them. Come.”

===Chinese===
孩子们接过那几绺头发，可是谁也没把它当成救生器材，对它毫无信心。他们还是往后缩。

“你们真是傻得可以，”海里的夫人宽容地说，“既然不打算把魔法奉陪到底，当初干吗要去招惹它呢？告诉你，这是魔法里最简单的一种，也是最安全的一种。你们要是碰巧召来一个火灵，脖子上盘着一条火蜥蜴（Salamander），不得不钻进维苏威火山（Vesuvius）去，那可怎么办？”

她想到这情景，快活地笑了。可她的笑声里也透着几分恼怒。

“来吧，别犯傻了，”她说，“这样的机会你们再也碰不上第二个了。况且我觉得，这空气里满是你们人类讨厌的微生物——不信任、猜疑、恐惧、愤怒、怨恨——讨厌的小病菌。我可不想冒险沾上它们。来吧。”

===Original===
“No,” said Francis, and held out to her the lock of her hair; so did Mavis and Bernard. But Kathleen had tied the lock of hair round her neck, and she said:

“I *should* have liked to, but I promised Bernard I would not do anything unless he said I might.” It was toward Kathleen that the Mermaid turned, holding out a white hand for the lock.

Kathleen bent over the water trying to untie it, and in one awful instant the Mermaid had reared herself up in the water, caught Kathleen in her long white arms, pulled her over the edge of the pool, and with a bubbling splash disappeared with her beneath the dark water.

Mavis screamed and knew it; Francis and Bernard thought they did not scream. It was the Spangled Child alone who said nothing. He had not offered to give back the lock of soft hair. He, like Kathleen, had knotted it round his neck; he now tied a further knot, stepped forward, and spoke in tones which the other three thought the most noble they had ever heard.

“She give me the plum pie,” he said, and leaped into the water.

===Chinese===
“不去。”弗朗西斯说着，把那绺头发递还给她；梅维斯和伯纳德也递了回去。可是凯思琳已经把那绺头发系在了脖子上，她说：

“我*本来*是很想去的，可我答应过伯纳德，他不点头，我就什么都不做。”美人鱼转过身来，朝着凯思琳，伸出一只白手去接那绺头发。

凯思琳俯到水上去解那个结。就在一个可怕的瞬间里，美人鱼猛地从水里直起了身，用她那双长长的白胳膊一把搂住凯思琳，将她拽过潭沿，哗啦一声，带着她冒着水泡消失在幽暗的水底。

梅维斯尖叫起来，自己也听得清清楚楚；弗朗西斯和伯纳德则相信自己没有尖叫。只有亮片孩子一声不吭。他没有伸手归还那绺柔软的头发。他跟凯思琳一样，早已把它系在了脖子上；这时他又打了一个结，走上前去，用一种在另外三个人听来最最高贵、生平未闻的声调说：

“她给过我李子馅饼。”他说完，纵身跳进水里。

===Original===
He sank at once. And this, curiously enough, gave the others confidence. If he had struggled﻿—but no﻿—he sank like a stone, or like a diver who means diving and diving to the very bottom.

“She’s my special sister,” said Bernard, and leaped.

“If it’s magic it’s all right﻿—and if it isn’t we couldn’t go back home without her,” said Mavis hoarsely. And she and Francis took hands and jumped together.

It was not so difficult as it sounds. From the moment of Kathleen’s disappearance the sense of magic﻿—which is rather like very sleepy comfort and sweet scent and sweet music that you just can’t hear the tune of﻿—had been growing stronger and stronger. And there are some things so horrible that if you can bring yourself to face them you simply *can’t* believe that they’re true. It did not seem possible﻿—when they came quite close to the idea﻿—that a Mermaid could really come and talk so kindly and then drown the five children who had rescued her.

===Chinese===
他一下子就沉了下去。说来也怪，这反倒给了其余几个人胆量。他要是扑腾——可他没有——他像一块石头似的沉下去，像一个存心要潜水、要一潜潜到水底的潜水员。

“她是我的特别妹妹。”伯纳德说着，也跳了。

“要是这是魔法，那就没事——要不是，没有她，咱们也不能就这么回家去。”梅维斯声音沙哑地说。她和弗朗西斯牵起手，一同跳了下去。

这事并不像听上去那么难。从凯思琳消失的那一刻起，那种魔法的感觉——很像是浓浓的睡意般的舒坦，是甜香，是甜美的音乐、只是听不出调子——就越来越强，越来越强。世上有些事情可怕到这般地步：你只要能说服自己去面对它，就简直*没法*相信它是真的。他们把那个念头逼到眼前细看时，越发觉得不可能——一条美人鱼，当真会前来这么和蔼地说话，然后淹死救了她的五个孩子。

===Original===
“It’s all right,” Francis cried as they jumped.

“I﻿ ﻿…” He shut his mouth just in time, and down they went.

You have probably dreamed that you were a perfect swimmer? You know the delight of that dream-swimming, which is no effort at all, and yet carries you as far and as fast as you choose. It was like that with the children. The moment they touched the water they felt that they belonged in it﻿—that they were as much at home in water as in air. As they sank beneath the water their feet went up and their heads went down, and there they were swimming downward with long, steady, easy strokes. It was like swimming down a well that presently widened to a cavern. Suddenly Francis found that his head was above water. So was Mavis’s.

===Chinese===
“没事的！”跳下去的时候，弗朗西斯喊道。

“我……”他及时闭上了嘴，两人便沉了下去。

你多半梦见过自己是个完美无缺的游泳好手吧？你知道那种梦中游泳的妙处——毫不费力，却又随你的心意，要游多远、游多快都行。孩子们此刻正是这样。一碰到水，他们就觉得自己原是属于这里的——在水里就像在空气里一样安身自在。他们沉到水面以下，脚翘上去，头朝下，就这么用又长、又稳、又从容的划水一路向下。就像顺着一口井往下游，那口井很快展宽成一个洞窟。忽然，弗朗西斯发觉自己的头露出了水面。梅维斯的头也是。

===Original===
“All right so far,” she said, “but how are we going to get back?”

“Oh, the magic will do that,” he answered, and swam faster.

The cave was lighted by bars of phosphorescence placed like pillars against the walls. The water was clear and deeply green and along the sides of the stream were sea anemones and starfish of the most beautiful forms and the most dazzling colors. The walls were of dark squarish shapes, and here and there a white oblong, or a blue and a red, and the roof was of mother-of-pearl which gleamed and glistened in the pale golden radiance of the phosphorescent pillars. It was very beautiful, and the mere pleasure of swimming so finely and easily swept away almost their last fear. This, too, went when a voice far ahead called: “Hurry up, France﻿—Come on, Mavis,”﻿—and the voice was the voice of Kathleen.

They hurried up, and they came on; and the gleaming soft light grew brighter and brighter. It shone all along the way they had to go, making a path of glory such as the moon makes across the sea on a summer night. And presently they saw that this growing light was from a great gate that barred the waterway in front of them. Five steps led up to this gate, and sitting on it, waiting for them, were Kathleen, Reuben, Bernard and the Mermaid. Only now she had no tail. It lay beside her on the marble steps, just as your stockings lie when you have taken them off; and there were her white feet sticking out from under a dress of soft feathery red seaweed.

===Chinese===
“到现在为止都还好，”她说，“可咱们怎么回去呢？”

“哦，魔法自有办法。”他答道，游得更快了。

洞窟里靠一根根磷光照明，像柱子似的倚墙立着。水清澈而深绿，水道两旁尽是海葵和海星，形态最美，颜色最炫目。洞壁由深色的方块砌成，间或嵌着一块白色的长方，或是一蓝一红；洞顶是珍珠母的，在磷光柱淡金色的光辉里闪闪发亮。这一切美极了，光是游得这么漂亮、这么轻快的那份快活，就几乎把他们最后的恐惧一扫而空。最后这点恐惧，也在前头远处一个声音喊起来的时候飞散了：“快点，弗兰——来呀，梅维斯。”——那声音，是凯思琳的声音。

他们加快脚步赶上去，一路向前；那闪烁的柔光越来越亮。它照亮了他们要走的整条路，铺出一条光辉的路，就像夏夜里月亮在海上铺出的那条。不一会儿他们就看见，这越来越亮的光，来自一扇横锁着水道的大门。五级台阶通向这扇门，凯思琳、鲁本、伯纳德和美人鱼正坐在门上等他们。只是她现在没有尾巴了。尾巴摊在大理石台阶上她的身旁，就像你脱下来的长袜那样摊着；一双白白的脚，从一条轻软如羽的红色海藻裙底下伸出来。

===Original===
They could see it was seaweed though it was woven into a wonderful fabric. Bernard and Kathleen and the Spangled Boy had somehow got seaweed dresses too, and the Spangled Boy was no longer dressed as a girl; and looking down as they scrambled up the steps Mavis and Francis saw that they, too, wore seaweed suits﻿—“Very pretty, but how awkward to go home in,” Mavis thought.

“Now,” said the Mer-lady, “forgive me for taking the plunge. I knew you’d hesitate forever, and I was beginning to feel so cross! That’s your dreadful atmosphere! Now, here we are at the door of our kingdom. You do want to come in, don’t you? I can bring you as far as this against your will, but not any farther. And you can’t come any farther unless you trust me absolutely. Do you? Will you? Try!”

“Yes,” said the children, all but Bernard, who said stoutly:

“I don’t; but I’ll try to. I want to.”

===Chinese===
他们看得出那仍是海藻，虽说它织成了一种了不起的料子。伯纳德、凯思琳和亮片小子不知怎么也都穿上了海藻衣裳，亮片小子也不再是女孩打扮了；梅维斯和弗朗西斯手脚并用地爬上台阶时，低头一看，发现自己身上也穿着海藻的衣裳——“真漂亮，可穿着这个回家多别扭啊。”梅维斯想。

“好了，”人鱼夫人说，“原谅我先斩后奏跳了这一跳。我就知道你们会犹豫个没完，我都开始冒火了！这就是你们那可怕的空气闹的！瞧，现在咱们到了我们王国的大门口。你们是真想进来的，对不对？违着你们的心意，我只能把你们带到这儿，再远就带不动了。而且，除非你们完完全全信任我，你们自己也走不到更远的地方。信吗？肯信吗？试试看！”

“信。”孩子们说。只有伯纳德例外，他斩钉截铁地说：

“我不信；可我愿意试着信。我是想信的。”

===Original===
“If you want to, I think you *do*,” said she very kindly. “And now I will tell you one thing. What you’re breathing isn’t air, and it isn’t water. It’s something that both water people and air people can breathe.”

“The greatest common measure,” said Bernard.

“A simple equation,” said Mavis.

“Things which are equal to the same thing are equal to each other,” said Francis; and the three looked at each other and wondered why they had said such things.

===Chinese===
“你要是想信，那我想你*就是*信的。”她十分和气地说，“现在我要告诉你们一件事。你们此刻呼吸的，不是空气，也不是水。它是水族和空气族都能呼吸的东西。”

“最大公约数。”伯纳德说。

“一个简单方程。”梅维斯说。

“同等于一物者，彼此相等。”弗朗西斯说。说完，三个孩子面面相觑，不明白自己怎么会说出这种话来。

===Original===
“Don’t worry,” said the lady, “it’s only the influence of the place. This is the Cave of Learning, you know, very dark at the beginning and getting lighter and lighter as you get nearer to the golden door. All these rocks are made of books really, and they exude learning from every crack. We cover them up with anemones and seaweed and pretty things as well as we can, but the learning will leak out. Let us go through the gate or you’ll all be talking Sanskrit before we know where we are.”

She opened the gate. A great flood of glorious sunlight met them, the solace of green trees and the jeweled grace of bright blossoms. She pulled them through the door, and shut it.

“This is where we live,” she said. “Aren’t you glad you came?”

===Chinese===
“别担心，”夫人说，“这不过是此地的影响。你们要知道，这是学问洞（Cave of Learning），开头黑得很，越走近金色的门，就越来越亮。这些石头其实全是书做的，每一条缝里都在往外渗学问。我们尽量拿海葵、海藻和各种漂亮东西把它们遮起来，可学问总会渗出来。快穿过这道门吧，不然一眨眼的工夫，你们就要满口梵文了。”

她推开门。一大片灿烂的阳光扑面迎来，还有绿树的慰藉和繁花宝石般的优雅。她把他们拉进门去，把门关上了。

“这就是我们住的地方，”她说，“来了这趟，你们高兴吧？”

## VII. The Skies Are Falling / 七、天塌下来

===Original===
As the children passed through the golden doors a sort of swollen feeling which was beginning to make their heads quite uncomfortable passed away, and left them with a curiously clear and comfortable certainty that they were much cleverer than usual.

===Chinese===
孩子们穿过那几道金门时，一种发胀的感觉消散了——方才它正把他们脑袋胀得说不出的难受——留在心里的，是一种奇异的清明安适，叫他们确信自己比平常聪明得多。

===Original===
“I *could* do sums now, and no mistake,” Bernard whispered to Kathleen, who replied to the effect that dates no longer presented the slightest difficulty to her.

===Chinese===
"我这会儿*连算术都会做了*，错不了。"伯纳德悄悄对凯思琳说；凯思琳回了一句，意思是说，历史年代在她已经丝毫不成问题了。

===Original===
Mavis and Francis felt as though they had never before known what it was to have a clear brain. They followed the others through the golden door, and then came Reuben, and the Mermaid came last. She had picked up her discarded tail and was carrying it over her arm as you might a shawl. She shut the gate, and its lock clicked sharply.

===Chinese===
梅维斯和弗朗西斯只觉得，从前根本不曾知道脑子清爽是什么滋味。他们跟着别人走过金门，随后是鲁本（Reuben），美人鱼殿后。她捡起自己脱下的那条鱼尾，搭在胳膊上，就像你们搭一条披肩那样。她关上大门，门锁咔哒一声脆响。

===Original===
“We have to be careful, you know,” she said, “because of the people in the books. They are always trying to get out of the books that the cave is made of; and some of them are very undesirable characters. There’s a Mrs. Fairchild﻿—we’ve had a great deal of trouble with her, and a person called Mrs. Markham who makes everybody miserable, and a lot of people who think they are being funny when they aren’t﻿—dreadful.”

===Chinese===
"要知道，我们不得不小心，"她说，"全是因为书里那些人。他们总想从砌成山洞的那些书里逃出去，其中有些家伙实在要不得。有一位费尔柴尔德太太（Mrs. Fairchild）——她给我们添过好大的麻烦；还有一个叫马卡姆太太（Mrs. Markham）的，把人人都弄得愁眉苦脸；还有一大批自以为逗乐、其实一点也不逗的角色——可怕极了。"

===Original===
The party was now walking along a smooth grassy path, between tall, clipped box hedges﻿—at least they looked like box hedges, but when Mavis stroked the close face of one she found that it was not stiff box, but soft seaweed.

===Chinese===
一行人这时走在一条平整的草径上，两侧是修剪得整整齐齐的高高黄杨树篱——至少看着像黄杨树篱；可梅维斯伸手抚了抚其中一面密密的篱壁，才发现那不是硬挺的黄杨，而是柔软的海草。

===Original===
“Are we in the water or not?” said she, stopping suddenly.

===Chinese===
"我们这是在水里呢，还是不在水里？"她说着，猛地站住。

===Original===
“That depends on what you mean by water. Water’s a thing human beings can’t breathe, isn’t it? Well, you are breathing. So this can’t be water.”

===Chinese===
"那得看你说的水是什么意思。水嘛，是人类没法呼吸的东西，是不是？可你们这不正呼吸着吗？所以这儿不可能是在水里。"

===Original===
“I see that,” said Mavis, “but the soft seaweed won’t stand up in air, and it does in water.”

===Chinese===
"这我明白，"梅维斯说，"可软软的海草在空气里立不住，在水里才立得住。"

===Original===
“Oh, you’ve found out, have you?” said the Mermaid. “Well, then, perhaps it is water. Only you see it can’t be. Everything’s like that down here.”

===Chinese===
"哦，让你瞧出破绽来啦？"美人鱼说，"那么，这儿兴许真是水。只是你瞧，它又偏偏不可能是。底下的事情，件件都是这个样子。"

===Original===
“Once you said you lived in water, and you wanted to be wet,” said Mavis.

===Chinese===
"你从前说过，你们住在水里，还说自己想要湿着。"梅维斯说。

===Original===
“Mer-people aren’t responsible for what they say in your world. I told you that, you know,” the Mermaid reminded them.

===Chinese===
"人鱼族在你们那个世界里说过的话，是作不得数的。这话我跟你们讲过。"美人鱼提醒他们。

===Original===
Presently they came to a little coral bridge over a stream that flowed still and deep. “But if what we’re in is water, what’s that?” said Bernard, pointing down.

===Chinese===
不久，他们来到一座小小的珊瑚桥前，桥下一道溪流，流得又静又深。"可要是咱们泡着的这算水，那这算什么？"伯纳德指着桥下说。

===Original===
“Ah, now you’re going too deep for me,” said the Mermaid, “at least if I were to answer I should go too deep for you. Come on﻿—we shall be too late for the banquet.”

===Chinese===
"啊，这下你可把我问到深水里去了，"美人鱼说，"至少，我若答了，你们就要跟着沉到深水里去了。走吧——再耽搁，宴席要赶不上了。"

===Original===
“What do you have for the banquet?” Bernard asked; and the Mermaid answered sweetly: “Things to eat.”

===Chinese===
"宴席上有什么吃的？"伯纳德问；美人鱼甜甜地答道："吃的东西。"

===Original===
“And to drink?”

===Chinese===
"那喝的呢？"

===Original===
“It’s no use,” said she; “you can’t get at it that way. We drink﻿—but you wouldn’t understand.”

===Chinese===
"问也是白问，"她说，"你们这么问，是问不出来的。我们也喝——不过你们不会懂的。"

===Original===
Here the grassy road widened, and they came onto a terrace of mother-of-pearl, very smooth and shining. Pearly steps led down from it into the most beautiful garden you could invent if you tried for a year and a day with all the loveliest pictures and the most learned books on gardening to help you. But the odd thing about it was that when they came to talk it over afterward they never could agree about the shape of the beds, the direction of the walks, the kinds and colors of the flowers, or indeed any single thing about it. But to each it seemed and will always seem the most beautiful garden ever imagined or invented. And everyone saw, beyond a distant belt of trees the shining domes and minarets of very beautiful buildings, and far, far away there was a sound of music, so far away that at first they could only hear the music and not the tune. But soon that too was plain, and it was the most beautiful tune in the world.

===Chinese===
草径到这里豁然开阔，他们登上一座珍珠母铺就的平台，又平又滑，流光溢彩。一串珠光灿灿的台阶从平台上引下去，通向一座天下最美的花园——任你借上所有最美的图画、最渊博的园艺书，苦想上一年零一天，也造不出比它更美的园子来。只是这园子有桩怪处：事后他们凑在一起回味时，花坛是什么形状、小径朝哪个方向、花草是何等品类颜色，乃至关于它的任何一桩细节，他们从来都对不上口径。可在每个人心里，它当时是、将来也永远是人所能梦想、所能造出的最美的花园。隔着远处一带树影，人人都望见了美丽楼宇闪光的穹顶与尖塔；远远地、远远地，还传来一缕乐声，远得起初只听得见音乐，辨不出曲调。可不久曲调也清清楚楚了，那是世上最美的曲子。

===Original===
“Crikey,” said Reuben, speaking suddenly and for the first time, “ain’t it ’evingly neither. Not arf,” he added with decision.

===Chinese===
"乖乖，"鲁本突然开了口，这是他头一回说话，"这可不就是上了天堂嘛。可不是咋的。"他又斩钉截铁地补了一句。

===Original===
“Now,” said the Mermaid, as they neared the belt of trees, “you are going to receive something.”

===Chinese===
"听着，"一行人走近那带树影时，美人鱼说，"你们就要领受一样东西了。"

===Original===
“Oh, thank you,” said everybody, and no one liked to add: “What?”﻿—though that simple word trembled on every tongue. It slipped off the tip of Reuben’s, indeed, at last, and the Mermaid answered:

===Chinese===
"哦，谢谢你。"人人都说；谁也不好意思再添一句"什么？"——尽管这个简单的字眼在每条舌头上打颤。到头来，它终究从鲁本的舌尖上滑了出去，美人鱼便答道：

===Original===
“An ovation.”

===Chinese===
"一场夹道欢呼（ovation）。"

===Original===
“That’s something to do with eggs, I know,” said Kathleen. “Father was saying so only the other day.”

===Chinese===
"我知道，这个跟鸡蛋沾亲带故，"凯思琳说，"前几天爸爸还这么说来着。"

===Original===
“There will be no eggs in this,” said the Mermaid, “and you may find it a trifle heavy. But when it is over the fun begins. Don’t be frightened, Kathleen﻿—Mavis, don’t smooth your hair. Ugly untidiness is impossible here. You are about to be publicly thanked by our Queen. You’d rather not? You should have thought of that before. If you will go about doing these noble deeds of rescue you must expect to be thanked. Now, don’t forget to bow. And there’s nothing to be frightened of.”

===Chinese===
"这场里头可没有鸡蛋，"美人鱼说，"而且你们兴许会觉得它有点儿沉。不过等它一完，乐子就开场了。别害怕，凯思琳——梅维斯，别捋头发了。丑兮兮的乱相，在这儿是不会有的。你们就要当众受我们女王致谢了。你们不情愿？当初就该想到哇。既然你们肯到处去做这些舍己救人的义举，就得指望着受人感谢。记着，别忘了鞠躬。没什么可怕的。"

===Original===
They passed through the trees and came on a sort of open courtyard in front of a palace of gleaming pearl and gold. There on a silver throne sat the loveliest lady in the world. She wore a starry crown and a gown of green, and golden shoes, and she smiled at them so kindly that they forgot any fear they may have felt. The music ended on a note of piercing sweetness and in the great hush that followed the children felt themselves gently pushed forward to the foot of the throne. All around was a great crowd, forming a circle about the pearly pavement on which they stood.

===Chinese===
他们穿过树林，来到一座光华闪闪的珍珠黄金宫殿前的一片开阔庭院。白银的宝座上，坐着天下最可爱的一位夫人。她头戴星冠，身穿绿裙，脚踏金鞋；她朝孩子们笑得那样慈和，把也许有过的几分怕意都笑没了。乐声在一个清冽甜美的音符上收束，随后是一片盛大的静默；静默中，孩子们觉得自己被轻轻推到宝座跟前。四下里围着一大群人，在他们立足的珠光地坪外站成一圈。

===Original===
The Queen rose up in her place and reached toward them the end of her scepter where shone a star like those that crowned her.

===Chinese===
女王从座上立起身，把权杖的顶端朝他们递过来，杖头镶着一颗星，同加在她头顶后冠上的那些星星一模一样，闪着光。

===Original===
“Welcome,” she said in a voice far sweeter than the music, “Welcome to our Home. You have been kind, you have been brave, you have been unselfish, and all my subjects do homage to you.”

===Chinese===
"欢迎，"她开口，嗓音比乐声甜美得多，"欢迎你们光临我们的家。你们仁爱，你们勇敢，你们无私，我全体臣民都向你们致敬。"

===Original===
At the word the whole of that great crowd bent toward them like bulrushes in the wind, and the Queen herself came down the steps of her throne and held out her hands to the children.

===Chinese===
话音落地，那一大群人齐齐朝他们俯下身去，像风中的芦苇；女王亲自走下宝座的台阶，向孩子们伸出双手。

===Original===
A choking feeling in their throats became almost unbearable as those kind hands rested on one head after another.

===Chinese===
那双慈爱的手挨个儿抚在他们头上，他们喉咙里的哽咽，几乎忍无可忍。

===Original===
Then the crowd raised itself and stood upright, and someone called out in a voice like a trumpet:

===Chinese===
随后人群直起身，站得笔挺，有人用喇叭也似的声音高声喊道：

===Original===
“The children saved one of us﻿—*We die in captivity*. Shout for the children. Shout!”

===Chinese===
"孩子们救了我们中的一个——*我们被俘必死*。为孩子们欢呼吧。欢呼！"

===Original===
And a roar like the roar of wild waves breaking on rocks went up from the great crowd that stood all about them. There was a fluttering of flags or handkerchiefs﻿—the children could not tell which﻿—and then the voice of their own Mermaid, saying: “There﻿—that’s over. And now we shall have the banquet. Shan’t we, Mamma?”

===Chinese===
围站在他们四周的一大群人轰然发出吼声，像野浪扑碎在礁石上。旗帜还是手帕，一时翻飞——孩子们分不清是哪种——随后传来他们那位美人鱼的声音："好啦——这一节过去了。现在咱们要开宴了。对不对，妈妈？"

===Original===
“Yes, my daughter,” said the Queen.

===Chinese===
"对，我的女儿。"女王说。

===Original===
So the Mermaid they had rescued was a Queen’s daughter!

===Chinese===
这么说，他们救下的那条美人鱼，竟是女王的女儿！

===Original===
“I didn’t know you were a Princess,” said Mavis, as they followed the Queen along a corridor.

===Chinese===
"我还不知道你是位公主呢。"他们随女王走过一道长廊时，梅维斯说。

===Original===
“That’s why they have made such a fuss, I suppose,” said Bernard.

===Chinese===
"难怪他们这么大张旗鼓，我猜。"伯纳德说。

===Original===
“Oh, no, we should have given the ovation to anyone who had saved any of us from captivity. We love giving ovations. Only we so seldom get the chance, and even ordinary entertaining is difficult. People are so prejudiced. We can hardly ever get anyone to come and visit us. I shouldn’t have got you if you hadn’t happened to find that cave. It would have been quite impossible for me to give Kathleen that clinging embrace from shallow water. The cave water is so much more buoyant than the sea. I daresay you noticed that.”

===Chinese===
"哦不，不管是谁，只要把我们哪一个从囚禁里救出来，我们都会给他这样一场欢呼的。我们最爱办欢呼了。只可惜这样的机会难得，就连寻常待客也不容易。世人的成见太深。我们几乎从来请不到谁来家里做客。若不是你们碰巧发现那个山洞，我根本没法把你们弄来。要隔着浅水给凯思琳那样紧紧一搂，是万万办不到的。洞里的水比海水浮力大得多。想来你们也察觉了。"

===Original===
Yes﻿—they had.

===Chinese===
是的——他们察觉了。

===Original===
“May we sit next you at the banquet?” Kathleen asked suddenly, “because, you know, it’s all rather strange to us.”

===Chinese===
"宴席上，我们能挨着你坐吗？"凯思琳突然问，"因为，你知道，这一切对我们未免太生疏啦。"

===Original===
“Of course, dear,” said the sea lady.

===Chinese===
"当然可以，亲爱的。"海姑娘说。

===Original===
“But,” said Bernard, “I’m awfully sorry, but I think we ought to go home.”

===Chinese===
"可是，"伯纳德说，"实在对不住，我想我们该回家了。"

===Original===
“Oh, don’t talk of it,” said the Mermaid. “Why, you’ve only just come.”

===Chinese===
"哎呀，别说这个，"美人鱼说，"怎么，你们这才刚到呢。"

===Original===
Bernard muttered something about getting home in time to wash for tea.

===Chinese===
伯纳德含含糊糊咕哝了几句，说是得赶在喝茶前到家，好洗一洗。

===Original===
“There’ll be heaps of time,” said Francis impatiently; “don’t fuss and spoil everything.”

===Chinese===
"时间多的是，"弗朗西斯不耐烦地说，"别穷操心，把什么都搅和了。"

===Original===
“I’m not fussing,” said Bernard, stolid as ever. “I never fuss. But I think we ought to be thinking of getting home.”

===Chinese===
"我才不是穷操心，"伯纳德说，还是那副死心眼，"我从不瞎操心。可我想，我们该把回家的事放在心上了。"

===Original===
“Well, think about it then,” said Francis impatiently, and turned to admire the clusters of scarlet flowers that hung from the pillars of the gallery.

===Chinese===
"那就想你的去吧，"弗朗西斯不耐烦地说，转身去欣赏廊柱上悬垂的一簇簇猩红花朵。

===Original===
The banquet was very magnificent, but they never could remember afterward what it was that they ate out of the silver dishes and drank out of the golden cups. They none of them forgot the footmen, however, who were dressed in tight-fitting suits of silver scales, with silver fingerless gloves, and a sort of helmet on that made them look less like people than like fish, as Kathleen said.

===Chinese===
宴席极尽豪奢，可事后他们怎么也想不起来，银盘里吃过、金杯里喝过的究竟是什么。那些侍者，他们却谁也没忘：侍者一身银鳞的紧身衣，戴银色的无指手套，头上扣着一种头盔，照凯思琳的说法，把他们衬得不像人、倒更像鱼。

===Original===
“But they *are* fish,” said the Princess, opening her beautiful eyes; “they’re the Salmoners, and the one behind Mother’s chair is the Grand Salmoner. In your country I have heard there are Grand Almoners. We have Grand Salmoners.”

===Chinese===
"可他们*本来就是*鱼呀，"公主睁大她那双美丽的眼睛说，"他们是鲑赈员（Salmoners），妈妈座椅后头站着的那位是大鲑赈官。听说在你们国家，有大施赈官。我们有的是大鲑赈官。"

===Original===
“Are all your servants fish?” Mavis asked.

===Chinese===
"你们的仆人全都是鱼吗？"梅维斯问。

===Original===
“Of course,” said the Princess, “but we don’t use servants much except for state occasions. Most of our work is done by the lower orders﻿—electric eels, most of them. We get all the power for our machinery from them.”

===Chinese===
"那当然，"公主说，"只是除了大典之日，我们不大用得着仆人。大半的活计都由下等族类来做——多半是电鳗。我们机器的动力，全从它们身上取。"

===Original===
“How do you do it?” Bernard asked, with a fleeting vision of being some day known as the great man who discovered the commercial value of the electricity obtainable from eels.

===Chinese===
"怎么个取法呢？"伯纳德问，脑中闪过一个念头：有朝一日，自己将以那位发现了鳗鱼发电之商业价值的大人物而名扬四海。

===Original===
“We keep a tank of them,” said she, “and you just turn a tap﻿—they’re connected up to people’s houses﻿—and you connect them with your looms or lathes or whatever you’re working. That sets up a continuous current and the eels swim around and around in the current till the work’s done. It’s beautifully simple.”

===Chinese===
"我们养着一池子，"她说，"只管拧开龙头就行——它们统统接着通到各家各户——你要用的时候，把它们接上你的织机呀车床呀，或者你干活的什么家伙。这样便生出一股不断的电流，电鳗就在电流里一圈一圈地游，直到活儿干完。简单得漂亮。"

===Original===
“It’s simply beautiful,” said Mavis warmly. “I mean all this.” She waved her hand to the row of white arches through which the green of the garden and the blue of what looked like the sky showed plainly. “And you live down here and do nothing but play all day long? How lovely.”

===Chinese===
"真是漂亮极了，"梅维斯热烈地说，"我是说这一切。"她朝那一排白色的拱门挥了挥手，门外园子的绿意和那片看上去像天空的蔚蓝，清清楚楚地透进来。"你们就住在这底下，成天什么也不干，光是玩？多美啊。"

===Original===
“You’d soon get tired of play if you did nothing else,” said Bernard wisely. “At least I know I should. Did you ever make a steam engine?” he asked the Princess. “That’s what I call work.”

===Chinese===
"要是除了玩什么都不干，你们很快就会玩腻的，"伯纳德一副明白人的口气说，"至少我知道我准会腻。你做过蒸汽机吗？"他问公主，"那才是我所说的工作。"

===Original===
“It would be, to me,” she said, “but don’t you know that work is what you have to do and don’t like doing? And play’s whatever you want to do. Have some more Andrew Aromaticus.”

===Chinese===
"在我，那就算是工作了，"她说，"可你不知道吗——工作，就是你不得不做又不爱做的事；玩，就是你心想做的随便什么。再来点安德鲁香果（Andrew Aromaticus）吧。"

===Original===
She made a sign to a Salmoner, who approached with a great salver of fruit. The company were seated by fours and fives and sixes at little tables, such as you see in the dining rooms of the big hotels where people feed who have motors. These little tables are good for conversation.

===Chinese===
她向一名鲑赈员递了个眼色，那侍者便捧着一大银盘水果走上前来。宾主们四个一桌、五个一桌、六个一桌，分坐在一张张小桌旁，就像你们在大饭店的餐厅里见到的那些小桌——上那些饭店吃饭的，都是坐汽车来的人。这种小桌最便于交谈。

===Original===
“Then what *do* you do?” Kathleen asked.

===Chinese===
"那你们*到底*做些什么呀？"凯思琳问。

===Original===
“Well, we have to keep all the rivers flowing, for one thing﻿—the earthly rivers, I mean﻿—and to see to the rain and snow taps, and to attend to the tides and whirlpools, and open the cages where the winds are kept. Oh, it’s no easy business being a Princess in our country, I can tell you, whatever it may be in yours. What do your Princesses do? Do they open the wind cages?”

===Chinese===
"这个嘛，头一件，我们得让所有的河流流个不停——我是说陆地上的河——还得照管落雨落雪的龙头，照应潮汐和漩涡，还要去打开那些关着风的笼子。哦，我可以告诉你，在我们国家当公主可不是轻松差事，你们国家怎样我就不知道了。你们的公主都做些什么？她们开风的笼子吗？"

===Original===
“I﻿ ﻿… I don’t know,” said the children. “I think they only open bazaars.”

===Chinese===
"我……我不知道，"孩子们说，"我想，她们就只会给义卖会剪彩吧。"

===Original===
“Mother says they work awfully hard, and they go and see people who are ill in hospitals,” Kathleen was beginning, but at this moment the Queen rose and so did everyone else.

===Chinese===
"妈妈说，她们干活辛苦极了，还要去医院看望病人——"凯思琳刚开了个头，这时女王站起身来，众人也纷纷起立。

===Original===
“Come,” said the Princess, “I must go and take my turn at river-filling. Only Princesses can do the finest sort of work.”

===Chinese===
"来吧，"公主说，"我得去轮我灌河的班了。顶精细的活儿，只有公主干得了。"

===Original===
“What is the hardest thing you have to do?” Francis asked as they walked out into the garden.

===Chinese===
"你们要做的活计里，顶难的是什么？"他们往园子里走时，弗朗西斯问。

===Original===
“Keeping the sea out of our kingdom,” was the answer, “and fighting the Under Folk. We kept the sea out by trying very hard with both hands, inside our minds. And, of course, the sky helps.”

===Chinese===
"把海水挡在王国之外，"对方答道，"还有跟深渊族（the Under Folk）打仗。挡海水嘛，靠的是在心里头用两只手死命使劲。当然啦，天穹也帮衬着。"

===Original===
“And how do you fight the Under Folk﻿—and who are they?” Bernard wanted to know.

===Chinese===
"那你们怎么跟深渊族打——他们又是些什么人？"伯纳德想知道。

===Original===
“Why, the thickheaded, heavy people who live in the deep sea.”

===Chinese===
"嗐，就是住在深海里那些脑子不开窍、沉甸甸的家伙呗。"

===Original===
“Different from you?” Kathleen asked.

===Chinese===
"跟你们不一样吗？"凯思琳问。

===Original===
“My dear child!”

===Chinese===
"哎，亲爱的孩子！"

===Original===
“She means,” explained Mavis, “that we didn’t know there were any other kind of people in the sea except your kind.”

===Chinese===
"她的意思是，"梅维斯解释道，"我们原先不知道，海里除了你们这一族，还有别样的人。"

===Original===
“You know much less about us than we do about you,” said the Princess. “Of course there are different nations and tribes, and different customs and dresses and everything. But there are two great divisions down here besides us, the Thick-Heads and the Thin-Skins, and we have to fight both of them. The Thin-Skins live near the surface of the water, frivolous, silly things like nautiluses and flying fish, very pleasant, but deceitful and light-minded. They are very treacherous. The Thick-Heads live in the cold deep dark waters. They are desperate people.”

===Chinese===
"你们对我们的了解，远不如我们对你们的了解，"公主说，"海里当然有各种民族、各种部落，风俗、衣着、样样都各不相同。可除了我们这一族，底下还有两大支：厚头族（the Thick-Heads）和薄皮族（the Thin-Skins），这两族我们都得打。薄皮族住在靠近水面的地方，都是些轻浮愚蠢的玩意儿，像鹦鹉螺呀、飞鱼呀，挺讨人喜欢，可又爱撒谎、没个定性，奸诈得很。厚头族住在又冷又深又黑的水里，是些亡命之徒。"

===Original===
“Do you ever go down there?”

===Chinese===
"你们下到那儿去过吗？"

===Original===
The Princess shuddered.

===Chinese===
公主打了个寒噤。

===Original===
“No,” she said, “but we might have to. If the water ever came into our kingdom they would attack us, and we should have to drive them out; and then we should have to drive them right down to their own kingdom again. It happened once, in my grandfather’s time.”

===Chinese===
"没有，"她说，"可没准儿有一天不得不去。万一水进了我们的王国，他们就会来攻打我们，我们就得把他们赶出去；赶出去之后，还得一路把他们赶回他们自己的王国去。这种事发生过一回，在我祖父的时代。"

===Original===
“But how on earth,” asked Bernard, “did you ever get the water out again?”

===Chinese===
"可是你们究竟，"伯纳德问，"后来是怎么把水再弄出去的呢？"

===Original===
“It wasn’t on earth, you know,” said the Princess, “and the Whales blew a good deal of it out﻿—the Grampuses did their best, but they don’t blow hard enough. And the Octopuses finished the work by sucking the water out with their suckers.”

===Chinese===
"要知道，那可不是在陆地上办的事，"公主说，"鲸鱼们把好些水都吹了出去——虎鲸们也尽了力，可它们喷得不够带劲。末了是章鱼们用吸盘把水吸干，才算收了工。"

===Original===
“Do you have cats here then?” asked Kathleen, whose attention had wandered, and had only caught a word that sounded like Pussies.

===Chinese===
"这么说，你们这儿有猫了？"凯思琳问。她方才走了神，只抓住了一个听着像'猫咪'的词。

===Original===
“Only Octopussies,” said the Princess, “but then they’re eight times as pussy as your dry-land cats.”

===Chinese===
"只有八爪猫儿（Octopussies），"公主说，"不过话说回来，它们的猫劲儿，可比你们旱地上的猫大上八倍。"

===Original===
What Kathleen’s attention had wandered to was a tall lady standing on a marble pedestal in the middle of a pool. She held a big vase over her head, and from it poured a thin stream of water. This stream fell in an arch right across the pool into a narrow channel cut in the marble of the square in which they now stood, ran across the square, and disappeared under a dark arch in the face of the rock.

===Chinese===
凯思琳方才走神看的，是立在池子中央一座大理石基座上的一位高挑夫人。她把一只大瓮举过头顶，瓮里泻出一股细细的水流。这股水流成一道拱弧，横跨池面，落进他们此刻站着的这片大理石场坪上凿出的一条窄槽里，流过场坪，钻进岩壁上一个黑黢黢的拱洞，不见了。

===Original===
“There,” said the Princess, stopping.

===Chinese===
"到了。"公主说着，停下脚步。

===Original===
“What is it?” asked Reuben, who had been singularly silent.

===Chinese===
"这是个啥？"一直出奇地沉默的鲁本问。

===Original===
“This,” she said simply, “is the source of the Nile. And of all other rivers. And it’s my turn now. I must not speak again till my term of source-service is at an end. Do what you will. Go where you will. All is yours. Only beware that you do not touch the sky. If once profane hands touch the sky the whole heaven is overwhelmed.”

===Chinese===
"这个嘛，"她平平淡淡地说，"就是尼罗河的源头。也是普天之下所有河流的源头。现在轮到我了。守源值役满期之前，我不能再开口说话。随你们做什么，随你们去哪里，这里的一切都归你们。只一件要当心：别去碰天。凡俗之手一碰着天，整重天穹便要倾覆。"

===Original===
She ran a few steps, jumped, and landed on the marble pedestal without touching the lady who stood there already. Then, with the utmost care, so that the curved arc of the water should not be slackened or diverted, she took the vase in her hands and the other lady in her turn leaped across the pool and stood beside the children and greeted them kindly.

===Chinese===
她跑了几步，纵身一跳，落上大理石基座，没有碰着原先立在那儿的夫人。随后她极其小心地——不让那道弯弯的水弧松懈半分、偏斜半分——双手接过大瓮；那位夫人便轮到她跳过池子，站到孩子们身边，和蔼地招呼他们。

===Original===
“I am Maia. My sister has told me all you did for her,” she said; “it was I who pinched your foot,” and as she spoke they knew the voice that had said, among the seaweed-covered rocks at Beachfield: “Save her. We die in captivity.”

===Chinese===
"我是迈亚（Maia）。我妹妹把你们为她做的一切都讲给我听了，"她说，"掐你们脚的，就是我。"她一开口，他们便认出了这个声音——在比奇菲尔德海藻覆顶的礁石间说过的那个声音："救救她。我们被俘必死。"

===Original===
“What will you do?” she asked, “while my sister performs her source-service?”

===Chinese===
"在我妹妹行守源值役的当儿，你们打算做些什么呢？"她问。

===Original===
“Wait, I suppose,” said Bernard. “You see we want to know about going home.”

===Chinese===
"等着呗，我猜，"伯纳德说，"你是知道的，我们想知道回家的事。"

===Original===
“Didn’t you fix a time to be recalled?” asked Maia. And when they said no, her beautiful smiling face suddenly looked grave.

===Chinese===
"你们没定下召回的时辰吗？"迈亚问。听他们答说没有，她那张含笑的美丽面庞顿时郑重起来。

===Original===
“With whom have you left the charge of speaking the spell of recall?”

===Chinese===
"那念召回咒的差事，你们交给谁了？"

===Original===
“I don’t know what you mean,” said Bernard. “What spell?”

===Chinese===
"我不明白你的意思，"伯纳德说，"什么咒？"

===Original===
“The one which enabled me to speak to you that day in the shallows,” said Maia. “Of course my sister explained to you that the spell which enables us to come at your call is the only one by which you can yourselves return.”

===Chinese===
"就是那天在浅水里让我能对你们说话的那道咒，"迈亚说，"我妹妹当然给你们讲过了：那道能让我们应声而来的咒，也就是你们自己回去的唯一门路。"

===Original===
“She didn’t,” said Mavis.

===Chinese===
"她没讲过。"梅维斯说。

===Original===
“Ah, she is young and impulsive. But no doubt she arranged with someone to speak the spell and recall you?”

===Chinese===
"啊，她年纪轻，性子急。可她总归跟什么人讲好了、让人念咒召你们回去的吧？"

===Original===
“No, she didn’t. She doesn’t know any land people except us. She told me so,” said Kathleen.

===Chinese===
"没有，她没跟谁讲好。除了我们，她一个陆地上的人也不认识。她自己说的。"凯思琳道。

===Original===
“Well, is the spell written anywhere?” Maia asked.

===Chinese===
"那么，那道咒写在什么地方没有？"迈亚问。

===Original===
“Under a picture” they told her, not knowing that it was also written in the works of Mr. John Milton.

===Chinese===
"在一幅画底下。"他们告诉她。他们并不知道，这道咒在约翰·弥尔顿（John Milton）先生的大作里也写着呢。

===Original===
“Then I’m afraid you’ll have to wait ’til someone happens to read what is under the picture,” said Maia kindly.

===Chinese===
"那么，恐怕你们只好等下去，等哪天有人碰巧念了那幅画底下的字了。"迈亚语气温和地说。

===Original===
“But the house is locked up; there’s no one there to read anything,” Bernard reminded them.

===Chinese===
"可那座房子锁着门呢；里头一个人也没有，什么都念不成。"伯纳德提醒大家。

===Original===
There was a dismal silence. Then:

===Chinese===
一阵愁惨的沉默。然后：

===Original===
“Perhaps burglars will break in and read it,” suggested Reuben kindly. “Anyhow, what’s the use of kicking up a shine about it? *I* can’t see what you want to go back for. It’s a little bit of all right here, so it is﻿—I *don’t* think. Plucky sight better than anything *I* ever come across. I’m a-goin’ to enjoy myself I am, and see all the sights. Miss, there, said we might.”

===Chinese===
"兴许会有小偷撬门进去，把它念出来呢，"鲁本好心地出主意，"再说，为这点事哭丧着脸，有啥用处？*我*就瞧不出你们干吗非要回去。这地方真是没得挑，真的——要说有得挑，*我*头一个不信。比我*这辈子*碰见过的什么都强出一大截。我呀，这就去痛痛快快地玩，把景致瞧个遍。那边那位小姐说了，咱们可以随便逛。"

===Original===
“Well spoken indeed,” said Maia, smiling at his earnest face. “That is the true spirit of the explorer.”

===Chinese===
"说得真好，"迈亚望着他那张诚恳的脸，微笑道，"这才是探险家真正的精神。"

===Original===
“But we’re not explorers,” said Mavis, a little crossly, for her; “and we’re not so selfish as you think, either. Mother will be awfully frightened if we’re not home to tea. She’ll think we’re drowned.”

===Chinese===
"可我们不是探险家，"梅维斯说——就她而言，这口气算够冲的了，"我们也不像你想的那样自私。要是喝茶时我们还没到家，妈妈会吓坏了的。她会以为我们淹死了。"

===Original===
“Well, you *are* drowned,” said Maia brightly. “At least that’s what I believe you land people call it when you come down to us and neglect to arrange to have the spell of return said for you.”

===Chinese===
"这个嘛，你们*确实*是淹死了，"迈亚轻快地说，"至少我料想，你们陆上人对这种事是这么叫法的：人下到我们这儿来，又忘了安排人替他们念返家的咒。"

===Original===
“How horrible,” said Mavis. “Oh, Cathay,” and she clutched her sister tightly.

===Chinese===
"太可怕了，"梅维斯说，"哦，凯西（Cathay）。"她一把紧紧搂住妹妹。

===Original===
“But you needn’t *stay* drowned,” said the Princess. “Someone’s sure to say the spell somehow or other. I assure you that this is true; and then you will go home with the speed of an eel.”

===Chinese===
"可你们用不着*一直*淹死着，"公主说，"不管怎样，总会有人把那道咒念出来的。我向你担保这是真的；到那时，你们就会以鳗鱼的速度回家去。"

===Original===
They felt, somehow, in their bones that this was true, and it consoled them a little. Things which you feel in your bones are most convincing.

===Chinese===
不知怎么，他们打骨子里觉得这是真的，这让他们稍稍得了些安慰。凡是打骨子里认定的事，最教人信服。

===Original===
“But Mother,” said Mavis.

===Chinese===
"可是妈妈呢，"梅维斯说。

===Original===
“You don’t seem to know much about magic,” said Maia pityingly: “the first principle of magic is that time spent in other worlds doesn’t count in your own home. No, I see you don’t understand. In your home it’s still the same time as it was when you dived into the well in the cave.”

===Chinese===
"你们对魔法好像知道得不多，"迈亚怜惜地说，"魔法的第一条原理就是：在别的世界里花掉的时间，在自家家里是不算数的。不，我看你们还是不明白。在你们家里，此刻还停在你们跳进山洞里那口井的时候呢。"

===Original===
“But that’s hours ago,” said Bernard; and she answered:

===Chinese===
"可那已是好几个钟头以前的事了。"伯纳德说；她答道：

===Original===
“I know. But your time is not like our time at all.”

===Chinese===
"我知道。可你们的时间跟我们的时间，压根儿不是一回事。"

===Original===
“What’s the difference?”

===Chinese===
"有什么不一样？"

===Original===
“I can’t explain,” said the Princess. “You can’t compare them any more than you can compare a starlight and a starfish. They’re quite, quite different. But the really important thing is that your Mother won’t be anxious. So now why not enjoy yourselves?”

===Chinese===
"没法解释，"公主说，"这两样没法比较，就像星光跟海星没法比较一样。它们完完全全是两码事。不过真正要紧的是：你们的妈妈不会着急。那么现在，何不痛痛快快地玩呢？"

===Original===
And all this time the other Princess had been holding up the jar which was the source of all the rivers in all the world.

===Chinese===
而这全部时间里，另一位公主一直高举着那口瓮——普天之下所有河流的源头。

===Original===
“Won’t she be very tired?” asked Reuben.

===Chinese===
"她不会累得很吗？"鲁本问。

===Original===
“Yes, but suppose all the rivers dried up﻿—and she had to know how people were suffering﻿—that would be something much harder to bear than tiredness. Look in the pool and see what she is doing for the world.”

===Chinese===
"会的。可你想想，万一所有的河都干了——她还得知道世人正受着怎样的苦——那可比累难受多啦。往池子里瞧瞧，看看她正为这个世界做什么。"

===Original===
They looked, and it was like a colored cinematograph; and the pictures melted into one another like the old dissolving views that children used to love so before cinematographs were thought of.

===Chinese===
他们望去，那池子就像一部彩色电影；画面一幅融进一幅，像从前那种幻灯换景——在有人想出电影之前，孩子们顶顶喜欢那个。

===Original===
They saw the Red Indians building their wigwams by the great rivers﻿—and the beavers building their dams across the little rivers; they saw brown men setting their fish traps by the Nile, and brown girls sending out little golden-lighted love-ships on the Ganges. They saw the stormy splendor of the St. Lawrence, and the Medway’s pastoral peace. Little streams dappled with sunlight and the shadow of green leaves, and the dark and secret torrents that tear through the underworld in caverns and hidden places. They saw women washing clothes in the Seine, and boys sailing boats on the Serpentine. Naked savages dancing in masks beside tropical streams overshadowed by strange trees and flowers that we do not know﻿—and men in flannels and girls in pink and blue, punting in the backwaters of the Thames. They saw Niagara and the Zambesi Falls; and all the time the surface of the pool was smooth as a mirror and the arched stream that was the source of all they saw poured ceaselessly over their heads and fell splashing softly into its little marble channel.

===Chinese===
他们看见印第安人在大江大河边搭棚屋——河狸在小河上垒坝；看见棕色皮肤的男人在尼罗河边下鱼笼，棕色的姑娘把亮着金莹莹小灯的爱之小船放进恒河。他们看见圣劳伦斯河风狂浪骤的壮美，也看见梅德韦河田园牧歌的安宁。有缀满日光与绿叶之影的小溪，也有黑沉沉、隐秘秘的急流，在地底的洞窟与幽僻之处冲撞奔突。他们看见妇人在塞纳河里洗衣裳，男孩子们在蛇形湖上放船。赤身的野人戴着面具，在热带溪流旁跳舞，头顶遮着我们所不识的奇树异花——也有穿法兰绒的男子和着粉着蓝的姑娘，撑着长篙，荡在泰晤士河的静水回流里。他们看见了尼亚加拉和赞比西瀑布；而这全部时间里，池面平滑如镜，那道拱形水流——他们所见一切的源头——不住地从他们头顶泻过，轻柔地溅落进它那小小的大理石槽里。

===Original===
I don’t know how long they would have stayed leaning their elbows on the cool parapet and looking down on the changing pictures, but suddenly a trumpet sounded, drums beat, and everyone looked up.

===Chinese===
他们胳膊肘支在清凉的石栏上，低头看着一帧帧变幻的画面——这样待下去会待多久，我可说不上；忽然号角吹响，鼓声大作，人人抬头张望。

===Original===
“It’s for the review,” said Maia, through the rattle of the drums. “Do you care for soldiers?”

===Chinese===
"这是阅兵，"鼓声咚咚里，迈亚说，"你们喜欢士兵吗？"

===Original===
“Rather,” said Bernard, “but I didn’t know you had soldiers.”

===Chinese===
"喜欢得很，"伯纳德说，"可我不知道你们还有兵。"

===Original===
“We’re very proud of our troops,” said the Princess. “I am Colonel of the Lobster Battalion, and my sister commands the Crustacean Brigade; but we’re not going on parade today.”

===Chinese===
"我们的军队可让我们骄傲呢，"公主说，"我是龙虾营的上校，我妹妹统领甲壳旅；不过今天我们不参加检阅。"

===Original===
The sound of drums was drawing nearer. “This way to the parade ground,” said the Princess, leading the way. They looked at the review through a big arch, and it was like looking into a very big aquarium.

===Chinese===
鼓声越来越近。"检阅场这边走。"公主说着，领路前行。他们从一座大拱门里观礼，那光景，就像往一口硕大无朋的水族箱里张望。

===Original===
The first regiment they saw was, as it happened, the 23rd Lobsters.

===Chinese===
他们瞧见的第一支部队，碰巧是第二十三龙虾团。

===Original===
If you can imagine a Lobster as big as a Guardsman, and rather stouter, you will have some idea of the splendid appearance of this regiment. Only don’t forget that Lobsters in their natural regimentals are not red. They wear a sort of steel-blue armor, and carry arms of dreadful precision. They are terrible fellows, the 23rd, and they marched with an air at once proud and confident.

===Chinese===
你要是能想象一只近卫兵那么大、还要更粗壮些的龙虾，这支团队的威武气派你就能想见几分了。只是别忘了，龙虾天生的军装并不是红的。它们披一身钢蓝色的甲胄，操着精确得吓人的兵器。第二十三团都是些狠角色，行进间那份气概，又骄傲，又自信。

===Original===
Then came the 16th Swordfish﻿—in uniform of delicate silver, their drawn swords displayed.

===Chinese===
接着是第十六剑鱼团——军服是纤巧的银色，出鞘的剑锋齐齐亮出。

===Original===
The Queen’s Own Gurnards were magnificent in pink and silver, with real helmets and spiked collars; and the Boy Scouts﻿—“The Sea Urchins” as they were familiarly called﻿—were the last of the infantry.

===Chinese===
女王直属鲂鮄团一身粉色配银，威风凛凛，戴的是真正的头盔，衬着带尖刺的项圈；压轴的步兵是那支童子军——大伙儿顺口管他们叫"海胆们"。

===Original===
Then came Mermen, mounted on Dolphins and Sea Horses, and the Cetacean Regiments, riding on their whales. Each whale carried a squadron.

===Chinese===
然后是骑着海豚和海马的雄人鱼，还有鲸类各团，骑坐自家的鲸鱼。每头鲸驮一个中队。

===Original===
“They look like great trams going by,” said Francis. And so they did. The children remarked that while the infantry walked upright like any other foot soldiers, the cavalry troops seemed to be, with their mounts, suspended in the air about a foot from the ground.

===Chinese===
"它们瞧着倒像一列列开过去的大电车。"弗朗西斯说。瞧着也真像。孩子们注意到，步兵同寻常步兵一样直着身子走路，骑兵队伍却似乎连人带坐骑悬在离地约一英尺的空中。

===Original===
“And that shows it’s water,” said Bernard.

===Chinese===
"这就说明这儿是水。"伯纳德说。

===Original===
“No, it doesn’t,” said Francis.

===Chinese===
"不，说明不了。"弗朗西斯说。

===Original===
“Well, a whale’s not a bird,” said Bernard.

===Chinese===
"哼，鲸鱼可不是鸟。"伯纳德说。

===Original===
“And there are other things besides air and water,” said Francis.

===Chinese===
"再说，除了空气和水，还有别的东西呢。"弗朗西斯说。

===Original===
The Household Brigade was perhaps the handsomest. The Grand Salmoner led his silvery soldiers, and the 100th Halibuts were evidently the sort of troops to make the foes of anywhere “feel sorry they were born.”

===Chinese===
最威风的恐怕要数禁卫旅。大鲑赈官率领他那些银亮的士兵，而第一百大比目鱼团，一看就是那种走到哪儿都教敌人"悔不该生下来"的队伍。

===Original===
It was a glorious review, and when it was over the children found that they had been quite forgetting their desire to get home.

===Chinese===
好一场盛大的阅兵。阅兵结束，孩子们发觉，自己把回家的念想忘了个干净。

===Original===
But as the back of the last Halibut vanished behind the seaweed trees the desire came back with full force. Princess Maia had disappeared. Their own Princess was, they supposed, still performing her source-service.

===Chinese===
可当最后一条大比目鱼的背影消失在海草树后头，那念想又全须全尾地回来了。迈亚公主不见了。他们自己那位公主，他们想，还在行她的守源值役。

===Original===
Suddenly everything seemed to have grown tiresome.

===Chinese===
忽然之间，一切都变得乏味起来。

===Original===
“Oh, I do wish we could go home,” said Kathleen. “Couldn’t we just find the door and go out?”

===Chinese===
"唉，真希望咱们能回家，"凯思琳说，"咱们就不能找到那扇门，自己出去吗？"

===Original===
“We might *look* for the door,” said Bernard cautiously, “but I don’t see how we could get up into the cave again.”

===Chinese===
"门嘛，咱们倒可以*找找*，"伯纳德谨慎地说，"可我怎么也看不出，咱们怎么再回到上面那个山洞里去。"

===Original===
“We can swim all right, you know,” Mavis reminded them.

===Chinese===
"咱们游泳可是满在行的，别忘了。"梅维斯提醒大家。

===Original===
“I think it would be pretty low down to go without saying goodbye to the Princesses,” said Francis. “Still, there’s no harm in *looking* for the door.”

===Chinese===
"不跟公主们道个别就走，我看未免太不地道，"弗朗西斯说，"不过，*找找*门总没什么害处。"

===Original===
They did look for the door. And they did not find it. What they did find was a wall﻿—a great gray wall built of solid stones﻿—above it nothing could be seen but blue sky.

===Chinese===
他们也真去找了。却没有找到。找到的是一堵墙——一堵大石头垒成的灰色高墙——墙头上望出去，只看得见青天。

===Original===
“I do wonder what’s on the other side,” said Bernard; and someone, I will not say which, said: “Let’s climb up and see.”

===Chinese===
"我真想知道墙那头是什么光景，"伯纳德说；于是有一个人——我不说是谁——开了口："咱们爬上去瞧瞧。"

===Original===
It was easy to climb up, for the big stones had rough edges and so did not fit very closely, and there was room for a toe here and a hand there. In a minute or two they were all up, but they could not see down on the other side because the wall was about eight feet thick. They walked toward the other edge, and still they could not see down; quite close to the edge, and still no seeing.

===Chinese===
爬上去倒不难，大石块的边边棱棱都很糙，垒得便不严丝合缝，这儿容得下一个脚尖，那儿容得下一只手。一两分钟工夫，他们就全上了墙顶，可墙那边底下望不见——这墙足有八英尺厚。他们朝外侧的墙沿走，还是望不见；走到离墙沿很近很近了，仍旧望不见。

===Original===
“It isn’t sky at all,” said Bernard suddenly. “It’s a sort of dome﻿—tin I shouldn’t wonder, painted to look like sky.”

===Chinese===
"这根本不是天，"伯纳德忽然说，"是个穹顶——要我说，八成是白铁皮，漆成天的模样。"

===Original===
“It can’t be,” said someone.

===Chinese===
"不可能是的。"有人说。

===Original===
“It is though,” said Bernard.

===Chinese===
"偏偏就是。"伯纳德说。

===Original===
“There couldn’t be one so big,” said someone else.

===Chinese===
"哪能有这么大的穹顶。"又有人说。

===Original===
“But there *is*,” said Bernard.

===Chinese===
"可它*明明就有*。"伯纳德说。

===Original===
And then someone﻿—I will not tell you who﻿—put out a hand, and, quite forgetting the Princess’s warning, touched the sky. That hand felt something as faint and thin as a bubble﻿—and instantly this something broke, and the sea came pouring into the Mer-people’s country.

===Chinese===
接着有一个人——我不告诉你们是谁——伸出一只手，把公主的告诫忘了个一干二净，碰了碰天。那只手触到一样轻虚薄脆、像气泡似的东西——那东西霎时破了，海水便朝人鱼族的国度里滚滚灌来。

===Original===
“Now you’ve done it,” said one of those whose hand it wasn’t. And there was no doubt about it; the person who owned the hand *had* done it﻿—and done it very thoroughly. It was plain enough now that what they had been living in was not water, and that this was. The first rush of it was terrible﻿—but in less than a moment the whole kingdom was flooded, and then the water became clear and quiet.

===Chinese===
"这下你闯了祸啦。"说话的是个没伸过手的人。而这也毫无疑问；那只手的主人*确实*闯了祸——而且闯得干净彻底。眼下再明白不过：他们方才一直待着的，并不是水；而现在的，才是水。头一股水的来势可怕极了——可不到一忽儿，整个王国已是一片汪洋，随后水又变得清亮安静了。

===Original===
The children found no difficulty in breathing, and it was as easy to walk as it is on land in a high wind. They could not run, but they walked as fast as they could to the place where they had left the Princess pouring out the water for all the rivers in all the world.

===Chinese===
孩子们呼吸毫无障碍，走路就像在陆地上顶着大风走一样容易。他们跑不起来，便放开脚步尽力快走，赶回他们留下公主的地方——她正在那儿为普天下的河流倾泻着瓮中的水。

===Original===
And as they went, one of them said, “Oh don’t, don’t tell it was me. You don’t know what punishments they may have here.”

===Chinese===
走着走着，他们中有一个说："哦，别说，千万别说是我干的。你们不知道他们这儿会有些什么刑罚。"

===Original===
The others said of course they wouldn’t tell. But the one who had touched the sky felt that it was despised and disgraced.

===Chinese===
其他人当然说绝不会讲。可那个碰了天的人，只觉得自己遭人鄙夷，丢尽了脸。

===Original===
They found the pedestal, but what had been the pool was only part of the enormous sea, and so was the little marble channel.

===Chinese===
他们找到了那座基座，可原先的池子，如今只是茫茫大海的一角，那条小小的大理石水槽也一样。

===Original===
The Princess was not there, and they began to look for her, more and more anxious and wretched.

===Chinese===
公主不在那儿。他们开始找她，心里越来越焦急，越来越难过。

===Original===
“It’s all your fault,” said Francis to the guilty one who had broken the sky by touching it; and Bernard said, “You shut up, can’t you?”

===Chinese===
"这全怪你。"弗朗西斯对那个闯祸的——伸手把天碰破的那个——说；伯纳德却说："你少说两句行不行？"

===Original===
It was a long time before they found their Princess, and when they did find her they hardly knew her. She came swimming toward them, and she was wearing her tail, and a cuirass and helmet of the most beautiful mother-of-pearl﻿—thin scales of it overlapping; and the crest on her helmet was one great pearl, as big as a billiard ball. She carried something over her arm.

===Chinese===
他们过了好一阵才找到他们的公主，找到时几乎认不出她了。她朝他们游来，尾巴已经穿上身，还披着一副珍珠母的胸甲和头盔——薄薄的珠母鳞片层层相覆；盔顶的缨饰是一颗大珍珠，有台球那么大。她臂弯里搭着一样东西。

===Original===
“Here you are,” she said. “I’ve been looking for you. The future is full of danger. The water has got in.”

===Chinese===
"你们在这儿呢，"她说，"我正找你们。往后处处是危险。水已经进来了。"

===Original===
“Yes, we noticed that,” said Bernard.

===Chinese===
"是啊，这我们注意到了。"伯纳德说。

===Original===
And Mavis said: “Please, it was us. We touched the sky.”

===Chinese===
梅维斯说："求您，是我们干的。我们碰了天。"

===Original===
“Will they punish us?” asked Cathay.

===Chinese===
"他们会罚我们吗？"凯西问。

===Original===
“There are no punishments here,” said the pearly Princess gravely, “only the consequences of your action. Our great defense against the Under Folk is that thin blue dome which you have broken. It can only be broken from the inside. Our enemies were powerless to destroy it. But now they may attack us at any moment. I am going to command my troops. Will you come too?”

===Chinese===
"这里没有刑罚，"珠光莹莹的公主正色道，"只有你们的行为带来的后果。我们抵御深渊族的最大屏障，就是你们捅破的那层薄薄的蓝色穹顶。它只有从里头才破得了。我们的敌人怎么也毁不了它。可如今，他们随时都可能打进来。我要去统率我的队伍了。你们也去吗？"

===Original===
“Rather,” said Reuben, and the others, somewhat less cordially, agreed. They cheered up a little when the Princess went on.

===Chinese===
"那还用说，"鲁本说；其余几个也应了，只是没那么起劲。听公主接着一说，他们才稍稍振作。

===Original===
“It’s the only way to make you safe. There are four posts vacant on my staff, and I have brought you the uniforms that go with the appointments.” She unfolded five tails, and four little pearly coats like her own, with round pearls for buttons, pearls as big as marbles. “Put these on quickly,” she said, “they are enchanted coats, given by Neptune himself to an ancestor of ours. By pressing the third button from the top you can render yourself invisible. The third button below that will make you visible again when you wish it, and the last button of all will enable you to become intangible as well as invisible.”

===Chinese===
"唯有这样才能保你们平安。我的参谋班子正空着四个缺，随缺的制服我也给你们带来了。"她抖开五条鱼尾，又抖开四件跟她身上一样的小珠甲，圆珍珠做纽扣，颗颗有弹珠大。"快穿上，"她说，"这是有魔法的甲衣，是尼普顿（Neptune）他老人家亲自赐给我们一位祖先的。按住从上往下数第三颗纽扣，你就能隐身；那颗再往下第三颗，你想现身时一按便现身；最末一颗纽扣，则能叫你隐身之外，还摸不着、碰不上。"

===Original===
“Intangible?” said Cathay.

===Chinese===
"摸不着？"凯西问。

===Original===
“Unfeelable, so you’re quite safe.”

===Chinese===
"就是摸不到、打不着，这样你们就万无一失啦。"

===Original===
“But there are only four coats,” said Francis. “That is so,” said the Princess. “One of you will have to take its chance with the Boy Scouts. Which is it to be?”

===Chinese===
"可甲衣只有四件呀。"弗朗西斯说。"正是这么回事。"公主说，"你们当中得有一个，去童子军那儿碰碰运气。是哪一位呢？"

===Original===
Each of the children always said, and thought that it meant to say “I will,” but somehow or other the person who spoke first was Reuben. The instant the Princess had said “be,” Reuben shouted: “Me,” adding however almost at once, “please.”

===Chinese===
孩子们个个事后都一口咬定，说当时心里要讲的本是"我去"；可不知怎么，先出声的偏偏是鲁本。公主那句"是哪一位呢"的"呢"字刚落地，鲁本就喊："我！"不过紧跟着几乎立刻又添了一句："劳驾。"

===Original===
“Right,” said the Princess kindly, “off with you! The Sea Urchins’ barracks are behind that rock. Off with you! Here, don’t forget your tail. It enables you to be as comfortable in the water as any fish.”

===Chinese===
"好，"公主和气地说，"去吧！海胆们的营房就在那块石头后头。去吧！哎，别落下你的尾巴。有它在身，你在水里就跟随便哪条鱼一样自在。"

===Original===
Reuben took the tail and hastened away.

===Chinese===
鲁本接过尾巴，一溜烟跑了。

===Original===
“Now,” said the Princess. And they all began putting on their tails. It was like putting both your feet into a very large stocking. Then came the mail coats.

===Chinese===
"来吧。"公主说。大家便动手穿各自的尾巴。那滋味，就像把两只脚一齐塞进一只特大号的绒线长袜。接着穿鳞甲小衣。

===Original===
“Don’t we have swords?” Francis asked, looking down at his slim and silvery extremity.

===Chinese===
"咱们没有剑吗？"弗朗西斯问，一面低头打量自己那截苗条银亮的尾巴。

===Original===
“Swords? In the Crustacean Brigade? Never forget, children, that you belong to the Princess’s Own Oysters. Here are your weapons.” She pointed to a heap of large oyster shells, as big as Roman shields.

===Chinese===
"剑？在甲壳旅里？孩子们，永远别忘了，你们是公主直属牡蛎队的人。你们的兵器在这儿。"她指着一堆大牡蛎壳，个个有罗马盾牌那么大。

===Original===
“See,” she said, “you hold them this way as a rule. A very powerful spring is released when you hold them *that* way.”

===Chinese===
"瞧，"她说，"通常这么个拿法。等你*那么*一拿，一股极强劲的弹簧便绷开了。"

===Original===
“But what do you do with it?” Mavis asked.

===Chinese===
"可拿它干什么用呢？"梅维斯问。

===Original===
“Nip the feet of the enemy,” said the Princess, “and it holds on. Under Folk have no tails. You wait till they are near a rock; then nip a foeman’s foot with your good weapon, laying the other end on the rock. The oyster shell will at once attach itself to the rock and.﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
"夹敌人的脚，"公主说，"一夹上就死死咬住不放。深渊族没有尾巴。你们等他们挨近岩石，就拿你们的好兵器夹住一个敌人的脚，把另一头搭在岩石上。牡蛎壳立刻就会牢牢附在岩石上，然后……"

===Original===
A terrible shout rang out, and the Princess stopped.

===Chinese===
一声可怕的呐喊炸响，公主顿住了。

===Original===
“What is it; oh, what is it?” said the children. And the Princess shuddered.

===Chinese===
"是什么？哦，是什么呀？"孩子们说。公主打了个寒噤。

===Original===
Again that shout﻿—the most terrible sound the children had ever heard.

===Chinese===
那喊声又是一声——孩子们平生从未听过的、最可怕的声音。

===Original===
“What is it?” they said again.

===Chinese===
"到底是什么呀？"他们又问。

===Original===
The Princess drew herself up, as if ashamed of her momentary weakness, and said:

===Chinese===
公主挺直了身子，仿佛为方才一瞬间的软弱感到羞惭，说道：

===Original===
“It is the war cry of the Under Folk.”

===Chinese===
"这是深渊族的战吼。"

## VIII. The Water War / 八、水之战

===Original===
After the sound of that terrible shouting there came silence﻿—that is, there was silence where the children were, but all above they could hear the rush and rustle of a quick arming.

===Chinese===
那可怕的呐喊声过后，随之而来的是寂静——就是说，孩子们所在的地方一片寂静，可是在他们头顶上，却听得见一阵匆促备战的奔忙骚响。

===Original===
“The war cry of the People of the Depths,” said the Princess.

===Chinese===
"这是深渊族的战吼。"公主说。

===Original===
“I suppose,” said Kathleen forlornly, “that if they’re so near as that all is lost.”

===Chinese===
"我看，"凯思琳绝望地说，"他们既然已经近到这个地步，那就全完了。"

===Original===
“Lost? No, indeed,” cried the Princess. “The People of the Depths are very strong, but they are very heavy. They cannot rise up and come to us from the water above. Before they can get in they must scale the wall.”

===Chinese===
"完了？不，才不呢。"公主喊道。"深渊族力气很大，可他们也非常沉重。他们没法从上方的水里腾起身子扑到我们这儿来。他们要想打进来，就得先攀上城墙。"

===Original===
“But they will get over the wall﻿—won’t they?”

===Chinese===
"可他们总会越过城墙的——不是吗？"

===Original===
“Not while one of the Royal Halibuts still lives. The Halibuts have manned the wall; they will keep back the foe. But they won’t attack yet. They’ll send out their scouts and skirmishers. Till they approach, the Crustacean Brigade can do nothing. It is a hard thing to watch a fight in which you may not share. I must apologize for appointing you to such an unsatisfactory position.”

===Chinese===
"只要王家大比目鱼还有一条活着，他们就过不来。大比目鱼们已经上了城墙，他们会挡住敌人。不过他们暂时还不会出击。他们会先放出侦察兵和散兵。在他们逼近之前，甲壳旅无事可做。眼睁睁看着一场自己不能插手的仗，是件难受的事。我把你们安置在这么一个不尽如人意的位置上，实在要道一声抱歉。"

===Original===
“Thank you, *we* don’t mind,” said Cathay hastily. “What’s that?”

===Chinese===
"谢谢您，*我们*不在乎。"凯西连忙说。"那是什么？"

===Original===
It was a solid, gleaming sheet of silver that rose above them like a great carpet﻿—which split and tore itself into silver threads.

===Chinese===
那是一整片闪闪发亮的银色，像一块巨大的地毯在他们头顶升起来——随即它自己裂开、撕碎，扯成了一根根银线。

===Original===
“It is the Swordfish Brigade,” said the Princess. “We could swim up a little and watch them, if you’re not afraid. You see, the first attack will probably be delivered by one of their Shark regiments. The 7th Sharks have a horrible reputation. But our brave Swordfish are a match for them,” she added proudly.

===Chinese===
"是剑鱼旅。"公主说。"要是你们不害怕，我们可以往上游一点儿，看看他们。你们瞧，他们的第一击多半会由一支鲨鱼团来发动。第七鲨鱼团的名声可怕得很。不过我们勇敢的剑鱼足以同他们匹敌。"她自豪地补了一句。

===Original===
The Swordfish, who were slowly swimming to and fro above, seemed to stiffen as though to meet some danger at present unseen by the others. Then, with a swift, silent, terrible movement, the Sharks rushed on the noble defenders of Merland.

===Chinese===
剑鱼们在头顶缓缓地来回巡游，忽然浑身一绷，仿佛要迎击一个旁人还看不见的危险。紧接着，鲨鱼们以一种迅疾、无声而可怖的动静，向人鱼国高贵的保卫者们猛冲过去。

===Original===
The Swordfish with their deadly weapons were ready﻿—and next moment all the water was a wild whirl of confused conflict. The Sharks fought with a sort of harsh, rough courage, and the children, who had drawn away to a little distance, could not help admiring their desperate onslaught. But the Swordfish were more than their match. With more skill, and an equally desperate gallantry, they met and repulsed the savage onslaught of the Sharks.

===Chinese===
剑鱼们带着致命的武器严阵以待——下一瞬间，整片海水便成了一团翻腾混战的漩流。鲨鱼们打起来有一股凶悍粗野的狠劲，退开一小段距离观战的孩子们，也不由得佩服他们那股拼死的猛劲。可是剑鱼比他们更胜一筹：剑鱼们以更精熟的技艺、同样不要命的英勇气概，迎住鲨鱼凶猛的冲击，把他们击退了。

===Original===
Shoals of large, calm Cod swept up from the depths, and began to shoulder the dead Sharks sideways toward the water above the walls﻿—the dead Sharks and, alas! many a brave, dead Swordfish, too. For the victory had not been a cheap one.

===Chinese===
一群群个头很大、神色沉静的鳕鱼从深处涌了上来，开始侧着身子把死鲨鱼往城墙上方的水里推——推走的不光是死鲨鱼，唉，还有许多条英勇战死的剑鱼。因为这场胜利的代价并不小。

===Original===
The children could not help cheering as the victorious Swordfish reformed.

===Chinese===
得胜的剑鱼重新列队时，孩子们忍不住欢呼起来。

===Original===
“Pursuit is unnecessary,” said the Princess. “The Sharks have lost too heavily to resume the attack.”

===Chinese===
"不必追击了。"公主说，"鲨鱼损失惨重，无力再攻了。"

===Original===
A Shark in terror-stricken retreat passed close by her, and she clipped its tail with her oyster shell.

===Chinese===
一条魂飞魄散、仓皇败退的鲨鱼恰好从她身旁擦过，她挥起牡蛎壳，一下剪掉了它的尾巴。

===Original===
The Shark turned savagely, but the Princess with one tail-swish was out of danger, pushing the children before her outspread arms, and the Shark began to sink, still making vain efforts to pursue them.

===Chinese===
鲨鱼凶狠地回身反扑，可公主尾巴一甩就脱出了险境，张开双臂把孩子们推在身前。鲨鱼开始往下沉，还在徒劳地想追上他们。

===Original===
“The shell will drag him down,” said the Princess; “and now I must go and get a fresh shield. I wish I knew where the next attack would be delivered.”

===Chinese===
"这贝壳会拖着他沉下去。"公主说，"现在我得去弄一面新盾牌了。真希望我知道他们下一击会打向哪里。"

===Original===
They sank slowly through the water.

===Chinese===
他们缓缓沉入水中。

===Original===
“I wonder where Reuben is?” said Bernard.

===Chinese===
"不知鲁本在哪儿？"伯纳德说。

===Original===
“Oh, he’s quite safe,” said the Princess. “The Boy Scouts don’t go outside the walls﻿—they just do a good turn for anybody who wants it, you know﻿—and help the kind Soles to look after the wounded.”

===Chinese===
"哦，他保险得很。"公主说。"童子军是不出城墙的——他们嘛，专门给需要的人搭把手，这你知道——还帮着好心的鳎鱼照料伤员。"

===Original===
They had reached the great flooded garden again and turned toward the Palace, and as they went a Sea Urchin shell suddenly rose from behind one of the clipped hedges﻿—a Sea Urchin shell and behind it a long tail.

===Chinese===
他们又回到了那座水淹的大花园，转身朝王宫走去。正走着，一个海胆壳突然从一道修剪得整整齐齐的树篱后面升起来——一个海胆壳，壳后还拖着一条长尾巴。

===Original===
The shell was raised, and the face under it was Reuben’s.

===Chinese===
壳抬了起来，壳底下那张脸正是鲁本的。

===Original===
“Hi, Princess!” he shouted. “I’ve been looking for you everywhere. We’ve been scouting. I got a lot of seaweed, and they thought I was nothing *but* seaweed; and so I got quite close to the enemy.”

===Chinese===
"喂，公主！"他喊道。"我到处找你们。我们一直在侦察。我披了好多海草，他们竟当我*就*是一堆海草，我就这么着凑到敌人跟前去了。"

===Original===
“It was very rash,” said the Princess severely.

===Chinese===
"这也太莽撞了。"公主严厉地说。

===Original===
“The others don’t think so,” he said, a little hurt. “They began by saying I was only an irregular Sea Urchin, because I’ve got this jolly tail”﻿—he gave it a merry wag﻿—“and they called me Spatangus, and names like that. But they’ve made me their General now﻿—General Echinus. I’m a regular now, and no mistake, and what I was going to say is the enemy is going to attack the North Tower in force in half an hour.”

===Chinese===
"别人可不这么想。"他有点委屈地说。"起初他们说我不算个正经海胆，因为我长了这条好玩儿的尾巴"——他美滋滋地摇了摇尾巴——"管我叫什么斯帕坦古斯，还有一串这样的名儿。可如今他们已经推我当将军了——艾基努斯将军。我这会儿是正规军了，错不了。我要说的是：半个钟头以后，敌人就要大举进攻北塔。"

===Original===
“You good boy,” said the Princess. I do believe if it hadn’t been for his Sea Urchin’s uniform she would have kissed him. “You’re splendid. You’re a hero. If you could do it safely﻿—there’s heaps of seaweed﻿—could you find out if there’s any danger from the Book People? You know﻿—the ones in the cave. It’s always been our fear that they might attack, *too:* and if they did﻿—well, I’d rather be the slave of a Shark than of Mrs. Fairchild.” She gathered an armful of seaweed from the nearest tree, and Reuben wrapped himself in it and drifted off﻿—looking less like a live Boy Scout than you could believe possible.

===Chinese===
"真是好孩子。"公主说。我敢说，要不是他穿着那身海胆军服，她准会亲他一下的。"你真了不起。你是个英雄。要是你能平平安安地办到——海草有的是——能不能去探一探书里人那头有没有危险？你知道的——就是洞里那伙人。我们一直担心他们*也*会来攻：真要那样——唉，我宁可给鲨鱼当奴隶，也不给费尔柴尔德太太当奴隶。"她从最近的一棵树上抱下一捧海草，鲁本把自己裹在里面漂走了——那模样要多不像个活生生的童子军有多不像，简直叫人不敢相信。

===Original===
The defenders of Merland, now acting on Reuben’s information, began to mass themselves near the North Wall.

===Chinese===
人鱼国的守军如今有了鲁本的情报，开始向北墙附近集结。

===Original===
“Now is our time,” said the Princess. “We must go along the tunnel, and when we hear the sound of their heavy feet shaking the flow of ocean we must make sallies, and fix our shell shields in their feet. Major, rally your men.”

===Chinese===
"现在该我们了。"公主说。"我们必须沿隧道进发，一听见他们沉重的脚步震得海水发颤，就冲出去突袭，把贝壳盾牌扎进他们的脚里。少校，集合你的人马。"

===Original===
A tall Merchild in the Crustacean uniform blew a clear note, and the soldiers of the Crustacean Brigade, who having nothing particular to do had been helping anyone and everyone as best they could, which is the way in Merland, though not in Europe, gathered about their officers.

===Chinese===
一个身穿甲壳旅制服的高个小人鱼吹响一声清亮的号音。甲壳旅的士兵们本来就没什么正经差事，一直在尽力帮这个帮那个——这是人鱼国的做派，欧洲可没有——此刻便朝各自的军官聚拢过来。

===Original===
When they were all drawn up before her, the Princess addressed her troops.

===Chinese===
等队伍全在她面前排列整齐，公主开始对部队训话。

===Original===
“My men,” she said, “we have been suddenly plunged into war. But it has not found us unprepared. I am proud to think that my regiments are ready to the last pearl button. And I know that every man among you will be as proud as I am that our post is, as tradition tells us it has always been, the post of danger. We shall go out into the depths of the sea to fight the enemies of our dear country, and to lay down our lives, if need be, for that country’s sake.”

===Chinese===
"弟兄们，"她说，"我们突然之间被卷入了战争。但战争并没有把我们打个措手不及。一想到我的各个团都已准备停当，连最后一颗珍珠纽扣都系得端端正正，我就觉得骄傲。我知道，你们每一个人都会像我一样自豪，因为一如传统所言、历来如此，我们的岗位素来就是最危险的岗位。我们将开赴大海深处，同我们亲爱的祖国的敌人作战，必要时，为这个祖国献出生命。"

===Original===
The soldiers answered by cheers, and the Princess led the way to one of those little buildings, like Temples of Flora in old pictures, which the children had noticed in the gardens. At the order given a sergeant raised a great stone by a golden ring embedded in it and disclosed a dark passage leading underground.

===Chinese===
士兵们以欢呼声回应。公主在前引路，走向一座小建筑——就是孩子们在花园里注意到的那种，活像古画里的花神庙。一声令下，一名中士抓住嵌在石头里的金环，掀起一块大石板，露出一条通向地下的暗道。

===Original===
A splendid captain of Cockles, six feet high if he was an inch, with a sergeant and six men, led the way. Three Oyster officers followed, then a company of Oysters, the advance guard. At the head of the main body following were the Princess and her Staff. As they went the Princess explained why the tunnel was so long and sloped so steeply.

===Chinese===
一位威风凛凛的鸟蛤上尉——足有六英尺高，一英寸也少不了——带着一名中士和六名士兵在前面开路。三名牡蛎军官跟在后面，再后面是一连牡蛎，充当前卫。公主和她的参谋班子走在随后的主力队伍最前头。一路走着，公主顺带解释了这条隧道为什么这么长、坡又为什么这么陡。

===Original===
“You see,” she said, “the inside of our wall is only about ten feet high, but it goes down on the other side for forty feet or more. It is built on a hill. Now, I don’t want you to feel obliged to come out and fight. You can stay inside and get the shields ready for us to take. We shall keep on rushing back for fresh weapons. Of course the tunnel’s much too narrow for the Under Folk to get in, but they have their regiment of highly trained Sea Serpents, who, of course, can make themselves thin and worm through anything.”

===Chinese===
"你们瞧，"她说，"咱们城墙里面不过十来英尺高，可在另一面，墙往下延伸四十多英尺。墙是筑在一座山上的。我说这话，是不想让你们觉得非出去作战不可。你们可以待在里面，替我们把盾牌预备好，供我们取用。我们要不停地冲回来取新兵器。这条隧道当然窄得很，深渊族钻不进来，可他们有一支训练有素的海蛇团，海蛇嘛，自然能把自己变得细细的，什么窟窿都钻得过。"

===Original===
“Cathay doesn’t like serpents,” said Mavis anxiously.

===Chinese===
"凯西不喜欢蛇。"梅维斯不安地说。

===Original===
“You needn’t be afraid,” said the Princess. “They’re dreadful cowards. They know the passage is guarded by our Lobsters. They won’t come within a mile of the entrance. But the main body of the enemy will have to pass quite close. There’s a great sea mountain, and the only way to our North Tower is in the narrow ravine between that mountain and Merland.”

===Chinese===
"用不着怕。"公主说。"他们是天字第一号的胆小鬼。他们知道这条通道由我们的龙虾把守，洞口一英里之内都不敢挨近。不过敌人的主力就不得不从极近的地方经过了。那儿有一座很大的海山，通到我们北塔的路，只有那座大山和人鱼国之间那条窄窄的山峡。"

===Original===
The tunnel ended in a large rocky hall with the armory, hung with ten thousand gleaming shields, on the one side, and the guardroom crowded with enthusiastic Lobsters on the other. The entrance from the sea was a short, narrow passage, in which stood two Lobsters in their beautiful dark coats of mail.

===Chinese===
隧道尽头是一个宽敞的石殿：一边是兵器库，挂着一万面寒光闪闪的盾牌；另一边是卫兵室，挤满了热情高涨的龙虾。通向大海的入口是一条又短又窄的过道，过道里站着两只龙虾，披着漂亮的深色铠甲。

===Original===
Since the moment when the blue sky that looked first so like sky and then so like painted tin had, touched, confessed itself to be a bubble﻿—confessed, too, in the most practical way, by bursting and letting the water into Merland﻿—the children had been carried along by the breathless rush of preparations for the invasion, and the world they were now in had rapidly increased in reality, while their own world, in which till today they had always lived, had been losing reality at exactly the same rate as that by which the new world gained it. So it was that when the Princess said:

===Chinese===
自从那一刻起——那片蓝天起初那么像天空，随后又那么像漆过的马口铁，被他们一碰，终于招认自己是个泡泡，而且是以最切实的法子招认的：当场迸裂，让海水灌进了人鱼国——孩子们就一直被迎击入侵的匆匆准备裹挟着，忙得透不过气来。他们此刻身处的这个世界，真实性在飞快增长；而他们自己的世界，那个他们直到今天还一直生活着的世界，也正以一模一样的速度失去真实。正因如此，当公主说：

===Original===
“You needn’t go out and attack the enemy unless you like,” they all answered, in some astonishment:

===Chinese===
"你们不必出去攻打敌人，除非你们自己乐意。"他们全都有些惊讶地答道：

===Original===
“But we *want* to.”

===Chinese===
"可我们*偏*要去。"

===Original===
“That’s all right,” said the Princess. “I only wanted to see if they were in working order.”

===Chinese===
"这就对了。"公主说。"我不过是想看看它们灵不灵。"

===Original===
“If what were?”

===Chinese===
"什么灵不灵？"

===Original===
“Your coats. They’re coats of valor, of course.”

===Chinese===
"你们的甲衣呀。它们自然是勇气之衣。"

===Original===
“I think I could be brave without a coat,” said Bernard, and began to undo his pearl buttons.

===Chinese===
"我看不用甲衣，我自己也勇敢得了。"伯纳德说着，动手去解他的珍珠纽扣。

===Original===
“Of course you could,” said the Princess. “In fact, you must be brave to begin with, or the coat couldn’t work. It would be no good to a coward. It just keeps your natural valor warm and your wits cool.”

===Chinese===
"你当然能。"公主说。"说实在的，得先有胆量打底，甲衣才使得上劲。胆小鬼穿它一点儿用也没有。它不过是让你天生的勇气保住热气，让你的头脑保持冷静。"

===Original===
“It makes you braver,” said Kathleen suddenly. “At least I hope it’s me﻿—but I expect it’s the coat. Anyhow, I’m glad it does. Because I do want to be brave. Oh, Princess!”

===Chinese===
"它让人更勇敢。"凯思琳忽然说。"至少我希望那是我的功劳——不过我料想是甲衣的。不管怎样，有这效果我挺高兴。因为我实在想做个勇敢的人。哦，公主！"

===Original===
“Well?” said the Princess, gravely, but not unkindly, “what is it?”

===Chinese===
"怎么啦？"公主问，口气郑重，却不严厉。"什么事？"

===Original===
Kathleen stood a moment, her hands twisting in each other and her eyes downcast. Then in an instant she had unbuttoned and pulled off her coat of pearly mail and thrown it at the Princess’s feet.

===Chinese===
凯思琳站了一会儿，两只手彼此绞着，眼睛低垂。忽然间，她一下子解开那件珠光鳞甲的甲衣，把它扯下来，扔到了公主脚边。

===Original===
“I’ll do it without the coat,” she said, and drew a long breath.

===Chinese===
"不用甲衣，我也办得到。"她说，深深吸了一口气。

===Original===
The others looked on in silence, longing to help her, but knowing that no one could help her now but herself.

===Chinese===
其余三个默默望着，一心想帮她，却知道这时节除了她自己，谁也帮不了她。

===Original===
“It was me,” said Kathleen suddenly, and let go a deep breath of relief. “It was me that touched the sky and let in the water; and I am most frightfully sorry, and I know you’ll never forgive me. But﻿—”

===Chinese===
"是我。"凯思琳猛地说了出来，长长地舒了一口气。"是我碰了天，把水放进来的。我难过得要命，我知道你们永远也不会原谅我了。可是——"

===Original===
“Quick,” said the Princess, picking up the coat, “get into your armor; it’ll prevent your crying.” She hustled Kathleen into the coat and kept her arms around her. “Brave girl,” she whispered. “I’m glad you did it without the coat.” The other three thought it polite to turn away. “Of course,” the Princess added, “I knew﻿—but you didn’t know I knew.”

===Chinese===
"快，"公主说着拾起甲衣，"把铠甲穿上，免得你哭出来。"她三下两下帮凯思琳套上甲衣，又张开手臂搂住她。"勇敢的姑娘。"她悄声说。"你能不靠甲衣就做到，我很高兴。"另外三个识趣地转过头去。"其实呢，"公主补了一句，"我早就知道了——只是你不知道我知道罢了。"

===Original===
“How did you know?” said Kathleen.

===Chinese===
"您是怎么知道的？"凯思琳问。

===Original===
“By your eyes,” said the Princess, with one last hug; “they’re quite different now. Come, let us go to the gate and see if any of our Scouts are signaling.”

===Chinese===
"看你的眼睛。"公主说着，最后搂了她一下。"现在你的眼睛完全两样了。来，我们到城门去，看看咱们的侦察兵有没有在打信号。"

===Original===
