Because of course each invocation would have been answered by an inhabitant of Merland, and thus a small army could easily have been collected, the Jailer overpowered and a rush made for freedom.

===Chinese===
我必须承认，孩子们竟没有整夜翻来覆去地念"美丽的萨布丽娜"，这一点我跟公主一样纳闷。因为每念一遍，当然就会有一位人鱼国的居民应召而来，这样轻而易举就能聚起一支小小队伍，制服狱卒，一冲而出奔向自由。

===Original===
I wish I had time to tell you all that happened to Kathleen, because the daily life of a pampered lap-child to a reigning Queen is one that you would find most interesting to read about. As interesting as your Rover or Binkie would find it to read﻿—if he could read﻿—about the life of one of Queen Alexandra’s Japanese Spaniels. But time is getting on, and I must make a long story short. And anyhow you can never tell all about everything, can you?

===Chinese===
我真想有工夫把凯思琳后来的遭遇原原本本讲给你们听，因为给一位在位女王当娇宠的膝上宠儿，那种日常起居，你们读了一定觉得有趣极了——就跟你们家的罗弗（Rover）或宾基（Binkie）读到——要是它识文断字的话——亚历山德拉王后（Queen Alexandra）那只日本狆的生平一样有趣。可是时间不等人，长话只好短说。再说，天下的事，你本来也没法子件件都讲尽，对不对？

===Original===
The next day the Jailers brought food to the prison, as well as a second draught of oblivion, which, of course, had no effect, and they spent the day wondering how they could escape. In the evening the Jailer’s son brought more food and more oblivion-cup, and he lingered while they ate. He did not look at all unkind, and Francis ventured to speak to him.

===Chinese===
第二天，狱卒们给监狱送来了饭食，还有第二服遗忘之饮——当然毫无效用——这一整天，他们都在琢磨怎样才能逃出去。傍晚，狱卒的儿子又送来饭食和遗忘杯，他们吃的时候，他在一旁流连不去。他瞧着一点也不凶，弗朗西斯便壮起胆子同他搭话。

===Original===
“I say,” he said.

===Chinese===
"喂，我说。"他开了口。

===Original===
“What do you say?” the Under-lad asked.

===Chinese===
"你说什么呀？"那深渊族少年问。

===Original===
“Are you forbidden to talk to us?”

===Chinese===
"他们不许你跟我们说话吗？"

===Original===
“No.”

===Chinese===
"没有的事。"

===Original===
“Then do tell us what they will do with us.”

===Chinese===
"那就快给我们说说，他们要拿我们怎么办。"

===Original===
“I do not know. But we shall have to know before long. The prisons are filling up quickly﻿—they will soon be quite full. Then we shall have to let some of you out on what is called ticket-of-leave﻿—that means with your artificial tails on, which prevent you getting away, even if the oblivion-cup doesn’t take effect.”

===Chinese===
"我不知道。不过要不了多久，我们总得知道。监狱正飞快地住满——很快就要全满了。到那时，我们就只好放你们一些人出去，行话叫'假释'——就是说，给你们戴上人造的假尾巴，叫你们逃不走，哪怕遗忘杯不灵也一样。"

===Original===
“I say,” it was Bernard’s turn to ask.

===Chinese===
"喂，我说。"这回轮到伯纳德问了。

===Original===
“What do you say?”

===Chinese===
"你说什么？"

===Original===
“Why don’t the King and Queen go and fight, like the Mer Royal Family do?”

===Chinese===
"国王和王后怎么不亲自上阵打仗，像人鱼王室那样？"

===Original===
“Against the law,” said the Under-lad. “We took a King prisoner once, and our people were afraid our King and Queen might be taken, so they made that rule.”

===Chinese===
"法律不容许。"深渊族少年说，"我们从前俘虏过一位国王，我们的百姓生怕自己的国王和王后也被人掳去，就立下了那条规矩。"

===Original===
“What did you do with him﻿—the prisoner King?” the Princess asked.

===Chinese===
"你们把他怎么处置了——那位被俘的国王？"公主问。

===Original===
“Put him in an Iswater,” said the lad, “a piece of water entirely surrounded by land.”

===Chinese===
"把他关进了一座'水岛'（Iswater），"少年说，"就是一片四面八方全叫陆地围住的水。"

===Original===
“I should like to see him,” said the Princess.

===Chinese===
"我倒想去见见他。"公主说。

===Original===
“Nothing easier,” said the Under-lad, “as soon as you get your tickets-of-leaves. It’s a good long passage to the lake﻿—nearly all water, of course, but lots of our young people go there three times a week. Of course, he can’t be a King anymore now﻿—but they made him Professor of Conchology.”

===Chinese===
"再容易不过了。"深渊族少年说，"等你们一领到假释票就行。到那片湖去，路途可着实不近——当然，一路几乎全是水——可我们好多年轻人一星期还要去上三趟呢。他如今当然当不成国王了——不过他们让他当了贝类学教授。"

===Original===
“And has he forgotten he was a *King*?” asked the Princess.

===Chinese===
"那他把自己当过*国王*的事也忘了么？"公主问。

===Original===
“Of course: but he was so learned the oblivion-cup wasn’t deep enough to make him forget everything: that’s why he’s a Professor.”

===Chinese===
"当然忘了。可他学问太大，遗忘杯不够深，淹不没他一肚子的学问：所以才当上了教授。"

===Original===
“What was he King of?” the Princess asked anxiously.

===Chinese===
"他从前是哪一国的国王？"公主焦急地问。

===Original===
“He was King of the Barbarians,” said the Jailer’s son﻿—and the Princess sighed.

===Chinese===
"他是野蛮人的国王。"狱卒的儿子说。公主叹了口气。

===Original===
“I thought it might have been my father,” she said, “he was lost at sea, you know.”

===Chinese===
"我还当有可能是我的父王呢。"她说，"要知道，他是在海上失踪的。"

===Original===
The Under-lad nodded sympathetically and went away.

===Chinese===
深渊族少年同情地点了点头，走开了。

===Original===
“He doesn’t seem such a bad sort,” said Mavis.

===Chinese===
"他瞧着不像个坏家伙。"梅维斯说。

===Original===
“No,” said the Princess, “I can’t understand it. I thought all the Under Folk were terrible fierce creatures, cruel and implacable.”

===Chinese===
"是啊。"公主说，"这我就不懂了。我原以为深渊族个个都是凶得可怕的生灵，残忍成性，仇怨不解。"

===Original===
“And they don’t seem so very different from us﻿—except to look at,” said Bernard.

===Chinese===
"再说他们瞧着跟我们也差不到哪儿去——除了那副长相。"伯纳德说。

===Original===
“I wonder,” said Mavis, “what the war began about?”

===Chinese===
"我倒纳闷，"梅维斯说，"这仗当初是为了什么打起来的？"

===Original===
“Oh﻿—we’ve always been enemies,” said the Princess, carelessly.

===Chinese===
"哦——我们从来就是仇敌呗。"公主满不在乎地说。

===Original===
“Yes﻿—but how did you begin being enemies?”

===Chinese===
"是——可你们当初是怎么结上仇的呢？"

===Original===
“Oh, that,” said the Princess, “is lost in the mists of antiquity, before the dawn of history and all that.”

===Chinese===
"哦，那个呀，"公主说，"早就湮没在上古的迷雾里了，在历史破晓以前，诸如此类。"

===Original===
“Oh,” said Mavis.

===Chinese===
"哦。"梅维斯说。

===Original===
But when Ulfin came with the next meal﻿—did I tell you that the Jailer’s son’s name was Ulfin?﻿—Mavis asked him the same question.

===Chinese===
可等乌尔芬（Ulfin）送下一顿饭来的时候——对了，我告诉过你们没有，狱卒的儿子名叫乌尔芬？——梅维斯把同样的问题问了他。

===Original===
“I don’t know﻿—little land-lady,” said Ulfin, “but I will find out﻿—my uncle is the Keeper of the National Archives, graven on tables of stone, so many that no one can count them, but there are smaller tables telling what is on the big ones﻿—” he hesitated. “If I could get leave to show you the Hall of the Archives, would you promise not to try to escape?”

===Chinese===
"我不知道——小陆上娘子。"乌尔芬说，"可我会去查个明白——我伯父是国家档案馆总管，档案全镌刻在石板上，多得谁也数不清，另有小些的石板，注明大石板上刻的是什么——"他迟疑了一下。"要是我能求得准许，带你们去档案馆大厅看看，你们能保证不设法逃跑吗？"

===Original===
They had now been shut up for two days and would have promised anything in reason.

===Chinese===
他们到这时已被关了两天，只要不太出格，什么条件都肯答应。

===Original===
“You see, the prisons are quite full now,” he said, “and I don’t see why you shouldn’t be the first to get your leaves-tickets. I’ll ask my father.”

===Chinese===
"你们瞧，监狱如今已经全满了。"他说，"我看不出你们有什么理由不当头一批领到树叶假释票的。我去问问父亲。"

===Original===
“I say!” said Mavis.

===Chinese===
"喂，我说！"梅维斯说。

===Original===
“What do you say?” said Ulfin.

===Chinese===
"你说什么？"乌尔芬说。

===Original===
“Do you know anything about my sister?”

===Chinese===
"你知道我妹妹的下落吗？"

===Original===
“The Queen’s new lap-child? Oh﻿—she’s a great pet﻿—her gold collar with her name on it came home today. My cousin’s brother-in-law made it.”

===Chinese===
"女王新得的膝上宠儿？哦——她可真是大红人——她那只刻着名字的金项圈，今天送到家了。我表哥的连襟打的。"

===Original===
“The name﻿—Kathleen?” said Mavis.

===Chinese===
"刻的名字——是凯思琳？"梅维斯说。

===Original===
“The name on the collar is Fido,” said Ulfin.

===Chinese===
"项圈上刻的名字是菲多（Fido）。"乌尔芬说。

===Original===
The next day Ulfin brought their tickets-of-leaves, made of the leaves of the tree of Liberty which grows at the bottom of the well where Truth lies.

===Chinese===
第二天，乌尔芬带来了他们的树叶假释票——用自由之树的叶子做的；那棵树，就长在真理安卧的那口井底。

===Original===
“Don’t lose them,” he said, “and come with me.” They found it quite possible to move along slowly on hands and tails, though they looked rather like seals as they did so.

===Chinese===
"别弄丢了。"他说，"跟我来吧。"他们发现，凭双手加尾巴慢慢往前蹭，倒也走得动，只是那模样活像几只海豹。

===Original===
He led them through the strange streets of massive passages, pointing out the buildings, giving them their names as you might do if you were showing the marvels of your own city to a stranger.

===Chinese===
他领他们穿过一条条巨穴甬道构成的奇街异巷，指点着两旁的建筑，一一报上名目，就像你领一位外乡人观赏自己城里的胜景时那样。

===Original===
“That’s the Astrologers’ Tower,” he said, pointing to a huge building high above the others. “The wise men sit there and observe the stars.”

===Chinese===
"那是占星家塔楼。"他指着一座高出群宇的巨厦说，"智者们就坐在里面观星。"

===Original===
“But you can’t see the stars down here.”

===Chinese===
"可你们在这儿底下，看不见星星呀。"

===Original===
“Oh, yes, we can. The tower is fitted up with tubes and mirrors and water transparence apparatus. The wisest men in the country are there﻿—all but the Professor of Conchology. He’s the wisest of all. He invented the nets that caught you﻿—or rather, making nets was one of the things that he had learned and couldn’t forget.”

===Chinese===
"哦，看得见，看得见。塔楼里装配着管子呀、镜子呀，还有清水透光仪。全国顶有智慧的人都在那儿——只除了贝类学教授，而他比谁都更有智慧。捉住你们的那几张网就是他发明的——或者说，结网是他学过的诸般本事里的一门，想忘也忘不掉。"

===Original===
“But who thought of using them for catching prisoners?”

===Chinese===
"可这是谁想出来的主意——拿网来捉俘虏？"

===Original===
“I did,” said Ulfin proudly, “I’m to have a glass medal for it.”

===Chinese===
"我想出来的。"乌尔芬骄傲地说，"为这个，我还要得一枚玻璃勋章呢。"

===Original===
“Do you have glass down here?”

===Chinese===
"你们这儿底下也有玻璃？"

===Original===
“A little comes down, you know. It is very precious. We engrave it. That is the Library﻿—millions of tables of stone﻿—the Hall of Public Joy is next to it﻿—that garden is the mothers’ garden where they go to rest while their children are at school﻿—that’s one of our schools. And here’s the Hall of Public Archives.”

===Chinese===
"总有那么一点儿沉下来的，你们知道的。那可金贵了。我们在上头镌刻花纹。那是图书馆——几百万块石板——挨着它是公众欢乐堂——那座花园是母亲们的花园，孩子们上学的时候，她们好去歇歇腿——那是我们的一所学校。这儿就是公共档案馆。"

===Original===
The Keeper of the Records received them with grave courtesy. The daily services of Ulfin had accustomed the children to the appearance of the Under Folk, and they no longer found their strange, mournful faces terrifying, and the great hall where, on shelves cut out of the sheer rock, were stored the graven tables of Underworld Records, was very wonderful and impressive.

===Chinese===
档案总管以庄重的礼数接待了他们。乌尔芬天天跑腿伺候，孩子们对深渊族的模样早已看惯，不再觉得那些奇异的、含愁带悲的脸可怕了。那座大厅里，一层层石架凿生在整块山岩上，存放着镌有深渊国史册的石板，蔚为奇观，令人肃然。

===Original===
“What is it you want to know?” said the Keeper, rolling away some of the stones he had been showing them. “Ulfin said there was something special.”

===Chinese===
"你们想打听什么事？"总管说着，把他方才指给他们看的几块石板推到一旁。"乌尔芬说有件特别的事。"

===Original===
“Why the war began?” said Francis.

===Chinese===
"这仗为什么打起来的？"弗朗西斯说。

===Original===
“Why the King and Queen are different?” said Mavis.

===Chinese===
"国王和王后为什么跟别人不同？"梅维斯说。

===Original===
“The war,” said the Keeper of the Records, “began exactly three million five hundred and seventy-nine thousand three hundred and eight years ago. An Under-man, getting off his Sea Horse in a hurry trod on the tail of a sleeping Merman. He did not apologize because he was under a vow not to speak for a year and a day. If the Mer-people had only waited he would have explained, but they went to war at once, and, of course, after that you couldn’t expect him to apologize. And the war has gone on, off and on and on and off, ever since.”

===Chinese===
"这场战争，"档案总管说，"开始于整整三百五十七万九千三百零八年前。一个深渊族男人慌里慌张地下海马，一脚踩在了一位酣睡的男人鱼尾巴上。他没有道歉，因为他正许着誓愿，一年零一天不开口。人鱼族要是肯等一等，他本来是会解释明白的，可他们当场就开了战；这以后，当然，你就不好再指望他道歉了。于是这场战争便断断续续、停停打打，一直打到了今天。"

===Original===
“And won’t it ever stop?” asked Bernard.

===Chinese===
"那它就永远也停不了了吗？"伯纳德问。

===Original===
“Not till we apologize, which, of course, we can’t until *they* find out why the war began and that it wasn’t our fault.”

===Chinese===
"非等我们道歉不可，可我们偏偏又道不了这个歉，除非*他们*先查明白这仗是怎么起的，知道那并不是我们的错。"

===Original===
“How awful!” said Mavis; “then it’s all really about nothing.”

===Chinese===
"太可怕了！"梅维斯说，"这么说来，这一切争来斗去，其实什么也不为。"

===Original===
“Quite so,” said the Keeper, “what are your wars about? The other question I shouldn’t answer only I know you’ll forget it when the oblivion-cup begins to work. Ulfin tells me it hasn’t begun yet. Our King and Queen are *imported*. We used to be a Republic, but Presidents were so uppish and so grasping, and all their friends and relations too; so we decided to be a Monarchy, and that all jealousies might be taken away we imported the two handsomest Land Folk we could find. They’ve been a great success, and as they have no relations we find it much less expensive.”

===Chinese===
"正是如此。"总管说，"你们那些战争又是为了什么呢？另一个问题，我本是不该回答的，好在我知道，等遗忘杯一见效，你们就会把它忘掉。乌尔芬告诉我，它还没见效。我们的国王和王后是*进口*来的。我们本来是个共和国，可历届总统一个个眼高于顶、贪得无厌，连他们的亲朋故旧也全是一路货；于是我们决定改行君主制，又为了把一切猜忌连根拔掉，我们进口了所能寻到的最漂亮的一对陆地人。他们干得非常成功，而且没有三亲六眷，我们发觉这样省了好大一笔开销。"

===Original===
When the Keeper had thus kindly gratified the curiosity of the prisoners the Princess said suddenly:

===Chinese===
总管就这样好心地满足了俘虏们的好奇。这时公主突然说道：

===Original===
“Couldn’t we learn Conchology?”

===Chinese===
"我们能不能学贝类学？"

===Original===
And the Keeper said kindly, “Why not? It’s the Professor’s day tomorrow.”

===Chinese===
总管和气地答道："怎么不能？明天正赶上教授的日子呢。"

===Original===
“Couldn’t we go there today?” asked the Princess, “just to arrange about times and terms and all that?”

===Chinese===
"那我们今天能不能先上那儿去一趟？"公主问，"就把时间、学费这一类事情先讲定，好不好？"

===Original===
“If my Uncle says I may take you there,” said Ulfin, “I will, for I have never known any pleasure so great as doing anything that you wish will give me.”

===Chinese===
"只要我伯父准我带你们去，"乌尔芬说，"我就带。因为我生平还从不知道有什么快乐，比得上做一件您盼望的事所能给我的快乐。"

===Original===
The Uncle looked a little anxious, but he said he thought there could be no harm in calling on the Professor. So they went. The way was long for people who were not seals by nature and were not yet compelled to walk after the manner of those charming and intelligent animals. The Mer Princess alone was at her ease. But when they passed a building, as long as from here to the end of the Mile End Road, which Ulfin told them was the Cavalry Barracks, a young Under-man leaned out of a window and said:

===Chinese===
伯父面上微有忧色，但他说，登门拜望教授，想必不会有什么妨害。于是他们就去了。对天生不是海豹、又还不曾不得不照那种又可爱又聪明的动物的步态行走的人来说，这段路实在不短。唯独人鱼公主一人安之若素。可等他们走过一座建筑——长得从这儿一直排到米尔恩德路（Mile End Road）尽头，乌尔芬说那是骑兵营房——的时候，一个年轻的深渊族男人从一扇窗口探出身来，喊道：

===Original===
“What ho! Ulf.”

===Chinese===
"嘿喽！乌尔夫。"

===Original===
“What ho! yourself,” said Ulfin, and approaching the window spoke in whispers. Two minutes later the young Cavalry Officer who had leaned out of the window gave an order, and almost at once some magnificent Sea Horses, richly caparisoned, came out from under an arched gateway. The three children were mounted on these, and the crowd which had collected in the street seemed to find it most amusing to see people in fetter-tails riding on the chargers of the Horse Marines. But their laughter was not ill-natured. And the horses were indeed a boon to the weary tails of the amateur seals.

===Chinese===
"嘿喽你自己个儿。"乌尔芬应道，走近窗口，低低说了一阵。两分钟后，那位探身窗外的年轻骑兵军官下了道命令，几匹神骏的海马几乎立刻从一道拱形大门底下牵了出来，鞍鞯华丽。三个孩子被扶上了马背。街上围拢来瞧热闹的人群，看见几个拖着镣铐尾巴的人骑着骑马水兵队的战马，似乎都觉得妙不可言。不过他们的笑并没有恶意。而对这几只业余海豹疲惫不堪的尾巴来说，这些马实在是天大的恩物。

===Original===
Riding along the bottom of the sea was a wonderful experience﻿—but soon the open country was left behind and they began to go up ways cut in the heart of the rock﻿—ways long and steep, and lighted, as all that great Underworld was, with phosphorescent light.

===Chinese===
骑着马走在海底地面上，是一段奇妙的经历——可不久，开阔的原野便落在身后，他们开始走上凿进岩腹深处的坡道——又长又陡，并且像那整个广大的深渊国里的一切去处一样，靠磷光照亮。

===Original===
When they had been traveling for some hours and the children were beginning to think that you could perhaps have too much even of such an excellent thing as Sea Horse exercise, the phosphorescent lights suddenly stopped, and yet the sea was not dark. There seemed to be a light ahead, and it got stronger and stronger as they advanced, and presently it streamed down on them from shallow water above their heads.

===Chinese===
走了几个钟头之后，孩子们开始觉得，骑海马这样十全十美的好事，原来也会叫人吃不消；就在这时，磷光忽然到了头，海水却并不黑。前方似乎有一片光，他们越往前走，那光便越来越盛，不多时，它已从头顶上浅浅的水里直泻下来，泼在他们身上。

===Original===
“We leave the Sea Horses here,” said Ulfin, “they cannot live in the air. Come.”

===Chinese===
"海马就留在这儿。"乌尔芬说，"它们在空气里活不成。来吧。"

===Original===
They dismounted and swam up. At least Ulfin and the Princess swam and the others held hands and were pulled by the two swimmers. Almost at once their heads struck the surface of the water, and there they were, on the verge of a rocky shore. They landed, and walked﻿—if you can call what seals do walking﻿—across a ridge of land, then plunged into a landlocked lake that lay beyond.

===Chinese===
他们下了马，向上浮去。至少乌尔芬和公主是游上去的，其余几个手拉着手，由两个会水的拖拽着走。几乎转眼间，他们的头就顶破了水面——到了，他们正立在一片礁石岸的边上。他们上了岸，走——要是海豹那一套也配叫作走的话——横过一道土梁，又一头扎进那边一座四面被陆地锁住的湖里。

===Original===
“This is the Iswater,” said Ulfin as they touched bottom, “and yonder is the King.” And indeed a stately figure in long robes was coming toward them.

===Chinese===
"这就是那座水岛。"他们触到湖底时，乌尔芬说，"那边就是国王。"果然，一个身穿长袍的庄严身影，正朝他们走来。

===Original===
“But this,” said the Princess, trembling, “is just like our garden at home, only smaller.”

===Chinese===
"可是这儿，"公主颤声说，"这儿跟我们家乡的花园一模一样，只是小一点儿。"

===Original===
“It was made as it is,” said Ulfin, “by wish of the captive King. Majesty is Majesty, be it never so conquered.”

===Chinese===
"照现在这副模样造起来的，"乌尔芬说，"全凭那位被俘国王的心愿。君威终究是君威，哪怕败得再惨。"

===Original===
The advancing figure was now quite near them. It saluted them with royal courtesy.

===Chinese===
那走来身影这时已经离他们很近了。他以王者的谦和向他们致意。

===Original===
“We wanted to know,” said Mavis, “please, your Majesty, if we might have lessons from you.”

===Chinese===
"我们想请问陛下，"梅维斯说，"我们能不能跟您上课？"

===Original===
The King answered, but the Princess did not hear. She was speaking with Ulfin, apart.

===Chinese===
国王作了回答，公主却没有听见。她正在一旁同乌尔芬说话。

===Original===
“Ulfin,” she said, “this captive King is my Father.”

===Chinese===
"乌尔芬，"她说，"这位被俘的国王，是我的父王。"

===Original===
“Yes, Princess,” said Ulfin.

===Chinese===
"是，公主。"乌尔芬说。

===Original===
“And he does not know me﻿—”

===Chinese===
"可他不认得我了——"

===Original===
“He will,” said Ulfin strongly.

===Chinese===
"他会认得的。"乌尔芬斩钉截铁地说。

===Original===
“Did you know?”

===Chinese===
"你早知道？"

===Original===
“Yes.”

===Chinese===
"是。"

===Original===
“But the people of your land will punish you for bringing us here, if they find out that he is my Father and that you have brought us together. They will kill you. Why did you do it, Ulfin?”

===Chinese===
"可要是你国里的人查出来，他就是我的父王，是你把我们带到一处来的，他们必定要罚你。他们会杀了你的。你为什么还要这么做，乌尔芬？"

===Original===
“Because you wished it, Princess,” he said, “and because I would rather die for you than live without you.”

===Chinese===
"因为您盼望这样，公主。"他说，"也因为与其没有您而活着，我宁愿为您去死。"

## XI. The Peacemaker / 十一、调停者

===Original===
The children thought they had never seen a kinder face or more noble bearing than that of the Professor of Conchology, but the Mer Princess could not bear to look at him. She now felt what Mavis had felt when Cathay failed to recognize her﻿—the misery of being looked at without recognition by the eyes that we know and love. She turned away, and pretended to be looking at the leaves of the seaweed hedge while Mavis and Francis were arranging to take lessons in Conchology three days a week, from two to four.

===Chinese===
孩子们觉得，他们从来没有见过比这位贝类学教授更和善的面容、更高贵的仪态，可是人鱼公主却不敢朝他看。她此刻尝到的，正是当初凯西认不出梅维斯时梅维斯尝过的滋味——被自己认识、自己热爱的那双眼睛望着，却认不出自己，那是怎样的凄楚。她转过身去，装作细看海草树篱的叶子，而梅维斯和弗朗西斯正在同教授安排每星期上三次贝类学课，从两点到四点。

===Original===
“You had better join a class,” said the Professor, “you will learn less that way.”

===Chinese===
"你们还是进个合班好，"教授说，"那样学得的东西少些。"

===Original===
“But we want to learn,” said Mavis.

===Chinese===
"可我们是想学呀。"梅维斯说。

===Original===
And the Professor looked at her very searchingly and said, “Do you?”

===Chinese===
教授目光灼灼地端详了她一番，说："是吗？"

===Original===
“Yes,” she said, “at least﻿—”

===Chinese===
"是，"她说，"至少——"

===Original===
“Yes,” he said, “I quite understand. I am only an exiled Professor, teaching Conchology to youthful aliens, but I retain some remnants of the wisdom of my many years. I know that I am not what I seem, and that you are not what you seem, and that your desire to learn my special subject is not sincere and wholehearted, but is merely, or mainly, the cloak to some other design. Is it not so, my child?”

===Chinese===
"是呀，"他说，"我心里雪亮。我不过是个流亡的教授，给年幼的外邦孩子教贝类学罢了，可我多年积攒下的智慧，还剩下零零星星一点残余。我知道，我不是我表面看来的这个人；你们也不是你们表面看来的这几个人；你们想学我这门专门学问，那心愿并不真诚，也不恳切，只不过、或者说主要是给另一桩图谋打掩护。是不是这样，我的孩子？"

===Original===
No one answered. His question was so plainly addressed to the Princess. And she must have felt the question, for she turned and said, “Yes, O most wise King.”

===Chinese===
谁也没有作答。他这一问，分明是对着公主去的。公主想必也感到了这一问，因为她转过身来说："是的，哦，至智的君王。"

===Original===
“I am no King,” said the Professor, “rather I am a weak child picking up pebbles by the shore of an infinite sea of knowledge.”

===Chinese===
"我不是什么君王，"教授说，"毋宁说，我只是个孱弱的孩子，在无穷无尽的知识大海岸边捡拾卵石罢了。"

===Original===
“You *are*,” the Princess was beginning impulsively, when Ulfin interrupted her.

===Chinese===
"您*就是*——"公主一时冲动，正要说下去，乌尔芬打断了她。

===Original===
“Lady, lady!” he said, “all will be lost! Can you not play your part better than this? If you continue these indiscretions my head will undoubtedly pay the forfeit. Not that I should for a moment grudge that trifling service, but if my head is cut off you will be left without a friend in this strange country, and I shall die with the annoying consciousness that I shall no longer be able to serve you.”

===Chinese===
"小姐，小姐！"他说，"万事都要泡汤啦！您就不能把戏演得像样点儿吗？您要是再这样口无遮拦，我这颗脑袋十有八九要拿去抵罪。倒不是说这点儿微末的效劳我会舍不得，只是脑袋一掉，您在这异国他乡就孤零零没了朋友，而我到死还要揣着一桩恼人的心事：从此再不能伺候您了。"

===Original===
He whispered this into the Princess’s ear while the Professor of Conchology looked on with mild surprise.

===Chinese===
他把这番话悄悄送进公主的耳朵，贝类学教授在一旁瞧着，隐隐有些诧异。

===Original===
“Your attendant,” he observed, “is eloquent but inaudible.”

===Chinese===
"您这位随从，"他评点道，"雄辩滔滔，只是无声无息。"

===Original===
“I mean to be,” said Ulfin, with a sudden change of manner. “Look here, sir, I don’t suppose you care what becomes of you.”

===Chinese===
"我就是要让人听不见。"乌尔芬说，神态陡然一变。"听着，先生，我想您并不在乎自己落个什么下场。"

===Original===
“Not in the least,” said the Professor.

===Chinese===
"一点儿也不在乎。"教授说。

===Original===
“But I suppose you would be sorry if anything uncomfortable happened to your new pupils?”

===Chinese===
"不过，要是您这几位新学童遭了什么不痛快，您就要心疼了吧？"

===Original===
“Yes,” said the Professor, and his eye dwelt on Freia.

===Chinese===
"是的。"教授说，目光在弗蕾亚身上流连。

===Original===
“Then please concentrate your powerful mind on being a Professor. Think of nothing else. More depends on this than you can easily believe.”

===Chinese===
"那就请把您那颗强有力的心智，集中用在当教授这件事上。别的什么也别想。这中间干系之大，您轻易信不过来。"

===Original===
“Believing is easy,” said the Professor. “Tomorrow at two, I think you said?” and with a grave salutation he turned his back on the company and walked away through his garden.

===Chinese===
"相信倒容易。"教授说。"明天两点，我记得你们是这么说的？"说罢他郑重行了一礼，便转身撇下众人，穿过他的花园走了。

===Original===
It was a thoughtful party that rode home on the borrowed chargers of the Deep Sea Cavalry. No one spoke. The minds of all were busy with the strange words of Ulfin, and even the least imaginative of them, which in this case was Bernard, could not but think that Ulfin had in that strange oddly shaped head of his, some plan for helping the prisoners, to one of whom at least he was so obviously attached. He also was silent, and the others could not help encouraging the hope that he was maturing plans.

===Chinese===
骑着向深海骑兵队借来的战马往回走的这一行人，个个满腹心事。谁也不说话。所有人的脑子里都在转着乌尔芬那些奇怪的话，就连他们中间最缺少想象力的一个——这一回是伯纳德——也不由得想：乌尔芬那颗奇形怪状的脑袋里，准是装着什么搭救囚徒的计策，因为他对囚徒当中至少一位，显然一往情深。他自己也闷声不响，大家便不由得越盼越热，只当他正在把计谋酝酿成熟。

===Original===
They reached the many-windowed prison, gave up their tickets-of-leaves and reentered it. It was not till they were in the saloon and the evening was all but over that Bernard spoke of what was in every head.

===Chinese===
他们回到那座开着许多扇窗子的监狱，交回树叶假释票，重新进了里面。直到他们回到船上的大厅、这一晚差不多要过完的时候，伯纳德才把人人脑子里转的那件事说出了口。

===Original===
“Look here,” he said, “I think Ulfin means to help us to escape.”

===Chinese===
"我说，"他开腔道，"我看乌尔芬是想帮咱们逃走。"

===Original===
“Do you,” said Mavis. “I think he means to help us to something, but I don’t somehow think it’s as simple as that.”

===Chinese===
"是吗。"梅维斯说，"我看他是要帮咱们做成一件什么大事，可不知怎么的，我不觉得事情就那么简单。"

===Original===
“Nothing near,” said Francis simply.

===Chinese===
"差得远呢。"弗朗西斯说得直截了当。

===Original===
“But that’s all we want, isn’t it?” said Bernard.

===Chinese===
"可咱们要的不就是这个吗？"伯纳德说。

===Original===
“It’s not all *I* want,” said Mavis, finishing the last of a fine bunch of sea-grapes, “what I want is to get the Mer King restored to his sorrowing relations.”

===Chinese===
"我要的可不止*这个*。"梅维斯说，把一大串上好的海葡萄吃到了最后一颗，"我要的是送人鱼国王回到他哀伤的亲人们身边去。"

===Original===
The Mer Princess pressed her hand affectionately.

===Chinese===
人鱼公主深情地紧握了一下她的手。

===Original===
“So do I,” said Francis, “but I want something more than that even. I want to stop this war. For always. So that there’ll never be any more of it.”

===Chinese===
"我也是，"弗朗西斯说，"可我想要的还不止这个。我要叫这场战争停下来。永远停住。叫它再也不会重演。"

===Original===
“But how can you,” said the Mer Princess, leaning her elbows on the table, “there’s always been war; there always will be.”

===Chinese===
"可你怎么能呢，"人鱼公主两肘支在桌上说，"战争从来就有，往后也永远会有。"

===Original===
“Why?” asked Francis.

===Chinese===
"为什么？"弗朗西斯问。

===Original===
“I don’t know; it’s Merman nature, I suppose.”

===Chinese===
"不知道；我想，这是人鱼的天性吧。"

===Original===
“I don’t believe it,” said Francis earnestly, “not for a minute I don’t. Why, don’t you see, all these people you’re at war with are *nice*. Look how kind the Queen is to Cathay﻿—look how kind Ulfin is to us﻿—and the Librarian, and the Keeper of the Archives, and the soldiers who lent us the horses. They’re all as decent as they can stick, and all the Mer-people are nice too﻿—and then they all go killing each other, and all those brave, jolly soldier fish too, just all about nothing. I call it simply *rot*.”

===Chinese===
"我才不信，"弗朗西斯恳切地说，"半分钟也不信。嗐，你们没瞧见吗，同你们打仗的这些人，个个都*好*。瞧女王待凯西多亲——瞧乌尔芬待咱们多好——还有图书馆长，还有档案总管，还有借海马给咱们的那些兵。他们个个好得没挑儿，人鱼族这边的也都是好人——可他们偏偏要你杀我、我杀你，还有那些又勇敢又快活的士兵鱼，统统为的是压根儿不算事儿的事。我管这干脆就叫*荒唐*。"

===Original===
“But there always has been war I tell you,” said the Mer-Princess. “People would get slack and silly and cowardly if there were no wars.”

===Chinese===
"可我告诉你，战争从来就有过。"人鱼公主说。"要是没了战争，人就要变得懈怠、愚蠢、胆小。"

===Original===
“If I were King,” said Francis, who was now thoroughly roused, “there should never be any more wars. There are plenty of things to be brave about without hurting other brave people﻿—exploring and rescuing and saving your comrades in mines and in fires and floods and things and﻿—” his eloquence suddenly gave way to a breathless shyness﻿—“oh, well,” he ended, “it’s no use gassing; you know what I mean.”

===Chinese===
"我要是当国王，"弗朗西斯说，这时他已经完全激动起来了，"就永远不再打仗。勇敢的事有的是，用不着去伤害别的勇敢的人——探探险呀，救救人呀，矿难里、大火里、洪水里搭救自己的伙伴呀什么的，还有——"他的滔滔雄辩忽然化作一阵让人喘不上来的害臊——"哦，算了，"他收了尾，"瞎吹没用；你们懂我的意思。"

===Original===
“Yes,” said Mavis, “and oh, France﻿—I think you’re right. But what can we *do*?”

===Chinese===
"是啊，"梅维斯说，"哦，弗兰——我觉得你说得对。可咱们能*做*点什么呢？"

===Original===
“I shall ask to see the Queen of the Under Folk, and try to make her see sense. She didn’t look an absolute duffer.”

===Chinese===
"我要去求见深渊族女王，想法子叫她明白事理。她瞧着又不像个十足的笨蛋。"

===Original===
They all gasped at the glorious and simple daring of the idea. But the Mer Princess said:

===Chinese===
这个主意又辉煌又干脆，大胆得叫他们全都倒抽一口气。可是人鱼公主说：

===Original===
“I know you’d do everything you could﻿—but it’s very difficult to talk to kings unless you’ve been accustomed to it. There are books in the cave, *Straight Talks with Monarchs*, and *Kings I Have Spoken My Mind To*, which might help you. But, unfortunately, we can’t get them. You see, Kings start so much further than subjects do: they know such a lot more. Why, even I﻿—”

===Chinese===
"我知道你会尽你所能——可是，除非打小惯于此道，跟君王说话难得很。学问洞里有几本书，《与君王直言》（*Straight Talks with Monarchs*）、《我直言进谏过的国王们》（*Kings I Have Spoken My Mind To*），也许能帮上你的忙。可惜呀，我们弄不到它们。你要知道，国王们的起点比臣子远得多：他们懂的多着呢。嗐，就连我——"

===Original===
“Then why won’t *you* try talking to the Queen?”

===Chinese===
"那*你*怎么不去试着同女王谈谈呢？"

===Original===
“I shouldn’t dare,” said Freia. “I’m only a girl-Princess. Oh, if only my dear Father could talk to her. If he believed it possible that war could cease﻿ ﻿… *he* could persuade anybody of anything. And, of course, they would start on the same footing﻿—both Monarchs, you know.”

===Chinese===
"我可不敢。"弗蕾亚说，"我不过是个小姑娘公主罢了。哦，但愿我亲爱的父王能去同她谈。只要他相信战争有可能终止……*他*啊，什么人都能说服，什么事都能说成。而且他们自然也平起平坐——都是君主，你知道的。"

===Original===
“I see: like belonging to the same club,” said Francis vaguely.

===Chinese===
"我明白了：就像同属一家俱乐部。"弗朗西斯含含糊糊地说。

===Original===
“But, of course, as things are, my royal Father thinks of nothing but shells﻿—if only we could restore his memory.﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
"可是如今呢，我的父王心里除了贝壳再没有别的——但愿咱们能恢复他的记忆。……"
===Original===
“I say,” said Bernard suddenly, “does that Keep-your-Memory charm work backward?”

===Chinese===
"我说，"伯纳德忽然开腔，"那个保住记忆的护符，能不能倒着用？"

===Original===
“Backward?”

===Chinese===
"倒着用？"

===Original===
“I mean﻿—is it any use taking it after you’ve swallowed your dose of oblivion-cup? Is it a rester what’s its name as well as an antidote?”

===Chinese===
"我是说——要是先喝下了那一剂遗忘杯，回头再吃它，还管用吗？它除了是解药，是不是也是个复原……那个什么来着的？"

===Original===
“Surely,” said the Princess, “it is a restorative; only we have no charm to give my Father﻿—they are not made in this country﻿—and alas! we cannot escape and go to our own kingdom and return with one.”

===Chinese===
"自然是管用的，"公主说，"它本是复原的灵药；只是我们没有护符好给我的父王——这国里不造这种护符——唉！我们又逃不出去，回自己的王国去取一枚再来。"

===Original===
“No need,” said Bernard, with growing excitement, “no need. Cathay’s charm is there, in the inner pocket of her magic coat. If we could get that, give the charm to your Father, and then get him an interview with the Queen?”

===Chinese===
"用不着，"伯纳德说，越说越兴奋，"用不着。凯西的护符就在那儿，在她那件魔法甲衣的内袋里。咱们要是能把它弄到手，把护符给你父王，再让他见上女王一面，怎么样？"

===Original===
“But what about Cathay?” said Mavis.

===Chinese===
"那凯西怎么办呢？"梅维斯说。

===Original===
“If my Father’s memory were restored,” said the Princess, “his wisdom would find us a way out of all our difficulties. To find Cathay’s coat: that is what we have to do.”

===Chinese===
"只要我父王的记忆恢复了，"公主说，"他的智慧自会替我们想出办法，走出这一切难关。要找到凯西的甲衣：这就是我们眼下要办的事。"

===Original===
“Yes,” said Francis. “That’s all.” He spoke a little bitterly, for he had really rather looked forward to that straight talk with the King, and the others had not been as enthusiastic as he felt he had a right to expect.

===Chinese===
"是啊，"弗朗西斯说，"就剩这么点儿事了。"他的话音里带着些酸意，因为他原本实实在在盼着那一场对君王的直言相谈，而旁人的热心，远没有达到他自觉有权指望的那般热度。

===Original===
“Let’s call Ulfin,” said the Princess, and they all scratched on the door of polished bird’s-eye maple that separated their apartments from the rest of the prison. The electric bells were out of order, so one scratched instead of ringing. It was quite as easy.

===Chinese===
"去叫乌尔芬来吧。"公主说。于是大家都在那扇门上挠了起来——那是扇抛光的鸟眼枫木门，把他们的房间同监狱其余的地方隔开。电铃坏了，只好用挠代替按铃。倒也一样便当。

===Original===
Ulfin came with all speed.

===Chinese===
乌尔芬一路飞奔而来。

===Original===
“We’re holding a council,” said Freia, “and we want you to help. We know you will.”

===Chinese===
"我们在开议事会，"弗蕾亚说，"要请你帮忙。我们知道你一定肯。"

===Original===
“I know it,” said Ulfin, “tell me your needs﻿—”

===Chinese===
"我知道。"乌尔芬说，"把你们的需要告诉我——"

===Original===
And without more ado they told him all.

===Chinese===
二话不说，他们把一切都告诉了他。

===Original===
“You trust me, Princess, I am proud,” he told her, but when he heard Francis’s dream of universal peace he took the freckled paw of Francis and laid his lips to it. And Francis, even in the midst of his pride and embarrassment at this token, could not help noticing that the lips of Ulfin were hard, like horn.

===Chinese===
"公主，你们信得过我，我引以为荣。"他对她说。可等他听说弗朗西斯那个四海升平的梦想，他便捧起弗朗西斯那只雀斑点点的小爪子，举到了自己的唇边。而弗朗西斯呢，纵然正为这一礼数又得意又发窘，仍不免注意到：乌尔芬的嘴唇硬邦邦的，像角质一样。

===Original===
“I kiss your hand,” said Ulfin, “because you give me back my honor, which I was willing to lay down, with all else, for the Princess to walk on to safety and escape. I would have helped you to find the hidden coat﻿—for her sake alone, and that would have been a sin against my honor and my country﻿—but now that I know it is to lead to peace, which, warriors as we are, the whole nation passionately desires, then I am acting as a true and honorable patriot. My only regret is that I have one gift the less to lay at the feet of the Princess.”

===Chinese===
"我吻您的手，"乌尔芬说，"因为您把我的名誉还给了我——这名誉，我本来甘愿连同其余的一切一并放下，铺在地上让公主踏着走向安全、逃出险境。我原本也会帮你们找到那件藏起来的甲衣——只为她的缘故——而那本是对我的名誉、对我的国家的一桩罪过。可如今我知道这事通向和平——我们虽是尚武之邦，全国上下却如饥似渴地盼着和平——那么我所为的，就是一名真诚而高尚的爱国者的事了。我唯一的遗憾是，能奉献到公主脚边的礼物，从此少了一件。"

===Original===
“Do you know where the coats are?” Mavis asked.

===Chinese===
"你知道甲衣在哪儿吗？"梅维斯问。

===Original===
“They are in the Foreign Curiosities Museum,” said Ulfin, “strongly guarded: but the guards are the Horse Marines﻿—whose officer lent you your chargers today. He is my friend, and when I tell him what is toward, he will help me. I only ask of you one promise in return. That you will not seek to escape, or to return to your own country, except by the free leave and license of our gracious Sovereigns.”

===Chinese===
"它们在外国奇珍博物馆里，"乌尔芬说，"看守森严：不过看守是骑马水兵队——今天借战马给你们的，正是他们的军官。他是我的朋友，等我把事情的来龙去脉告诉他，他会帮我的。我只求你们回报我一个承诺：除非蒙我们仁慈的君主慨然恩准，你们决不设法逃走，也决不设法回自己的国家去。"

===Original===
The children easily promised﻿—and they thought the promise would be easily kept.

===Chinese===
孩子们一口就答应了——而且他们觉得，这诺言守起来也容易。

===Original===
“Then tomorrow,” said Ulfin, “shall begin the splendid Peace Plot which shall hand our names down, haloed with glory, to remotest ages.”

===Chinese===
"那么明天，"乌尔芬说，"就要开始那桩辉煌的和平密谋了——它要把我们的名字戴上荣耀的光环，一直传到最遥远的后世。"

===Original===
He looked kindly on them and went out.

===Chinese===
他慈和地望了望他们，出去了。

===Original===
“He *is* a dear, isn’t he?” said Mavis.

===Chinese===
"他*真*是个可爱人儿，对不对？"梅维斯说。

===Original===
“Yes, indeed,” said the Princess absently.

===Chinese===
"可不是嘛。"公主心不在焉地应道。

===Original===
And now next day the children, carrying their tickets-of-leaves, were led to the great pearl and turquoise building, which was the Museum of Foreign Curiosities. Many were the strange objects preserved there﻿—china and glass and books and land-things of all kinds, taken from sunken ships. And all the things were under dome-shaped cases, apparently of glass. The Curator of the Museum showed them his treasures with pride, and explained them all wrong in the most interesting way.

===Chinese===
到了第二天，孩子们带着树叶假释票，被领到那座珍珠和绿松石砌成的大楼跟前——那就是外国奇珍博物馆。那儿保存的稀奇物件可真多——瓷器呀，玻璃呀，书呀，还有从沉船里得来的形形色色陆地上的东西。所有的物件都罩在半球形的罩子底下，瞧着像是玻璃的。博物馆馆长得意洋洋地领他们看他的珍藏，用最有滋有味的方式，把每一件都讲解得驴唇不对马嘴。

===Original===
“Those discs,” he said, pointing to the china plates, “are used in games of skill. They are thrown from one hand to another, and if one fails to catch them his head is broken.”

===Chinese===
"那些圆盘，"他指着瓷盘子说，"是技巧游戏里用的。把它们从一只手里抛到另一只手里，谁要是接不住，脑袋就开花。"

===Original===
An egg boiler, he explained, was a Land Queen’s jewel case, and four egg-shaped emeralds had been fitted into it to show its use to the vulgar. A silver ice pail was labeled: “Drinking Vessel of the Horses of the Kings of Earth,” and a cigar case half full was called “Charm case containing Evil Charms: probably Ancient Barbarian.” In fact it was very like the museums you see on land.

===Chinese===
他解释说，一只煮蛋器是陆地女王的珠宝匣，里头恰好嵌着四颗蛋形祖母绿，正合那用途，好教凡夫俗子明白它是干什么用的。一只银冰桶标着："尘世诸王马匹的饮具"；一只装了半盒的雪茄盒，被唤作"邪恶魔符收藏匣：大概属于上古野蛮人"。说实在的，这同你们在陆地上见到的博物馆像得很呐。

===Original===
They were just coming to a large case containing something whitish and labeled, “Very valuable indeed,” when a messenger came to tell the Curator that a soldier was waiting with valuable curiosities taken as loot from the enemy.

===Chinese===
他们正走到一个大展柜跟前，里面装着一件微微发白的东西，标签写着"确属非常珍贵"，恰好有个信差来禀报馆长：外面有个士兵等着，带来了从敌人那儿缴获的贵重珍品。

===Original===
“Excuse me one moment,” said the Curator, and left them.

===Chinese===
"失陪片刻。"馆长说着，撇下他们走了。

===Original===
“*I* arranged that,” said Ulfin, “quick, before he returns﻿—take your coats if you know any spell to remove the case.”

===Chinese===
"这是*我*安排下的。"乌尔芬说，"快，趁他没回来——你们要是会什么咒语能挪开罩子，就把甲衣取走。"

===Original===
The Princess laughed and laid her hand on the glassy dome, and lo! it broke and disappeared as a bubble does when you touch it.

===Chinese===
公主笑了，把手往那玻璃似的圆罩上一放——看哪！罩子应手而破，散得无影无踪，就像泡泡一碰就破那样。

===Original===
“Magic,” whispered Ulfin.

===Chinese===
"魔法。"乌尔芬悄声说。

===Original===
“Not magic,” said the Princess. “Your cases are only bubbles.”

===Chinese===
"不是魔法，"公主说，"你们的罩子不过是些泡泡罢了。"

===Original===
“And I never knew,” said Ulfin.

===Chinese===
"我竟一直不知道。"乌尔芬说。

===Original===
“No,” said the Princess, “because you never dared to touch them.”

===Chinese===
"是啊，"公主说，"因为你们从来不敢碰它们。"

===Original===
The children were already busy pulling the coats off the ruby slab where they lay. “Here’s Cathay’s,” whispered Mavis.

===Chinese===
孩子们早已忙着把甲衣从摊放它们的红宝石台板上拽下来。"这件是凯西的。"梅维斯低声说。

===Original===
The Princess snatched it and her own pearly coat which, in one quick movement, she put on and buttoned over Cathay’s little folded coat, holding this against her. “Quick,” she said, “put yours on, all of you. Take your mer-tails on your arms.”

===Chinese===
公主一把抓过它，又抓过她自己那件珠光甲衣，手疾眼快，把珠光甲衣穿在身上，扣上纽扣，罩在凯西那件叠得小小的甲衣外面，把它贴身掩在怀里。"快，"她说，"你们全都把自己那件穿上。鱼尾都搭在胳膊上带走。"

===Original===
They did. The soldiers at the end of the long hall had noticed the movements and came charging up toward them.

===Chinese===
他们照做了。长厅那一头的士兵们已经察觉这边的动静，呐喊着朝他们冲过来。

===Original===
“Quick, quick!” said the Princess, “now﻿—altogether. One, two, three. Press your third buttons.”

===Chinese===
"快，快！"公主说，"现在——一起来。一，二，三。按你们的第三颗纽扣。"

===Original===
The children did, and the soldiers tearing up the hall to arrest the breakers of the cases of the Museum﻿—for by this time they could see what had happened﻿—almost fell over each other in their confusion. For there, where a moment ago had been four children with fin-tail fetters, was now empty space, and beside the rifled Museum case stood only Ulfin.

===Chinese===
孩子们按了。顺着长厅猛冲过来、要捉拿打碎博物馆柜罩的凶手的士兵们——因为到这时他们已经看清出了什么事——乱作一团，险些你绊我我绊你。因为方才还站着四个戴尾鳍镣铐的孩子的地方，如今空空如也，被劫掠一空的博物馆柜子旁边，只剩下乌尔芬一个人。

===Original===
And then an odd thing happened. Out of nowhere, as it seemed, a little pearly coat appeared, hanging alone in air (water, of course, it was really. Or was it?). It seemed to grow and to twine itself round Ulfin.

===Chinese===
接着，一件怪事发生了。不知打哪儿，仿佛凭空出现了一件小小的珠光甲衣，独自悬在空气里（当然喽，其实是水里。可到底是不是呢？）。只见它仿佛渐渐胀开，缠绕上乌尔芬的身子。
===Original===
“Put it on,” said a voice from invisibility, “put it on,” and Ulfin did put it on.

===Chinese===
"穿上它。"一个声音从无形之处传来，"穿上它。"乌尔芬果真把它穿上了。

===Original===
The soldiers were close upon him. “Press the third button,” cried the Princess, and Ulfin did so. But as his right hand sought the button, the foremost soldier caught his left arm with the bitter cry﻿—

===Chinese===
士兵们眼看就要扑到他身上。"按第三颗纽扣。"公主喊道。乌尔芬的右手刚去摸纽扣，冲在最前头的士兵已一把揪住他的左臂，恨声喊道——

===Original===
“Traitor, I arrest you in the King’s name,” and though he could now not see that he was holding anything, he could feel that he was, and he held on.

===Chinese===
"叛徒，我以国王的名义逮捕你。"虽说这会儿他已经看不见自己抓着什么，却觉出手里有东西，便死死攥住不放。

===Original===
“The last button, Ulfin,” cried the voice of the unseen Princess, “press the last button,” and next moment the soldier, breathless with amazement and terror, was looking stupidly at his empty hand. Ulfin, as well as the three children and the Princess, was not only invisible but intangible, the soldiers could not see or feel anything.

===Chinese===
"最后一颗纽扣，乌尔芬。"看不见的公主的声音喊道，"按最后一颗纽扣。"下一刹那，那士兵惊骇交加，气都喘不过来，呆呆地望着自己空空如也的手。乌尔芬同三个孩子和公主一样，不但隐形，而且无形无质，士兵们什么也看不见、什么也摸不着了。

===Original===
And what is more, neither could the Princess or the children or Ulfin.

===Chinese===
不但如此，公主、孩子们和乌尔芬自己，也彼此看不见、摸不着。

===Original===
“Oh, where are you? Where am I?” cried Mavis.

===Chinese===
"哦，你在哪儿？我又在哪儿？"梅维斯喊道。

===Original===
“Silence,” said the Princess, “we must keep together by our voices, but that is dangerous. *A la porte!*” she added. How fortunate it was that none of the soldiers understood French!

===Chinese===
"静一静。"公主说，"咱们只有靠声音聚拢，可这太危险。*A la porte!*"她又添了一句。亏得士兵们谁也不懂法语！

===Original===
As the five were invisible and intangible and as the soldiers were neither, it was easy to avoid them and to get to the arched doorway. The Princess got there first. There was no enemy near﻿—all the soldiers were crowding around the rifled Museum case, talking and wondering, the soldier who had seized Ulfin explaining again and again how he had had the caitiff by the arm, “as solid as solid, and then, all in a minute, there was nothing﻿—nothing at all,” and his comrades trying their best to believe him. The Princess just waited, saying, “Are you there?” every three seconds, as though she had been at the telephone.

===Chinese===
这五位既看不见又摸不着，士兵们却两样都不缺，要避开他们、走到拱门那儿，本来容易得很。公主第一个到了。附近没有一个敌人——士兵们全都拥在被劫掠一空的博物馆柜子四周，议论纷纷，啧啧称奇；揪过乌尔芬的那个士兵一遍又一遍地讲，他分明攥着那家伙的胳膊，"结结实实的，忽然间，一眨眼，就什么都没有了——什么都没有了"，他的弟兄们则竭尽全力想相信他的话。公主只管等着，每三秒钟问一声："你在吗？"就像在打电话一般。

===Original===
“Are you there?” said the Princess for the twenty-seventh time. And then Ulfin said, “I am here, Princess.”

===Chinese===
"你在吗？"公主问到第二十七遍，乌尔芬终于说："我在，公主。"

===Original===
“We must have connecting links,” she said﻿—“bits of seaweed would do. If you hold a piece of seaweed in your hand I will take hold of the other end of it. We cannot feel the touch of each other’s hands, but we shall feel the seaweed, and you will know, by its being drawn tight that I have hold of the other end. Get some pieces for the children, too. Good stout seaweed, such as you made the nets of with which you captured us.”

===Chinese===
"咱们得有互相连着的链环。"她说——"几段海草就行。你手里攥住一段海草，我攥住另一头。咱们彼此的手碰不到一块儿，可海草摸得着，海草一绷紧，你就知道我这头攥住了。也给孩子们备几段。要结实的好海草，就照你们织网逮咱们用的那种。"

===Original===
“Ah, Princess,” he said, “how can I regret that enough? And yet how can I regret it at all since it has brought you to me.”

===Chinese===
"唉，公主啊，"他说，"这事我悔也悔不过来！可话说回来，我又怎么能有半分悔意呢——正是它把您带到了我身边。"

===Original===
“Peace, foolish child,” said the Princess, and Ulfin’s heart leaped for joy because, when a Princess calls a grown-up man “child,” it means that she likes him more than a little, or else, of course, she would not take such a liberty. “But the seaweed,” she added, “there is no time to lose.”

===Chinese===
"安静点儿，傻孩子。"公主说。乌尔芬的心欢喜得直跳，因为一位公主管一个长大成人的男人叫"孩子"，意思就是她喜欢他，喜欢得不止一点点——不然的话，她当然不会这样放肆。"不过海草，"她补了一句，"一刻也耽搁不得。"

===Original===
“I have some in my pocket,” said Ulfin, blushing, only she could not see that. “They keep me busy making nets in my spare time﻿—I always have some string in my pocket.”

===Chinese===
"我口袋里就有。"乌尔芬说，脸都红了，只是她看不见。"他们叫我一有空就织网——我口袋里向来揣着些草绳。"

===Original===
A piece of stringy seaweed suddenly became visible as Ulfin took it out of his invisible pocket, which, of course, had the property of making its contents invisible too, so long as they remained in it. It floated toward the Princess, who caught the end nearest to her and held it fast.

===Chinese===
乌尔芬从那只隐了身的口袋里往外一掏，一段草绳般的海草顿时显了形——那口袋自然有这般特性：东西只要装在里面，便也跟着隐身。海草朝公主飘去，公主一把捞住离她近的那一头，牢牢攥紧。

===Original===
“Where are you?” said a small voice.

===Chinese===
"你在哪儿呀？"一个小小的声音问。

===Original===
It was Mavis﻿—and almost at once Francis and Bernard were there too. The seaweed chain was explained to them, and they each held fast to their ends of the seaweed links. So that when the soldiers, a little late in the day, owing to the careful management of Ulfin’s friend, reached the front door, there was nothing to be seen but four bits of seaweed floating down the street, which, of course, was the sort of thing that nobody could possibly notice unless they *knew*.

===Chinese===
是梅维斯——紧接着，弗朗西斯和伯纳德也到了。海草连链的道理讲给他们听了，他们各自攥紧了自己那几段海草的草头。于是，当士兵们——托乌尔芬那位朋友周密打点的福，已经迟了一步——赶到前门时，眼里只余下四小段海草顺着大街飘然而去，这种情形呢，当然了，除非人家心里*有数*，是谁也决计留意不到的。

===Original===
The bits of seaweed went drifting to the Barracks, and no one noticed that they floated on to the stables and that invisible hands loosed the halters of five Sea Horses. The soldier who ought to have been looking after the horses was deeply engaged in a game of Animal Grab with a comrade. The cards were of narwhal ivory, very fine, indeed, and jeweled on every pip. The invisible hands saddled the Sea Horses and invisible forms sprang to the saddles, and urged the horses forward.

===Chinese===
那几段海草一路飘进了营房，谁也没有留意它们又飘进了马厩，更没人瞧见几双看不见的手解开了五匹海马的缰绳。本该看马的士兵，正同一名伙伴埋头玩"动物抢牌"（Animal Grab）玩得起劲。牌是独角鲸牙做的，讲究极了，牌面上每个点子都嵌着宝石。看不见的手给海马备好鞍，几副看不见的身子一跃上鞍，催马便行。

===Original===
The unfortunate Animal Grabber was roused from his game by the sight of five retreating steeds﻿—saddled and bridled indeed, but, as far as he could see, riderless, and long before other horses could be got out and saddled the fugitives were out of sight and pursuit was vain. Just as before they went across country to the rock cut and then swam up, holding by the linking seaweed.

===Chinese===
那位倒霉的"动物抢牌"玩家，一抬眼瞥见五匹骏马扬长而去——鞍辔俱全，在他眼里却不见骑手——顿时从牌局里惊跳起来。等旁的海马再牵出来、备好鞍，逃亡者早已无影无踪，追赶也是徒劳。同上回一样，他们越野穿行，到凿岩坡道跟前，攥着相连的海草一路游了上去。

===Original===
Because it was Tuesday and nearly two o’clock, the Professor of Conchology was making ready to receive pupils, which he did in an arbor of coral of various shades of pink, surrounded by specimen shells of all the simpler species. He was alone in the garden, and as they neared him, the Princess, the three children and Ulfin touched the necessary buttons and became once more visible and tangible.

===Chinese===
因为这天是星期二，快到两点钟了，贝类学教授正准备接见学童——他授课的地方是一座浓淡各色粉红的珊瑚凉亭，四下摆着各种较简单门类的标本贝壳。园子里只有他一个人。等他们走近他，公主、三个孩子和乌尔芬按了该按的纽扣，重新变回看得见、摸得着的样子。

===Original===
“Ha,” said the Professor, but without surprise. “Magic. A very neat trick, my dears, and excellently done.”

===Chinese===
"哈。"教授说，却毫无惊讶之意。"魔法。好一手干净利落的戏法，亲爱的孩子们，干得漂亮极了。"

===Original===
“You need not remove your jacket,” he added to Ulfin, who was pulling off his pearly coat. “The mental exercises in which we propose to engage do not require gymnasium costume.”

===Chinese===
"你不必脱外衣。"他又对正往下扒那件珠光甲衣的乌尔芬说，"我们接下来要做的是头脑操练，用不着体操的装束。"

===Original===
But Ulfin went on taking off his coat, and when it was off he handed it to the Princess, who at once felt in its inner pocket, pulled out a little golden case and held it toward the Professor. It has been well said that no charm on earth﻿—I mean underwater﻿—is strong enough to make one forget one’s antidote. The moment the Professor’s eye fell on the little golden case, he held out his hand for it, and the Princess gave it to him. He opened it, and without hesitation as without haste, swallowed the charm.

===Chinese===
可乌尔芬只管脱他的甲衣，脱下来便递给公主；公主立刻伸手往内袋里一探，掏出一只小小的金匣，向教授递去。有句话说得好：世上——我是说水底下——还没有哪种魔符，效力强到能叫人忘掉自己的解药。教授的目光一落在那只小金匣上，手便伸了过去，公主把它交给了他。他打开匣子，不迟疑也不慌忙，把护符一口吞了下去。

===Original===
Next moment the Princess was clasped in his arms, and the moment after that, still clasped there, was beginning a hurried explanation; but he stopped her.

===Chinese===
转眼间，公主已被他紧紧搂进怀里；再一转眼，她还搂在怀里，便急急忙忙要开口解释；他却止住了她。

===Original===
“I know, my child, I know,” he said. “You have brought me the charm which gives back to me my memory and makes a King of Merland out of a Professor of Conchology. But why, oh why, did you not bring me my coat﻿—my pearly coat?” said the King, “it was in the case with the others.”

===Chinese===
"我知道，我的孩子，我知道。"他说，"你给我带来了护符，它把记忆还给了我，把一个贝类学教授重新变成了人鱼国的国王。可是为什么，哦，为什么，你不把我的甲衣也带来——我那件珠光甲衣？"国王说，"它本来同其余的那些一起，就在柜子里。"

===Original===
No one had thought of it, and everyone felt and looked exceedingly silly, and no one spoke till Ulfin said, holding out the coat which the Princess had given back to him﻿—

===Chinese===
谁都没有想到这一层，人人都觉得自己傻透了，脸上也一副傻相，谁也不开口，直到乌尔芬伸出手，捧着公主方才还给他的那件甲衣，说道——

===Original===
“You will have this coat, Majesty. I have no right to the magic garments of your country.”

===Chinese===
"陛下就请收下这件甲衣。贵国的魔法衣裳，我无权占用。"

===Original===
“But,” said Francis, “you need the coat more than anybody. The King shall have mine﻿—I shan’t want it if you’ll let me go and ask for an interview with the King of the Under Folk.”

===Chinese===
"可是，"弗朗西斯说，"您比谁都需要甲衣。国王就拿我这件吧——只要你们肯让我去求见深渊族国王，我就用不着它了。"

===Original===
“No, have mine,” said Mavis﻿—and “have mine,” said Bernard, and the Princess said, “Of course my Father will have mine.” So they all protested at once. But the King raised his hand, and there was silence, and they saw that he no longer looked only a noble and learned gentleman, but that he looked every inch a King.

===Chinese===
"不，用我的。"梅维斯说。"用我的。"伯纳德说。公主则说："我父王当然用我的。"一时间大家争让起来。可国王一举手，四下便肃静了。他们这才看出，他如今看上去已不只是一位高贵博学的绅士，而是浑身上下、十足十的一位国王。

===Original===
“Silence,” he said, “if anyone speaks with the King and Queen of this land it is fitting that it should be I. See, we will go out by the back door, so as to avoid the other pupils who will soon be arriving in their thousands, for my Conchology Course is very popular. And as we go, tell me who is this man of the Under Folk who seems to be one of you”﻿—(“I am the Princess’ servant,” Ulfin put in)﻿—“and why you desire to speak with the King of this land.”

===Chinese===
"肃静。"他说，"若要同这片国土的国王和王后讲话，相宜的人也理当是我。这样吧，我们从后门出去，免得撞上别的学童——他们转眼就要成千上万地涌来，因为我的贝类学课程大得人心。一路走，一路告诉我：这个深渊族人是谁，瞧着倒像你们一伙的"——（"我是公主的仆人。"乌尔芬插口道）——"还有，你们为什么想同这个国家的国王说话。"

===Original===
So they made great haste to go out by the back way so as not to meet the Conchology students, and cautiously crept up to their horses﻿—and, of course, the biggest and best horse was given to the King to ride. But when he saw how awkwardly their false tails adapted themselves to the saddle he said, “My daughter, you can remove these fetters.”

===Chinese===
于是他们急急从后路出去，免得撞见贝类学的学生，又蹑手蹑脚、悄悄摸到他们的海马跟前——自然，最大最好的那匹让给了国王骑。可他一见那些假尾巴搭上马鞍时那副别扭相，便道："我的女儿，你能解掉这些镣铐。"

===Original===
“How?” said she. “My shell knife won’t cut them.”

===Chinese===
"怎么解？"她说，"我的贝壳刀割不断。"

===Original===
“Bite through the strings of them with your little sharp teeth,” said the King, “nothing but Princess teeth is sharp enough to cut through them. No, my son﻿—it is not degrading. A true Princess cannot be degraded by anything that is for the good of her subjects and her friends.”

===Chinese===
"用你那口尖尖的小牙，把系绳一根根咬断。"国王说，"唯有公主的牙齿才够尖利，啃得断它们。不，我的孩子——这并不辱没人。一位真正的公主，凡是为了臣民和朋友的益处去做的事，没有一件能辱没了她。"

===Original===
So the Mer Princess willingly bit through the strings of the false tails﻿—and everybody put on his or her proper tail again, with great comfort and enjoyment﻿—and they all swam toward the town.

===Chinese===
于是人鱼公主心甘情愿地咬断了假尾巴的系绳——人人重新装上自己原来那条像模像样的尾巴，说不出的舒坦快活——大家一起朝城里游去。

===Original===
And as they went they heard a great noise of shouting, and saw parties of Under Folk flying as if in fear.

===Chinese===
走着走着，他们听见一片震天的呐喊，还看见一伙伙深渊族的人仓皇奔逃，仿佛大难临头。

===Original===
“I must make haste,” said the King, “and see to it that our Peace Conference be not too late”﻿—so they hurried on.

===Chinese===
"我得抓紧了，"国王说，"务必别让我们的和平会议开得太迟。"于是他们加紧赶路。

===Original===
And the noise grew louder and louder, and the crowds of flying Under Folk thicker and fleeter, and by and by Ulfin made them stand back under the arch of the Astrologers’ Tower to see what it was from which they fled. And there, along the streets of the great city of the Under Folk, came the flash of swords and the swirl of banners and the army of the Mer Folk came along between the great buildings of their foes, and on their helmets was the light of victory, and at their head, proud and splendid, rode the Princess Maia and﻿—Reuben.

===Chinese===
呐喊声越来越响，奔逃的深渊族人群越来越稠、脚步越来越急。后来乌尔芬让他们退避到占星家塔楼的拱门底下，好看看他们逃的到底是什么。只见在那座深渊族大城的街道上，剑光闪闪，旌旗翻卷，人鱼族的大军正行进在敌人高楼巨厦之间，他们的头盔上闪着胜利的光；队伍最前头，威风凛凛、神采焕然地骑马走着的，是迈亚公主，还有——鲁本。

===Original===
“Oh﻿—Reuben, Reuben! We’re saved,” called Mavis, and would have darted out, but Francis put his hand over her mouth.

===Chinese===
"哦——鲁本，鲁本！我们得救了。"梅维斯喊着就要冲出去，弗朗西斯一把捂住了她的嘴。

===Original===
“Stop!” he said, “don’t you remember we promised not to escape without the Queen’s permission? Quick, quick to the Palace, to make peace before our armies can attack it.”

===Chinese===
"站住！"他说，"你们不记得吗，咱们答应过，不奉女王恩准决不逃走。快，快去王宫，赶在咱们的军队攻打它之前，把和平谈成。"

===Original===
“You speak well,” said the Mer King. And Ulfin said, “This is no time for ceremony. Quick, quick, I will take you in by the tradesmen’s entrance.” And, turning their backs on that splendid and victorious procession, they marched to the back entrance of the royal Palace.

===Chinese===
"你说得好。"人鱼国王说。乌尔芬说："这时候讲不得虚礼了。快，快，我带你们从下人进出的边门进宫。"于是他们背对那支凯旋辉煌的队伍，向王宫的后门走去。

## XII The End / 十二、结局

===Original===
The Queen of the Under Folk sat with her husband on their second-best throne, which was much more comfortable than their State one, though not so handsome. Their sad faces were lighted up with pleasure as they watched the gambols of their new pet, Fido, a dear little earth-child, who was playing with a ball of soft pink seaweed, patting it, and tossing it and running after it as prettily as any kitten.

===Chinese===
深渊族女王同丈夫坐在他们那第二好的宝座上——这座宝座比大典用的那一座舒服得多，只是不及它华贵。夫妻俩满脸愁容，此刻却因看着新爱宠菲多的嬉戏而亮起了光彩。菲多是个可爱的小陆地孩子，正玩着一只粉色软海草扎的球，拍一拍，抛一抛，又追着跑，那份娇俏劲儿，赛过随便哪只小猫崽。

===Original===
“Dear little Fido,” said the Queen, “come here then,” and Fido, who had once been Cathay, came willingly to lean against the Queen’s knee and be stroked and petted.

===Chinese===
“小菲多，乖，”女王说，“到这儿来。”菲多——她从前叫凯西——乖乖地走过去，偎在女王膝头，由她抚摩宠爱。

===Original===
“I have curious dreams sometimes,” said the Queen to the King, “dreams so vivid that they are more like memories.”

===Chinese===
“我有时会做些怪梦，”女王对国王说，“那些梦真切得很，倒不像梦，更像是从前的记忆。”

===Original===
“Has it ever occurred to you,” said the King, “that we have no memories of our childhood, of our youth﻿—?”

===Chinese===
“你可曾想过，”国王说，“我们对自己的童年、自己的少年，竟没有半点记忆——？”

===Original===
“I believe,” said the Queen slowly, “that we have tasted in our time of the oblivion-cup. There is no one like us in this land. If we were born here, why can we not remember our parents who must have been like us? And dearest﻿—the dream that comes to me most often is that we once had a child and lost it﻿—and that it was a child like us﻿—”

===Chinese===
“我相信，”女王缓缓地说，“我们当年喝过遗忘之杯。这个国度里，再没有一个人长得像我们。倘若我们生在此地，为什么想不起我们的父母？他们必定长得像我们才是。还有，最亲爱的——我最常做的那个梦，是我们从前有过一个孩子，后来丢了——而且那孩子，长得像我们——”

===Original===
“Fido,” said the King in a low voice, “is like us.” And he, too, stroked the head of Cathay, who had forgotten everything except that she was Fido and bore the Queen’s name on her collar. “But if you remember that we had a child it cannot be true﻿—if we drank of the oblivion-cup, that is, because, of course, that would make us forget everything.”

===Chinese===
“菲多，”国王低声说，“长得就像我们。”他也伸手抚了抚凯西的头；她什么都忘光了，只记得自己叫菲多，项圈上刻着女王给她起的名字。“不过，你若还记得我们有过孩子，那就不会是真的——我是说，倘若我们喝过遗忘杯的话；因为那东西理所当然，会叫人把一切都忘掉。”

===Original===
“It could not make a mother forget her child,” said the Queen, and with the word caught up Fido-which-was-Cathay and kissed her.

===Chinese===
“它再厉害，也断断不能叫母亲忘掉自己的孩子。”女王话音才落，就一把抱起如今唤作菲多的凯西，亲了她一口。

===Original===
“Nice Queen,” purred Cathay-which-was-Fido, “I do love you.”

===Chinese===
“好女王，”如今唤作菲多的凯西像小猫似的呜噜呜噜撒着娇，“我真爱你。”

===Original===
“I am sure we had a child once,” said the Queen, hugging her, “and that we have been made to forget.”

===Chinese===
“我敢肯定，我们从前有过一个孩子，”女王搂着她说，“只是被人弄得忘了。”

===Original===
Even as she spoke the hangings of cloth of gold, pieced together from the spoil of lost galleons, rustled at the touch of someone outside. The Queen dried her eyes, which needed it, and said, “Come in.”

===Chinese===
她话音未落，那幅用失事大帆船的劫获品拼缀成的金缕帷幔，便叫外头什么人一碰，窸窸窣窣响了起来。女王擦了擦眼睛——也实在需要擦一擦——说了声：“进来。”

===Original===
The arras was lifted and a tall figure entered.

===Chinese===
帷幔掀起，一个高高的身影走了进来。

===Original===
“Bless my soul,” said the King of the Under Folk, “it’s the Professor of Conchology.”

===Chinese===
“天哪，”深渊族国王说，“这不是贝类学教授嘛。”

===Original===
“No,” said the figure, advancing, “it is the King of the Mer-people. My brother King, my sister Queen, I greet you.”

===Chinese===
“不是，”那人影一面走上前来一面说，“我是人鱼族的国王。王兄，王嫂，我向二位请安。”

===Original===
“This is most irregular,” said the King.

===Chinese===
“这可是大大的不合规矩。”国王说。

===Original===
“Never mind, dear,” said the Queen, “let us hear what his Majesty has to say.”

===Chinese===
“别计较，亲爱的，”女王说，“先听听陛下有什么见教。”

===Original===
“I say﻿—Let there be peace between our people,” said the Mer-King. “For countless ages these wars have been waged, for countless ages your people and mine have suffered. Even the origin of the war is lost in the mists of antiquity. Now I come to you, I, your prisoner﻿—I was given to drink of the cup of oblivion and forgot who I was and whence I came. Now a counter-charm has given me back mind and memory. I come in the name of my people. If we have wronged you, we ask your forgiveness. If you have wronged us, we freely forgive you. Say: Shall it be peace, and shall all the sons of the sea live as brothers in love and kindliness forevermore?

===Chinese===
“我此来只说一句——让我们两族之间永享太平。”人鱼国王说。“这仗打了不知多少世代，你们的百姓和我的百姓也苦了不知多少世代，连战争的起因，都早已迷失在上古的迷雾里了。如今我来到你们面前——我，你们的一名俘虏——当年有人给我喝了遗忘之杯，我便忘了自己是谁、从何而来。如今一道解咒，还给了我神智和记忆。我以我全体百姓的名义前来。倘若我们亏负了你们，我们请求宽恕；倘若你们亏负了我们，我们毫无芥蒂地宽恕你们。请回答：要不要太平？大海的全体子孙，愿不愿从此相亲相爱、和和睦睦，永生永世做兄弟？”

===Original===
“Really,” said the King of the Under Folk, “I think it is not at all a bad idea﻿—but in confidence, and between Monarchs, I may tell you, sir, that I suspect my mind is not what it was. You, sir, seem to possess a truly royal grasp of your subject. My mind is so imperfect that I dare not consult it. But my heart﻿—”

===Chinese===
“说实在的，”深渊族国王说，“我觉得这主意一点也不坏——不过私下里说，国王对国王，我告诉你，先生，我疑心我这副头脑已经不比从前了。您呢，先生，对自己的事倒真有十足王家的把握。我这副头脑太不济，我都不敢去请教它了。倒是我的心——”

===Original===
“Your heart says Yes,” said the Queen. “So does mine. But our troops are besieging your city,” she said, “they will say that in asking for peace you were paying the tribute of the vanquished.”

===Chinese===
“你的心说的是‘愿意’，”女王说，“我的心也是。只是我们的军队还在围攻你的城，”她说，“人家会说，你今日求和，是战败者低头纳贡。”

===Original===
“My people will not think this of me,” said the King of Merland, “nor would your people think it of you. Let us join hands in peace and the love of royal brethren.”

===Chinese===
“我的百姓绝不会这样想我，”人鱼国国王说，“你们的百姓也绝不会这样想你们。让我们携手言和，以王室手足之爱相亲相敬。”

===Original===
“What a dreadful noise they are making outside,” said the King, and indeed the noise of shouting and singing was now to be heard on every side of the Palace.

===Chinese===
“外头吵得多吓人。”国王说。的确，这会儿王宫四面八方都响着呼喊和歌唱的声音。

===Original===
“If there was a balcony now where we could show ourselves,” suggested the King of Merland.

===Chinese===
“这会儿要是有座阳台，能让咱们露一露面就好了。”人鱼国国王提议道。

===Original===
“The very thing,” said the Queen, catching up her pet Fido-which-was-Cathay in her arms and leading the way to the great curtained arch at the end of the hall. She drew back the swinging, sweeping hangings of woven seaweed and stepped forth on the balcony﻿—the two Kings close behind her. But she stopped short and staggered back a little, so that her husband had to put an arm about her to support her, when her first glance showed her that the people who were shouting outside the Palace were not, as she had supposed, Under Folk in some unexpected though welcome transport of loyal enthusiasm, but ranks on ranks of the enemy, the hated Mer Folk, all splendid and menacing in the pomp and circumstance of glorious war.

===Chinese===
“正该如此。”女王说着，一把抱起爱宠菲多——昔日的凯西——领头朝大厅尽头那座挂着帷幔的大拱门走去。她掀开那幅飘飘垂垂的织海草帷幔，跨上阳台——两位国王紧随其后。可她猛地站住，脚下微微一个趔趄，丈夫只得伸臂扶住她。原来头一眼望去，她就看清了：在宫外呼喊的，并不是她料想中的深渊族百姓——出于一场意外却也受欢迎的忠义狂热——而是一列又一列的敌人，可恨的人鱼族，个个鲜亮堂皇、威风凛凛，全副披挂着光荣战争的盛大仪仗。

===Original===
“It is the enemy!” gasped the Queen.

===Chinese===
“是敌人！”女王倒抽一口气。

===Original===
“It is my people,” said the Mer King. “It is a beautiful thing in you, dear Queen, that you agreed to peace, without terms, while you thought you were victorious, and not because the legions of the Mer Folk were thundering at your gates. May I speak for us?”

===Chinese===
“是我的百姓，”人鱼国王说，“亲爱的女王，您答应议和、不附任何条件，是在您自以为稳操胜券的时候，而不是在人鱼族的军团正轰您城门的时候——这是您身上顶美的品格。请允许我代表我们双方说几句话，可以吗？”

===Original===
They signed assent. And the Mer King stepped forward full into view of the crowd in the street below.

===Chinese===
他们示意应允。人鱼国王便跨步上前，稳稳站到楼下街市人群一眼望得见的地方。

===Original===
“My people,” he said in a voice loud, yet soft, and very, very beautiful. And at the words the Mer Folk below looked up and recognized their long-lost King, and a shout went up that you could have heard a mile away.

===Chinese===
“我的百姓，”他说，嗓音洪亮，却又柔和，而且非常、非常动听。一听这话，楼下的人鱼族纷纷仰起头来，认出了他们失散已久的国王，顿时爆出一片欢呼，一英里外都听得见。

===Original===
The King raised his hand for silence.

===Chinese===
国王抬手示意肃静。

===Original===
“My people,” he said, “brave men of Merland﻿—let there be peace, now and forever, between us and our brave foes. The King and Queen of this land agreed to make unconditional peace while they believed themselves to be victorious. If victory has for today been with us, let us at least be the equals of our foes in generosity as in valor.”

===Chinese===
“我的百姓，”他说，“人鱼国的勇士们——让我们同勇敢的对手之间，从今往后，永享太平。这片国土的国王和王后，是在自以为得胜的时候，答应无条件议和的。倘若今天的胜利在我们这边，那么至少，让我们在宽宏上也不输给对手，一如我们在骁勇上从不输给他们。”

===Original===
Another shout rang out. And the King of the Under Folk stepped forward.

===Chinese===
又一阵欢呼响了起来。深渊族国王跨前一步。

===Original===
“My people,” he said, and the Under Folk came quickly forward toward him at the sound of his voice. “There shall be peace. Let these who were your foes this morning be your guests tonight and your friends and brothers forevermore. If we have wronged them, we beg them to forgive us: if they have wronged us, we beg them to allow us to forgive them.” (“Is that right?” he asked the Mer King in a hasty whisper, who whispered back, “Admirable!”) “Now,” he went on, “cheer, Mer Folk and Under Folk, for the splendid compact of Peace.”

===Chinese===
“我的百姓，”他说。深渊族人一听见他的声音，便快步朝他拥来。“太平必成。今晨还是你们敌人的，今晚便是你们的客人，从此永远做你们的朋友和兄弟。倘若我们亏负了他们，我们求他们宽恕；倘若他们亏负了我们，我们求他们允许我们宽恕他们。”（“这么说对吗？”他急急地悄声问人鱼国王；人鱼国王悄声回他：“妙极了。”）“现在，”他接着说，“欢呼吧，人鱼族和深渊族，为这光彩的和平之约欢呼！”

===Original===
And they cheered.

===Chinese===
于是他们欢呼了。

===Original===
“Pardon, your Majesty”﻿—it was Ulfin who spoke﻿—“it was the stranger Francis who first conceived the Peace Idea.”

===Chinese===
“启禀陛下”——说话的是乌尔芬——“头一个想出和平这个主意的，是那位外乡人弗朗西斯。”

===Original===
“True,” said the Mer King, “where is Francis?”

===Chinese===
“不错，”人鱼国王说，“弗朗西斯人在哪儿？”

===Original===
But Francis was not to be found; it was only his name which was presented to the people from the balcony. He himself kept his pearly coat on and kept the invisibility button well pressed down, till the crowd had dispersed to ring all the diving bells with which the towers of the city were so handsomely fitted up, to hang the city with a thousand seaweed flags, and to illuminate its every window and door and pinnacle and buttress with more and more phosphorescent fish. In the Palace was a banquet for the Kings and the Queen and the Princesses, and the three children, and Cathay-who-was-Fido. Also Reuben was called from the command of his Sea Urchins to be a guest at the royal table. Princess Freia asked that an invitation might be sent to Ulfin﻿—but when the King’s Private Secretary, a very intelligent cuttlefish, had got the invitation ready, handsomely written in his own ink, it was discovered that no Ulfin was to be found to receive it.

===Chinese===
可是弗朗西斯哪儿也找不着；从阳台上向百姓们呈献的，只是他的名字。他自己呢，珠光甲衣一直穿在身上，隐身纽扣一直按得牢牢的，直到人群散去——去敲响全城塔楼上装配得漂漂亮亮的那些潜水钟，去给全城挂上千百面海草旗，去用越来越多的磷光鱼，把每一扇门窗、每一座尖顶、每一道扶壁都照亮。王宫里设下了盛宴，宴请两位国王、王后和公主们、三个孩子，还有如今唤作菲多的凯西。鲁本也从海胆们的统领任上被召来，成了御桌上的宾客。弗蕾亚公主请求给乌尔芬也送去一份请帖——可是等国王的私人秘书，一条非常精明的乌贼，用自己的墨汁把请帖写得漂漂亮亮，却发现哪儿也找不到乌尔芬来领帖了。

===Original===
It was a glorious banquet. The only blot on its rapturous splendor was the fact that Cathay still remained Fido, the Queen’s pet﻿—and her eyes were still those cold, unremembering eyes which her brothers and sister could not bear to meet. Reuben sat at the right hand of the Queen, and from the moment he took his place there he seemed to think of no one else. He talked with her, sensibly and modestly, and Francis remarked that during his stay in Merland Reuben had learned to talk as you do, and not in the language of gypsy circus-people. The Commander-in-Chief of the Forces of the Under Folk sat at the left hand of his King. The King of the Mer Folk sat between his happy daughters, and the children sat together between the Chief Astrologer and the Curator of the Museum of Foreign Curiosities, who was more pleased to see them again than he had ever expected to be, and much more friendly than they had ever hoped to find him. Everyone was extremely happy, even Fido-which-was-Cathay, who sat on the Queen’s lap and was fed with delicacies from the Queen’s own plate.

===Chinese===
那是一场盛大辉煌的宴会。唯一败兴的，是凯西仍旧是女王的爱宠菲多——她那双眼睛，依旧是那种冷冷的、不认人的眼睛，她的哥哥姐姐怎么也不忍心去对视。鲁本坐在女王右首；从落座那一刻起，他仿佛心里就再没有第二个人了。他同女王交谈，谈得有见地而又谦逊；弗朗西斯评论说，鲁本在人鱼国住的这一阵，学会了像你们这样说话，不再操那口吉普赛马戏班的腔调了。深渊族全军总司令坐在他的国王左首。人鱼族国王坐在他那两个喜气洋洋的女儿中间。孩子们则一块儿坐在御前大星官和外国奇珍博物馆馆长之间——那位馆长重见他们时那份欢喜，连他自己都始料未及；那份亲热劲儿，也远超出孩子们敢于指望的。人人都快活极了，连如今唤作菲多的凯西也不例外：她坐在女王膝上，专享女王自己碟中的珍馐美味。

===Original===
It was at about the middle of the feast, just after everybody had drunk the health of the two Commanders-in-Chief, amid tempestuous applause, that a serving-fish whispered behind his fin to the Under Folk Queen:

===Chinese===
宴席约莫进行到一半，众人刚向两位总司令祝过健康、掌声雷动之后不久，一条上菜鱼便用鱼鳍掩着嘴，向深渊族女王低声禀报：

===Original===
“Certainly,” she said, “show him in.”

===Chinese===
“当然可以，”她说，“带他进来。”

===Original===
And the person who was shown in was Ulfin, and he carried on his arm a pearly coat and a scaly tail. He sank on one knee and held them up to the Mer King, with only one doubtful deprecating glance at the Curator of the Museum of Foreign Curiosities.

===Chinese===
被带进来的正是乌尔芬。他臂弯里搭着一件珠光甲衣和一条鳞光尾巴，单膝跪下，把它们高高捧向人鱼国王，只朝外国奇珍博物馆馆长望了一眼，那一眼里半是忐忑、半是告罪。

===Original===
The King took them, and feeling in the pocket of the coat drew out three golden cases.

===Chinese===
国王接过，往珠光甲衣口袋里一探，摸出三只金匣子。

===Original===
“It is the royal prerogative to have three,” he said smilingly to the Queen, “in case of accidents. May I ask your Majesty’s permission to administer one of them to your Majesty’s little pet. I am sure you are longing to restore her to her brothers and her sister.”

===Chinese===
“身上带三只，是王家的特权，”他笑吟吟地对女王说，“以防万一。可否求陛下恩准，让我把其中一只给陛下的小爱宠服下？我知道，您一定巴不得把她还给她的哥哥和姐姐呢。”

===Original===
The Queen could not but agree﻿—though her heart was sore at losing the little Fido-Kathleen, of whom she had grown so fond. But she was hoping that Reuben would consent to let her adopt him, and be more to her than many Fidos. She administered the charm herself, and the moment Cathay had swallowed it the royal arms were loosened, and the Queen expected her pet to fly to her brothers and sister. But to Cathay it was as though only an instant had passed since she came into that hall, a prisoner. So that when suddenly she saw her brothers and sister honored guests at what was unmistakably a very grand and happy festival, and found herself in the place of honor on the very lap of the Queen, she only snuggled closer to that royal lady and called out very loud and clear, “Hullo, Mavis! Here’s a jolly transformation scene. That was a magic drink she gave us and it’s made everybody jolly and friends﻿—I am glad. You dear Queen,” she added, “it is nice of you to nurse me.”

===Chinese===
女王不能不容应——尽管一想到要失去小菲多——小凯思琳——她就心头发酸，她早已疼极了这孩子。好在她还盼着鲁本肯让她收养，那样他对她的情分，赛过十个菲多。她亲手喂下了那道魔咒；凯西刚一咽下去，王家的臂膀便松开了。女王满心等着她的爱宠扑向哥哥姐姐。可对凯西来说，从她被当作俘虏带进这座大厅，到此刻，仿佛只过了一眨眼的工夫。所以，当她猛然看见哥哥姐姐成了这场明明白白又盛大又欢喜的宴席上的贵客，又发觉自己正坐在荣耀的位子上——女王的膝头——她只是往那位王家贵妇怀里偎得更紧，又清又亮地大声喊道：“喂，梅维斯！这场大变戏法可真带劲。她方才给我们喝的是一种魔水，把大家都变得高高兴兴、和和好好——我真高兴。亲爱的女王，”她添了一句，“你这样疼我、照看我，真好。”

===Original===
So everybody was pleased: only Princess Freia looked sad and puzzled and her eyes followed Ulfin as he bowed and made to retire from the royal presence. He had almost reached the door when she spoke quickly in the royal ear that was next to her.

===Chinese===
这一来人人欢喜：只有弗蕾亚公主又难过又困惑，一双眼睛只顾追着乌尔芬——他正躬着身，要从御前告退。他差不多已经走到门口，公主急忙凑到挨着她身边的那只王家耳朵边，飞快地说了一句。

===Original===
“Oh, Father,” she said, “don’t let him go like that. He ought to be at the banquet. We couldn’t have done anything without him.”

===Chinese===
“哦，父王，”她说，“别让他就这么走了。他理当赴宴。没有他，我们什么都办不成。”

===Original===
“True,” said the King, “but I thought he had been invited, and refused.”

===Chinese===
“这话不假，”国王说，“可我以为早就给他送了请帖，是他自己推辞了。”

===Original===
“Refused?” said the Princess, “oh, call him back!”

===Chinese===
“推辞？”公主说，“哦，快把他叫回来！”

===Original===
“I’ll run if I may,” said Mavis, slipping out of her place and running down the great hall.

===Chinese===
“要是我可以去，我这就跑一趟。”梅维斯说着，从座位上溜下来，沿着大厅一路跑了下去。

===Original===
“If you’ll sit a little nearer to me, Father,” said Maia obligingly, “the young man can sit between you and my sister.”

===Chinese===
“父王要是肯往我这边坐近一点儿，”迈亚殷勤地说，“这位年轻人就可以坐在您和我姐姐中间了。”

===Original===
So that is where Ulfin found himself, and that was where he had never dared to hope to be.

===Chinese===
乌尔芬就这样落座在那个位子上；而那个位子，是他从来不敢指望的。

===Original===
The banquet was a strange as well as a magnificent scene﻿—because, of course, the Mer-people were beautiful as the day, the five children were quite as pretty as any five children have any need to be, and the King and Queen of the Under Folk were as handsome as handsome. So that all this handsomeness was a very curious contrast to the strange heavy features of the Under Folk who now sat at table, so pleasant and friendly, toasting their late enemies.

===Chinese===
这场宴席，既盛大，又奇特——因为，不用说，人鱼族个个美得如白昼一般；五个孩子呢，也出落得要多俊有多俊——做孩子的，俊到这个份上尽够了；深渊族的国王和王后，更是俊上添俊。于是，这一片俊美，同满座深渊族人那粗重奇特的眉目，恰成一种顶新奇的对照——他们此刻坐在席间，和和气气、客客气气，正向不久前的敌人举杯。

===Original===
The contrast between the Princess Freia and Ulfin was particularly marked, for their heads bent near together as they talked.

===Chinese===
弗蕾亚公主和乌尔芬之间的对照尤其醒目，因为两人说话的时候，头几乎凑到了一处。

===Original===
“Princess,” he was saying, “tomorrow you will go back to your kingdom, and I shall never see you again.”

===Chinese===
“公主，”他正说着，“明天您就要回您的王国去了，我从此再也见不到您了。”

===Original===
The Princess could not think of anything to say, because it seemed to her that what he said was true.

===Chinese===
公主想不出一句话来回答，因为在她听来，他说的分明是真话。

===Original===
“But,” he went on, “I shall be glad all my life to have known and loved so dear and beautiful a Princess.”

===Chinese===
“不过，”他接着说，“我此生认识过、爱慕过这样一位又亲又美的公主，我一辈子都要为此高兴。”

===Original===
And again the Princess could think of nothing to say.

===Chinese===
公主又一次想不出话来说了。

===Original===
“Princess,” he said, “tell me one thing. Do you know what I should say to you if I were a Prince?”

===Chinese===
“公主，”他说，“告诉我一件事。您可知道，倘若我是一位王子，我会对您说什么？”

===Original===
“Yes,” said Freia; “I know what you would say and I know what I should answer, dear Ulfin, if you were only a commoner of Merland﻿ ﻿… I mean, you know, if your face were like ours. But since you are of the Under Folk and I am a Mermaid, I can only say that I will never forget you, and that I will never marry anyone else.”

===Chinese===
“知道，”弗蕾亚说，“我知道你会说什么，也知道我会怎样回答，亲爱的乌尔芬——倘若你只是人鱼国的一介平民……我是说，你知道的，倘若你的脸长得跟我们一样。可你是深渊族的人，我是美人鱼，我只能说：我永远不会忘记你，也永远不会嫁给任何别人。”

===Original===
“Is it only my face then that prevents your marrying me?” he asked with abrupt eagerness, and she answered gently, “Of course.”

===Chinese===
“这么说，拦着你不肯嫁给我的，就只我这张脸了？”他冷不丁地急切问道。她温柔地回答：“当然是。”

===Original===
Then Ulfin sprang to his feet. “Your Majesties,” he cried, “and Lord High Astrologer, has not the moment come when, since we are at a banquet with friends, we may unmask?”

===Chinese===
乌尔芬霍地站起身来。“两位陛下，”他喊道，“还有御前大星官——如今我们是在朋友之间的宴席上，摘下面具的时候，岂不是已经到了？”

===Original===
The strangers exchanged wondering glances.

===Chinese===
几个外乡的孩子交换着惊奇的眼色。

===Original===
The Sovereigns and the Astrologers made gestures of assent﻿—then, with a rustling and a rattling, helmets were unlaced and corselets unbuckled, the Under Folk seemed to the Mer-people as though they were taking off their very skins. But really what they took off was but their thick scaly armor, and under it they were as softly and richly clad, and as personable people as the Mer Folk themselves.

===Chinese===
两位君主和星官们做了个应允的手势——于是，窸窸窣窣、哐哐啷啷一阵响，头盔解了系带，胸甲松了搭扣。在人鱼族看来，深渊族仿佛正在剥自己的皮。其实他们脱下的，不过是厚厚的鳞甲；甲胄底下，他们穿得一样柔软华丽，人一样仪表堂堂，同人鱼族自己毫无二致。

===Original===
“But,” said Maia, “how splendid! We thought you were always in armor﻿—that﻿—that it grew on you, you know.”

===Chinese===
“哎呀，”迈亚说，“多神气呀！我们还以为你们成天穿着盔甲——以为——以为那是长在身上的呢，你知道。”

===Original===
The Under Folk laughed jollily. “Of course it was always on us﻿—since﻿—when you saw us, we were always at war.”

===Chinese===
深渊族开怀大笑。“它当然一直穿在身上——因为——你们见到我们的时候，我们一直在打仗呀。”

===Original===
“And you’re just like us!” said Freia to Ulfin.

===Chinese===
“你跟我们长得一模一样！”弗蕾亚对乌尔芬说。

===Original===
“There is no one like you,” he whispered back. Ulfin was now a handsome dark-haired young man, and looked much more like a Prince than a great many real Princes do.

===Chinese===
“举世再没有第二个你。”他低声答道。乌尔芬这时已是一个黑发的俊美青年，看上去比许许多多货真价实的王子还更像王子。

===Original===
“Did you mean what you said just now?” the Princess whispered. And for answer Ulfin dared to touch her hand with soft firm fingers.

===Chinese===
“你方才的话，是当真的吗？”公主悄声问。作为回答，乌尔芬鼓起勇气，伸出手指，轻轻而坚定地碰了碰她的手。

===Original===
“Papa,” said Freia, “please may I marry Ulfin?”

===Chinese===
“爸爸，”弗蕾亚说，“请答应我嫁给乌尔芬，好不好？”

===Original===
“By all means,” said the King, and immediately announced the engagement, joining their hands and giving them his blessing in the most businesslike way.

===Chinese===
“完全应当。”国王说，随即宣布了婚约，把两人的手拉到一处，用顶干脆利落、公事公办的派头给了他们祝福。

===Original===
Then said the Queen of the Under Folk:

===Chinese===
这时深渊族女王说道：

===Original===
“Why should not these two reign over the Under Folk and let us two be allowed to remember the things we have forgotten and go back to that other life which I know we had somewhere﻿—where we had a child.”

===Chinese===
“何不就让这两个孩子统治深渊族，而让我们两个得准想起忘掉的事，回到我们另一种生活里去——我知道我们在什么地方有过那种生活——在那儿，我们有个孩子。”

===Original===
“I think,” said Mavis, “that now everything’s settled so comfortably we ought perhaps all of us to be thinking about getting home.”

===Chinese===
“我看，”梅维斯说，“如今一切都安排得这么妥帖，咱们几个也许该想想回家了。”

===Original===
“I have only one charm left, unfortunately,” said the Mer King, “but if your people will agree to your abdicating, I will divide it between you with pleasure, dear King and Queen of the Under Folk; and I have reason to believe that the half which you will each of you have, will be just enough to counteract your memories of this place, and restore to you all the memories of your other life.”

===Chinese===
“可惜我只剩一道魔咒了，”人鱼国王说，“不过，亲爱的深渊族国王和王后，倘若你们的百姓肯答应你们退位，我乐意把它给你们两个平分。我有理由相信，你们一人分到的那一半，分量恰好，既抵消你们对此地的记忆，又把你们另一种生活的记忆，尽数还给你们。”

===Original===
“Could not Reuben go with us?” the Queen asked.

===Chinese===
“鲁本不能同我们一起走吗？”女王问。

===Original===
“No,” said the Mer King, “but he shall follow you to earth, and that speedily.”

===Chinese===
“不能，”人鱼国王说，“但我会让他随你们到陆地上去——快得很。”

===Original===
The Astrologer Royal, who had been whispering to Reuben, here interposed.

===Chinese===
一直同鲁本窃窃私语的御前大星官，这时插话了。

===Original===
“It would be well, your Majesties,” he said, “if a small allowance of the cup of oblivion were served out to these land children, so that they may not remember their adventures here. It is not well for the Earth People to know too much of the dwellers in the sea. There is a sacred vessel which has long been preserved among the civic plate. I propose that this vessel should be presented to our guests as a mark of our esteem; that they shall bear it with them, and drink the contents as soon as they set foot on their own shores.”

===Chinese===
“两位陛下，”他说，“最好给这几位陆地孩子发放一小份遗忘之杯，好叫他们记不得在这里的种种奇遇。陆上的人把海中居民的事知道得太多，并不是好事。有一件神圣的器皿，在市藏的金银器皿中珍藏已久。我提议把这件器皿赠与我们的贵客，以表敬意；请他们随身携带，一踏上自己的国土，便饮尽其中所盛之物。”

===Original===
He was at once sent to fetch the sacred vessel. It was a stone ginger beer bottle.

===Chinese===
大家立刻派他去取那件神圣的器皿。那是一只石头的姜汁啤酒瓶。

===Original===
“I do really think we ought to go,” said Mavis again.

===Chinese===
“我真的觉得，我们该走了。”梅维斯又说了一遍。

===Original===
There were farewells to be said﻿—a very loving farewell to the Princesses, a very friendly one to the fortunate Ulfin, and then a little party left the Palace quietly and for the last time made the journey to the quiet Iswater where the King of Merland had so long professed Conchology.

===Chinese===
该道的别都道了——同两位公主依依不舍地道了别，同好运的乌尔芬热热乎乎地道了别。随后一小队人悄然离开王宫，最后一次前往那座安静的“水岛”（Iswater）——人鱼国国王在那儿当了那么久的贝类学教授。

===Original===
Arrived at this spot the King spoke to the King and Queen of the Under Folk.

===Chinese===
到了地方，国王对深渊族国王和王后开了口。

===Original===
“Swallow this charm,” he said, “in equal shares﻿—then rise to the surface of the lake and say the charm which I perceive the Earth children have taught you as we came along. The rest will be easy and beautiful. We shall never forget you, and your hearts will remember us, though your minds must forget. Farewell.”

===Chinese===
“把这道魔咒吞下去，”他说，“一人一半——然后升上湖面，念出那句咒语——我看出来了，来的路上陆地孩子们已经把它教给你们了。余下的事，又容易又美好。我们永远不会忘记你们；你们的心也会记得我们，尽管你们的头脑必须忘掉。珍重，再会。”

===Original===
The King and Queen rose through the waters and disappeared.

===Chinese===
国王和王后穿过湖水升上去，不见了。

===Original===
Next moment a strong attraction like that which needles feel for magnets drew the children from the side of the Mer King. They shut their eyes, and when they opened them they were on dry land in a wood by a lake﻿—and Francis had a ginger beer bottle in his hand. The King and Queen of the Under Folk must have said at once the charm to recall the children to earth.

===Chinese===
紧接着，一股强大的吸引力——就像磁石吸针那样的吸引力——把孩子们从人鱼国王身边拽走了。他们闭上眼睛；再睁开时，人已在旱地上，一座湖边的树林里——弗朗西斯手里还拿着一只姜汁啤酒瓶。深渊族国王和王后想必立刻念了那道把孩子们召回陆地的咒语。

===Original===
“It works more slowly on land, the Astrologer said,” Reuben remarked. “Before we drink and forget everything I want to tell you that I think you’ve all been real bricks to me. And if you don’t mind, I’ll take off these girls’ things.”

===Chinese===
“‘它在陆上见效得慢些’，大星官说的。”鲁本道，“趁还没喝、还没把一切忘掉，我要告诉你们：你们对我个个都够朋友，真是好样的。你们不介意的话，我要把这些女孩儿家的行头脱了。”

===Original===
He did, appearing in shirt and knickerbockers.

===Chinese===
他果真脱了，现身时穿着衬衫和灯笼裤。

===Original===
“Goodbye,” he said, shaking hands with everyone.

===Chinese===
“再见，”他说着，同每个人握了手。

===Original===
“But aren’t you coming home with us?”

===Chinese===
“你不跟我们一起回家吗？”

===Original===
“No,” he said, “the Astrologer told me the first man and woman I should see on land would be my long-lost Father and Mother, and I was to go straight to them with my little shirt and my little shoe that I’ve kept all this time, the ones that were mine when I was a stolen baby, and they’d know me and I should belong to them. But I hope we’ll meet again some day. Goodbye, and thank you. It was ripping being General of the Sea Urchins.”

===Chinese===
“不，”他说，“大星官告诉我，我在陆上头一眼见到的男人和女人，就会是我失散多年的爸爸妈妈；我要带着我一直保存到今天的小衬衫和小鞋，径直到他们跟前去——那是我这被偷走的娃娃还是婴孩时穿过的东西——他们就会认出我，我也就属于他们了。不过我希望咱们后会有期。再见，谢谢你们。当海胆们的将军，痛快极了。”

===Original===
With that they drank each a draught from the ginger beer bottle, and then, making haste to act before the oblivion-cup should blot out with other things the Astrologer’s advice, Reuben went out of the wood into the sunshine and across a green turf. They saw him speak to a man and a woman in blue bathing dresses who seemed to have been swimming in the lake and now were resting on the marble steps that led down to it. He held out the little shirt and the little shoe, and they held their hands out to him. And as they turned the children saw that their faces were the faces of the King and Queen of the Under Folk, only now not sad anymore, but radiant with happiness, because they had found their son again.

===Chinese===
说罢，他们每人从姜汁啤酒瓶里喝了一大口。随后，赶在遗忘杯把大星官的嘱咐连同别的种种一并抹去之前，鲁本抢紧行动：他走出树林，走进阳光里，跨过一片青青草皮。孩子们看见他向一男一女走去——两人穿着蓝色浴衣，像是刚在湖里游过泳，正歇在通往湖边的大理石台阶上。他捧出小衬衫和小鞋，他们朝他伸出了双手。待那两人转过身来，孩子们看清了：那两张脸，正是深渊族国王和王后的脸，只是如今愁云尽扫，幸福得容光焕发——因为他们找回自己的儿子了。

===Original===
“Of course,” said Francis, “there isn’t any time in the other world. I expect they were swimming and just dived, and all that happened to them just in the minute they were underwater.”

===Chinese===
“当然喽，”弗朗西斯说，“那个世界里是没有时间的。我猜他们本来在游泳，一个猛子扎下去，他们遇上的一切，全发生在他们待在水底下的那一小会儿里。”

===Original===
“And Reuben is really their long-lost heir?”

===Chinese===
“这么说，鲁本真是他们失散多年的继承人？”

===Original===
“They seemed to think so. I expect he’s exactly like an ancestor or something, and you know how the Queen took to him from the first.”

===Chinese===
“他们看样子是这么想的。我猜呀，他准是长得同某位祖先一模一样什么的；再说你们也知道，那位女王打从一开头就多么疼他。”

===Original===
And then the oblivion-cup took effect﻿—and they forgot, and forgot forever, the wonderful world that they had known underseas, and Sabrina fair and the circus and the Mermaid whom they had rescued.

===Chinese===
然后遗忘杯的药力发作了——他们忘掉了，永远地忘掉了他们在海底见识过的那个奇妙世界，忘掉了“美丽的萨布丽娜”、马戏团，还有他们搭救的那条美人鱼。

===Original===
But Reuben, curiously enough, they did not forget: they went home to tea with a pleasant story for their father and mother of a Spangled Boy at the circus who had run away and found his father and mother.

===Chinese===
可是说来奇怪，鲁本他们却没有忘：他们回家去喝茶，给爸爸妈妈带了个好听的故事——马戏班里有个亮片男孩，逃了出来，找到了自己的爸爸妈妈。

===Original===
And two days after a motor stopped at their gate and Reuben got out.

===Chinese===
两天以后，一辆汽车在他们家院门口停了下来，鲁本从车上下来。

===Original===
“I say,” he said, “I’ve found my father and mother, and we’ve come to thank you for the plum pie and things. Did you ever get the plate and spoon out of the bush? Come and see my father and mother,” he ended proudly.

===Chinese===
“喂，”他说，“我找到我的爸爸妈妈了，我们特来谢谢你们那李子馅饼和那些吃食。那只盘子和那把勺子，你们后来从树丛里取出来了吗？来见见我的爸爸妈妈吧。”末了他骄傲地说。

===Original===
The children went, and looked once more in the faces of the King and Queen of the Under Folk, but now they did not know those faces, which seemed to them only the faces of some very nice strangers.

===Chinese===
孩子们去了，又一次望了望深渊族国王和王后的脸——可如今他们已认不出那两张脸了；在他们眼里，那不过是两张顶和善的陌生人的脸。

===Original===
“I think Reuben’s jolly lucky, don’t you?” said Mavis.

===Chinese===
“我看鲁本的运气真是好到家了，你们说呢？”梅维斯说。

===Original===
“Yes,” said Bernard.

===Chinese===
“是的。”伯纳德说。

===Original===
“So do I,” said Cathay.

===Chinese===
“我也这么想。”凯西说。

===Original===
“I wish Aunt Enid had let me bring the aquarium,” said Francis.

===Chinese===
“伊妮德姨妈要是肯让我带上水族箱就好了。”弗朗西斯说。

===Original===
“Never mind,” said Mavis, “it will be something to live for when we come back from the sea, and everything is beastly.”

===Chinese===
“别遗憾，”梅维斯说，“等我们从海边回去、样样事情都糟透了的时候，它正好是个盼头。”

===Original===
And it was.

===Chinese===
后来也果真如此。
